Tattvabodhavidhāyinī 69-133 
(Īśvarasvarūpavāda)
 
 atra īśvarakṛtajagadvādinaḥ prāhuḥ – yuktam uktam sarvajñapraṇītaṃ śāsanam, tatpraṇītatvāc ca tat pramāṇam iti | idam tv ayuktam rāgadveśādikān śatrūn jitavanta iti jināḥ | sāmānyayogina eveśvaravyatiriktā etallakṣaṇayoginaḥ na punaḥ śāsanādisarvajagatsraṣṭā īśvaraḥ | tathā ca patañjaliḥ – 
 kleśakarmavipākāśayair aparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvaraḥ [Yogad. I 24]
 iti | anena sūtreṇa rāgādilakṣanakleṣaṣatrurahitatvaṃ sahajam īśvarasya pratipāditam na punar vipakṣabhāvanādyabhyāsavyāpārāt kleśādikṣayas tasya, yena rāgādīn svavyāpāreṇa jitavanta iti vacaḥ sārthakaṃ tadviṣayatvena syāt | tathā cānyair apy uktam – 
 jñānam apratighaṃ yasya aiśvaryam ca jagatpateḥ |
 vairāgyaṃ caiva dharmaś ca saha siddhaṃ catuṣṭayam || [   ]
 ity āśaṅkyāha – bhavajiṇāṇam iti, bhavanti nārakatiryagnarāmaraparyāyatvenotpadyante prāṇino 'sminn iti bhavaḥ saṃsāraḥ; taddhetutvād rādādayo 'tra bhavaśabdenopacārād vivakṣitāḥ, taṃ jitavanta iti jināḥ | upacārśrayaṇe ca prayojanam – na hy avikalakāraṇe rāgādau adhvaste tatkāryasya saṃsārasya jayaḥ śakyo vidhātum iti pratīpādanaṃ | bhavakāraṇabhūtarāgādijaye copāyaḥ pratipāditaḥ prāk; tadupāyena ca vipakṣarāgādijayadvāreṇa tatkāryabhūtasya bhavasya jayaḥ saṃbhavati nānyatheti | na hy upāyavyatirekeṇopeyasiddhiḥ; anyathopeyasya nirhetukatvena deśakālasvabhāvapratiniyamo na syād iti sarvaprāṇinām īśvaratvam, na vā kasyacit syāt | pratipāditaś cāyam arthaḥ – 
 nityaṃ sattvam asattvaṃ vāhetor anyānapekṣaṇāt |
 apekṣāto hi bhāvānāṃ kādācitkatvasaṃbhavaḥ || XE "[PV I 35=37]"[PV I 35=37]
 ityādinā granthena dharmakīrtinā | tan na rāgādikleśavigamaḥ svabhāvata eveśvarasyeti yuktam |
 
 [prāsaṅgī paralokacarcā]
 [pūrvapakṣaḥ paraloke pratyavasthānam]
 atra bṛhaspatimatānusārinaḥ svabhāvasaṃsiddhajñānādidharmakalāpādhyāsitasya sthāṇor abhāvapratipādaṇam jainena kurvatāsmākaṃ sāhāyyam anuṣṭhitam iti manvānāḥ prāhuḥ – yuktam uktaṃ yat "svabhāvasaṃsiddhajñānādisaṃpatsamanvitasyeśvarasyābhāvaḥ" "nārakatiryagnarāmarūpapariṇatisvabhāvatayā utpadyante prānino 'sminn" ity etac cāyuktam abhihitam, paralokasadbhāve pramāṇābhāvāt | tathā hi – paralokasadbhāvāvedakam pramāṇaṃ pratyakṣam anumānam āgamo vā jainenābhyupagamanīyaḥ, anyasya pramāṇatvena tenāniṣṭeḥ | na cātraitad vaktavyam – bhavato 'pi kiṃ tatpratikṣepakaṃ pramāṇaṃ ? yato nāsmābhis tatpratikṣepakapramāṇāt tadabhāvaḥ pratipādyate kintu paropanyastapramāṇaparyanuyogamatram eva kriyate | ata eva
 "sarvatra paryanuyogaparāṇy eva sūtrāni bṛhaspateḥ" 
 iti cārvākair abhihitam |
 sa ca paropanyastapramāṇaparyanuyogaḥ tadabhyupagamasya praśnādidvāreṇa vicāraṇā na punaḥ svasiddhapramāṇopanya#āsaḥ, yena "atīndriyārthapratikṣepakatvena pravarttamānaṃ pramāṇam āśrayāsiddhatvādidoṣaduṣṭatvena kathaṃ pravarttate" ity asmān prati bhavatāpi paryanuyogaḥ kriyeta | ata eva paralokaprasādhakapramāṇābhupagamaṃ pareṇa grāhayitvā tadabhyupagamasyānena prakāreṇa vicāraḥ kriyate – 
 tatra na tāvat paralokapratipādakatvena cakṣurādikaraṇavyāpārasamāsāditātmalābhaṃ sannihitapratiniyatarūpādiviṣayatvāt pratyakṣaṃ pravarttate | nāpy atīnriyaṃ yogipratyakṣaṃ tatra pravarttata iti vaktuṃ śakyam, paralokādivat tasyāpy asiddheḥ |
 nāpy anumānaṃ pratyakṣapūrvakaṃ tatra pravṛttim āsādayati, pratyakṣāpravṛttau tatpūrvakasyānumānasyāpi tatrāpraṛtteḥ | atha yady api pratyakṣāvagatapratibandhaliṅgaprabhavam anumānaṃ na tatra pravarrtate tathāpi sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ tatra pravarttiṣyate, tad api na yuktam; yatas tad api sāmānyato dṛṣṭam avagatapratibandhaliṅgodbhavam, āhosvit anavagatapratibandhaliṅgasamuttham ? yady anavagatapratibandhaliṅgodbhavam iti pakṣaḥ, sa na yuktaḥ; tathābhūtaliṅgaprabhavasya svaviṣayavyabhicāreṇa aśvadarśanānantarodbhūtarājyācptivikalpasyevāpramāṇatvāt | atha pratipannasaṃbandhaliṅgaprabhavaṃ tat tatra pravarttata iti pakṣaḥ; so 'pi na yuktaḥ; pratibandhāvagamasyaiva tatra liṅgasyāsaṃbhavāt | tathā hi – pratyakṣasya tatra liṅgasaṃbandhāvagamanimittasyābhāve 'numānaṃ liṅgasaṃbandhagrāhakam abhupagantavyam | tatra yadi tad eva paralokasadbhāvāvedakam anumānaṃ svaviṣayābhimatenārthenātmotpādakaliṅgasaṃbandhagrāhakaṃ tadetaretarāśrayatvadoṣaḥ | athānumānāntarād gṛhītapratibandhāl liṅgād upajāyamānaṃ tadviṣayaṃ tadabhyupagamyate tadānavasthā | tathā, sarvam apy anumānam asmān pratyasiddham | tathā hi – bṛhaspatisūtram -
  "anumānam apramāṇam" [    ]
 iti | anena pratijñānapratipādaṇaṃ kṛtam, aniścitārthapratipādakatvā(tvāt) siddhapramāṇāvhāsavad iti hetudṛṣṭāntāv abhyūhyau |
 viṣayavicāreṇa vānumānaprāmāṇyam ayuktam – dharmadharmyubhayasvatantrasādhane shiddhasādhyatā yataḥ, ato viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ sādhyaḥ | prameyaviśeṣayāṃ pramāṃ kurvat pramāṇaṃ pramāṇatām aśnute | itaretarāvacchinnaś ca samudāyo 'tra prameyaḥ, tadapekṣayā ca pakṣadharmatvādīnāṃ anyatamasyāpi rūpasyāprasiddhiḥ | na hi samudāydharmatā hetoḥ, nāpi samudāyenānvayo vyatireko vā, dharmimātrāpekṣayā parkṣadharmatve sādhyadhamāpekṣayā ca vyāptau gauṇateti | uktaṃ ca – 
  "pramāṇasyāgauṇatvād anumānād arthaniścayo durlabhaḥ" [   ]
 iti |
 
 dharmidharmatāgrahaṇe 'pi na gauṇatāparihāraḥ, pratīyamānāpekṣayā gauṇamukhyavyavahārasya cintyatvāt; samudāyaś ca pratīyate | ekadeśāśrayaṇenāpi trairūpyam ayuktam, vyāptyasiddheḥ | na hi sattāmātreṇāvinābhāvo gamakaḥ api tv avagataḥ, anyathātiprasaṅgāt | sa ca sakalasapakṣavipakṣāpratyakṣīkaraṇe durvijñāno 'sarvavidā | tad uktam -
 gomān ity eva martyena bhāvyam aśvavatāpi kim ? [   ] iti |
 deśakālāvasthābhedena ca bhāvānāṃ nānātvāvagamād anāśvāsaḥ | tad uktam -
 avasthādeśakālānām [Vākyapa° pra° kā° 32] ityādi | āha ca -
 avinābhāvasambandhasya grahītum aśakyatvāt [   ]
 yac ca "sāmānyasya tadviṣayasyābhāvāt, svārthaparārthabhedāsambhavāt, viruddhānumānavirodhayoḥ sarvatra
 sambhavāt, kvacic ca viruddhāvyabicāriṇaḥ" ityādi dūṣaṇajālaṃ tad anudghoṣaṇīyam eva, yato 'niścitārthapratipādakatvād "anumānam apramāṇam" ity anumānāpramāṇatāpratipādane kṛte śeṣadūṣaṇajālasya mṛtamāraṇaakalpatvāt | tato 'numānasyāpramāṇatvād atīndriyaparalokasadbhāvapratipādane kutas tasya pravṛttiḥ ?
 athedam eva janma pūrvajanmāntaram antareṇa na yuktam iti janmāntaralakṣaṇasya paralokasya siddhir iṣyate, tat kim iyam arthāpattiḥ, athānumānaṃ vā ? na tāvad arthāpattiḥ, tallakṣaṇābhāvāt,
 dṛṣṭaḥ śruto vānyathā nopapadyate [Mīmāṃsād° A° 1, Pā° 1, Sū° 5, Śābarabhā°]
 iti hi tasyālakṣaṇaṃ vicakṣaṇair iṣyate | na tu janmāntaram antareṇa nopapattimad idaṃ janmeti siddham, mātāpitṛsāmagrīmātrakeṇa tasyopapatteḥ, tanmāṭrahetukatve cānyaparikalpanāyām atiprasaṅgāt |
 atha prajñāmedhādayo janmādāv abhyāsapūrvakā dṛṣṭāḥ katham atatpūrvakā bhaveyuḥ, na vahnipūrvako dhūmas tatpūrvakatām antareṇa kadācid api bhavann upalabdhaḥ, tad apy asat; avinābhāvasambandhasya deśakālavyāptilakṣaṇasya pratyakṣeṇa pratipattum aśakteḥ – sannihitamātrapratipattinimittaṃ hi pratyakṣam upalabhyate "na hi sakaladeśakālayor vinā vahnim asambhava eva dhūmasya" iti pratyakṣataḥ pratītir yuktā; ato na dhūmo 'pi vahnipūrvakaḥ sarvatra pratyakṣānupalambhābhyāṃ siddha iti kutas tena dṛṣṭāntena janmāntarasvarūpaparalokasādhanam ? tasmāt kecit prajñāmedhādayas tathābhūtābhyāsapūrvakāḥ, kecid mātāpitṛśarīrapūrvakā iti | na ca prajñādayaḥ śarīrato vyatiricyamānasvabhāvāḥ saṃvedanaviṣayatām upayānti, śarīraṃ ca tadanvayavyatirekānuvṛttimad eva dṛṣṭam iti katham anyathā vyavasthām arhati ?
 atha pūrvopāttādṛṣṭam antareṇa kathaṃ mātāpitṛvilakṣaṇaṃ śarīram ? nanv etenaiva vyabhicāro dṛśyate, na hi sarvadā kāraṇānurūpam eva kāryam, tena vilakṣaṇād api mātāpitṛśarīrād yadi prajñāmeghādibhir vilakṣaṇaṃ tadapatyasya śarīram upajāyeta, kadācit tadākārānukāri tat ka evātre virodhaḥ ? yathā kaścit śālūkād eva śālūkaḥ, gomayāt tathā kaścit upadeśād vikalpaḥ, kaścit tadākārapadārthadarśanāt | atha darśanād api vikalpaḥ pūrvavikalpavāsanām antareṇa kathaṃ bhavet ? tarhi gomayād api śālūkaḥ kathaṃ śālūkam antareṇeti etad api praṣṭavyam | tasmāt kāryakāraṇabhāvamātram evaitat | tatra ca niyamābhāvād vijñānād api mātāpitṛśarīrād vijñānam upajāyatām atha vā yathā vikalpād vyavahitād api vikalpa upajāyate tathā vyavahitād api mātāpitṛśarīrata eveti na bhedaṃ paśyāmaḥ | yathā caikamātāpitṛśarīrād anekāpatyotpattis tathaikasmād eva brahmaṇaḥ  prajotpattir iti na jātyantaraparigrahaḥ kasyacid iti na paralokasiddhiḥ | na hi mātāpitṛsambandhamātram eva paralokaḥ, tatheṣṭāv abhyupagamanvirodhāt |
 athānādyanta ātmāsti tam āśritya paralokaḥ sādhyate, na hy ek-ānubhavitṛvyatirekeṇānusandhānaṃ sambhavati, bhinnānubhavitary anusandhānādṛṣṭeḥ, tad apy uktam;
 paralokino 'bhāvāt paralokābhāvaḥ [   ] iti vacanāt |
 na hy anādyananta ātmā pratyakṣapramāṇaprasiddhaḥ | anumānena cetaretar_śrayadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ – siddhe ātmany ekarūpeṇānusandhānavikalpasyāvinābhūtatve ātmasiddheḥ, tatsiddheś cānusandhānasya tadavinābhūtatvasiddir itītaretarāśrayasadbhāvān naikasyāpi siddiḥ | na cāsiddam asiddhena sādhyate | kiṃ ca, darśanānusandhānayoḥ pūrvāparabhāvinoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ pratyakṣasiddhiḥ, tat kuto 'nusandhānasmaraṇād ātmasiddhiḥ ? api ca, śarīrāntargatasya jñāvasyābhūtatvena kathaṃ janmāntaraśarīrasañcāraḥ ? athāntarābhavaśarīrasantatyā sañcaraṇam ucyate, tad api paralokān na viśiṣyate | sañcāraś ca na dṛṣṭo jīvata iha janmani, maraṇasamaye bhaviṣyatīti duradhigamam etat na paralokasiddhiḥ | atha vā siddhe 'pi paraloke pratiniyatakarmaphalasaṃbandhāsidhder vyartham evānumānena paralokāstitvasādhanam | athāgamāt pratiniyatakarmaphalasambandhasiddhiḥ, tathā sati paralokāstitvam apy āgamād eva siddam iti kim anumānaprayāsena ?
 na cāgamād api paralokasiddhiḥ, tasya prāmāṇyāsiddheḥ | na cāpramāṇasiddhaṃ paralokādikam abhyupagantuṃ yuktam, tadabhāvasyāpi tathābhyupagamaprasaṅgāt | tan na paralokasādhakapramāṇapratipādanam akṛtvā
 S. 72
 bhāvaśabdavyutpattir arthasaṃsparśiny abhidhātuṃ yuktā | – ithādiśabdavyutpattitulyā tu yadi kriyate tadā nāsmābhir api tatpratipādakapramāṇaparyanuyoge manaḥ praṇidhīyata iti pūrvapakṣaḥ ||
 
 [uttarapakṣaḥ – paralokasya vyavasthāpanam]
 atrocyate – yad uktam "paryanuyogamātram asmābhiḥ kriyate" iti, tatra vaktavyam – paryanuyogo 'pi kriyamāṇaḥ kiṃ pramāṇataḥ kriyate, utāpramāṇataḥ ? yadi pramāṇatas tad ayuktam, yatas tatkāry api pramāṇaṃ kiṃ pratyakṣatam, utānumānādi ? yadi pratyakṣam, tad ayuktam. pratyakṣasyāvicārakatvena paryanuyogasvarūpavicāraracanācaturatvāt |
 7
 na ca pratyakṣasyāpi pramāṇatvaṃ yuktam, bhavadabhyupagamena tallakṣaṇāsambhavāt | tadasambhavaś ca svarūpavyavasthāpakadharmasya lakṣaṇatvāt | tatra pratyakṣasya prāmāṇyasvarūpavyavasthāpako dharmo 'visaṃvāditvalakṣaṇo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ | tac cāvisaṃvāditvaṃ pratyakṣaprāmāṇyenāvinābhūtam abhyupagamyam, anyathābhūtāt tataḥ pratyakṣaprāmāṇyāsiddheḥ; siddhau vā yataḥ kutaścid yat kiñcid anabhimatam api sidhyed ity atiprasaṅgaḥ | sa cāvinābhāvas tasya kutaṣcit pramāṇād avagantavyaḥ, anavagatapratibandhād arthāntarapratipattau nālikeradvīpavāsino 'py anavagatapratibandhād dhūmād dhūmadhvajapratipattiḥ syāt | avinābhāvāvagamaś cākhiladeśakālavyāptyā pramāṇato 'bhyupagamanīyaḥ; anyathā yasyām eva pratykṣavyaktau saṃvāditvaprāmāṇyayor asāv avagatas tasyām evāvisaṃvāditvāt tat sidhyet na vyaktyantare, tatra tasyānavagamāt | na cāvagatalakṣyalakṣaṇasambandhāvyaktir deśākālāntaram anuvartate, tasyāḥ pratyakṣavyaktes tadaiva dhvaṃsād vyaktyantarānanugamāt | anugame vā vyaktirūpatāvirahāt anugatasya sāmānyarūpatvāt tasya ca bhavatānabhyupagamāt | abhyupagame vā na sāmānyalakṣaṇānumānāviṣayābhāvapratipādanena tatpratikṣepo yuktaḥ | sa ca pramāṇataḥ pratyakṣe lakṣyalakṣaṇayor vyāptyāvinābhāvāvagamo yadi pratyakṣād abhyupagamyate tad ayuktam, pratyakṣasysa sannihitasvaviṣayapratibhāsamātra eva bhavatā vyāpārābhyupagamāt | athaikatra vyaktau pratyakṣeṇa tayor avisaṃvāditvaprāmāṇyayor avinābhāvāvagamād anyatrāpi "evambhūtaṃ pratyakṣam pramāṇam" iti pratyakṣeṇāpi lakṣyalakṣaṇayor vyāptyā pratibandhāvagamas tarhy anyatrāpi "evambhūtaṃ jñānalakṣaṇaṃ kāryam evambhūtajñānakāryaprabhavam" iti tenaiva kathaṃ na sarvopasaṃhāreṇa kāryalakṣaṇahetoḥ svasādhyāvinābhāvāvagamaḥ | yena "anumānam apramāṇam, avinābhāvasambandhasya vyāptyā grahītum aśakyatvāt" iti dūṣaṇam anumānavādinaṃ prati bhavatāsajyamānaṃ śobhate | kiṃ ca, avisasṃvāditvalakṣaṇo dharmaḥ pratyakṣaprāmāṇyalakṣyavyavasthāpakaḥ pratyakṣapratibaddhatvena niśceyaḥ; anyathā tatraiva tataḥ prāmāṇyalakṣaṇalakṣyavyavasthā na syāt, asambaddhasya kenacit saha pratyāsattiviprakarṣābhāvāt tadvad anyatrāpi tatas tadvyavasthāprasaṅgaḥ | tathābhyupagame ca yathā saṃvāditvalakṣaṇo dharmo lakṣyānavagame 'pi pratyakṣadharmisambandhitvenāvagamyate tathā dhūmo 'pi parvataikadeśe analānavagatāv api pradeśasambandhitayāvagamyata iti katham "samudāyaḥ sādhyaḥ, tadapekṣayā ca pakṣadharmatvaṃ hetor avagantavyam, na ca pakṣadharmatvāpratipattau sādhyadharmānalaviśiṣṭatatpradeśapratipattiḥ; pratipattau vā pakṣadharmatvādyanusaraṇaṃ vyartham, tatpratipatteḥ prāg eva tadutpatteḥ | samudāyasysa sādhyatvenopacārāt tadekadeśadharmidharmatvāvagame 'pi pakṣadharmatvāvagamād adoṣe upacaritaṇ pakṣadharmatvaṃ hetoḥ syād ity anumānasya gauṇatvāpatteḥ pramāṇasyāgauṇatvād anumānād arthanirṇayo durlabhaḥ" iti codyāvasaraḥ ? pratyakṣaprāmāṇyalakṣaṇe 'pi kriyamāṇe 'sya sarvasya samānatvena pratipāditatvāt | yadā cāvisaṃvāditvalakṣaṇapratyakṣaprāmāṇyalakṣyayoḥ sarvopasaṃhāreṇa vyāptir abhyupagamyate, avisaṃvāditvalakṣaṇaś ca prāmāṇyavyavasthāpako dharmas tatrāṅgīkriyate pūrvoktanyāyena tadā katham anumānāṃ nābhyupagamyate pramāṇatayā ? tathā hi – "yat kiñcid dṛṣṭaṃ tasya yatrāvinābhāvas tadvidas tasya tad gamakaṃ tatra" ity etāvanmātram evānumānasyāpi lakṣaṇam | tac ca pratyakṣaprāmāṇyalakṣaṇam abhyupagacchatābhyupagamyate devānāṃ priyeṇa | tathā, prāmāṇyam apy anumānasyābhyupagatam eva, yato yad evāvisaṃvāditvalakṣaṇaṃ pratyakṣasya prāmāṇyam anumānasāpi tad eva | tad uktam -
 S. 73
 arthasyāsambhave 'bhāvāt pratyakṣe 'pi pramāṇatā |
 pratibaddhasyabhāvasya taddhetutve samaṃ dvayam || [Pvin I 3] iti |
 arthāsambhave 'bhāvaḥ pratyakṣasya samvādasvabhāvaḥ prāmāṇye nimittam, sa ca sādhyārthābhāve 'bhāvino liṅgād upajāyamānasyānumānasyāpi samāna iti kathaṃ na tasyāpi prāmāṇyābhyupagamaḥ ?
 kiṃ ca, ayaṃ cārvākaḥ pratyakṣaikapramāṇavādī yadi parebhyaḥ pratyakṣalakṣaṇam anavabudhyamānebhyas tat pratipādayati tadā teṣāṃ jñānasambandhitvaṃ kutaḥ pramāṇād avagacchati ? na tāvat pratyakṣāt – paracetovṛttīnāṃ pratyakṣāto jñātum aśakyatvāt – kiṃ tarhi svātmani jñānapūrvakau vyāpāravyāhārau pramāṇato niścitya pareṣv api tathābhūtataddarśanāt tatsambandhitvam avabudhyate tatas tebhyas tat pratipādayati | tathābhyupagame ca vyāpāravyāhārāder liṅgasya jñānasambandhitvalakṣaṇasvasādhyāvyabhicāritvaṃ pakṣadharmatvaṃ cābhyupagataṃ bhavatīti katham anumānotthāpakasyārthasya trairūpyam asiddham – yena nāsmābhir anumānapratikṣepaḥ kriyate kiṃ tu trilakṣaṇaṃ yad anumānavādibhir liṅgam abhyupagataṃ tan na lakṣaṇabhāg bhavatīti pratipādyate iti vacaḥ śobhām anubhavati – pratyakṣalakṣaṇapratipādanārthaṃ paracetovṛttiparijñānābhyupagame trilakṣāṇahetvabhyupagamasyāvaśyaṃbhāvitvapratipādanāt ? atha vāsmābhiḥ pratyakṣam api pramāṇatvenābhyupagamyate – yena tallakṣaṇapraṇayane 'vaśyaṃbhāvī anumānaprāmāṇyābhyupagamaḥ ity asmān prati bhavadbhiḥ pratipādyeta – yat tu "pratyakṣām evaikaṃ pramāṇam" [   ] iti vacanaṃ tat tāntrikalakṣaṇālakṣitalokasaṃvyavahāripratyakṣāpekṣayā, ata eva lakṣaṇalakṣitapratyakṣāpūrvakānumānasya "anumānam apramāṇam" ityādigranthasandarbheṇāprāmāṇyapratipādanaṃ vidhīyate na punar gopālādyajñalokavyavahāraracanācaturasya dhūmadarśanamātrāvirbhūtānalapratipattirūpasya, naitac cāru; tasyāpi mahānasādidṛṣṭāntadharmipravṛttapramāṇāvagatasvasādhyapratibandhaniścitasādhyadharmidharmadhūmabalodbhūtatvena tāntrikalakṣaṇalakṣitapratyakṣāpūrvakatvasya vastutaḥ pradarśitatvāt | etat pakṣadharmatvam iyaṃ cāsya dhūmasya vyāptiḥ iti sāṅketikavyavahārasya gopālādimūrkhalokāsambhavino 'kiñcitkaratvāt | pratyakṣasya cāvisaṃvāditvaṃ prāmāṇylakṣaṇam, tad yathā sambhavati tathā parataḥprāmāṇyaṃ vyavasthāpayadbhiḥ siddhaṃ ity etat padavyākhyāyāṃ darśitaṃ na punar ucyate | tat sthitam etat – na pratyakṣasysa bhavadabhiprāyeṇa prāmāṇyavyavasthāpakalakṣaṇasambhavaḥ; tadbhāve vānumānasyāpi prāmāṇyaprasiddhir iti na pratyakṣaṃ paryanuyogavidhāyi |
 nāpy anumānādikaṃ paryanuyogakāri, anumānādeḥ pramāṇatvenānabhyupagamāt | athāsmābhir yady apy anumānādikaṃ na pramāṇatayābhyupagamyate tathāpi pareṇa tat pramāṇatayābhyupagatam iti tatprasiddhena tena parasya paryanuyogo vidhīyate; nanu parasya tat pramāṇataṃ prāmāṇyābhyupagamaviṣayaḥ, athāpramāṇataḥ ? yadi pramāṇataḥ tadā bhavato 'pi pramāṇaviṣayas tat syāt; na hi pramāṇato 'bhyupagamaḥ kasyacid bhavati kasyacin neti yuktam | athāpramāṇato 'numāṇādikaṃ pramāṇatayābhyupagamyate pareṇa tadāpramāṇena na tena paryanuyogo yuktaḥ, apramāṇasya paralokasādhanavat tatsādhakapramāṇaparyanuyoge 'py asāmarthyāt | atha tena pramāṇalakṣaṇāparijñānāt tatprāmāṇyam abhyupagatam iti tatsiddhenaiva tena paralokādisādhanābhimatapramāṇaparyanuyogaḥ kriyate; nanv ajñānāt tat parasya praṃāṇatvenābhimatam, na cājñānād anyathātvenābhimanyanānaṃ vastu tatsādhyām arthakriyāṃ anurvartayati; anyathā viṣatvenājñair manyamānaṃ mahauṣadhādikam api tān mārtayitukāmena dīyamānaṃ svakāryakaraṇakṣamaṃ syāt | atha vāsmābhiḥ paralokaprasādhakapramāṇaparyanuyogo 'numānādinā svatantraprasiddhaprāmāṇyena, parābhyupagamāv agataprāmāṇyena vā kriyate kiṃ tarhi yadi paralokādiko 'tīndriyo 'rthaḥ pareṇābhyupagamyate tadā tatpratipādakaṃ pramāṇaṃ vaktavyam, pramāṇanibandhanā hi prameyavyavasthitiḥ, tasya ca pramāṇasya tallakṣaṇādyasambhavena tadviṣayābhimatasyāpy abhāvaḥ ity evaṃvicāraṇālakṣaṇaḥ paryanuyogaḥ kriyata iti na svatantrānumānopanyāsapakṣadharmyasiddhyādilakṣaṇadoṣāvakāśo bṛhaspatimatānusāriṇāṃ; nanv evam apy anayā bhaṅgyā bhavatā paralokādyatīndriyārthaprasādhakapramāṇaparyanuyoge prasaṅgasādhanākhyam anumānam tadviparyayasvarūpaṃ ca svavācaiva pratipāditaṃ bhavati |
 tathā hi – pramāṇanibandhanā prameyavyavasthitiḥ ity evaṃ vadatā prameyavyavasthā pramāṇanimittaiva pratipāditā bhavati; etac ca prasaṅgasādhanam, tac ca vyāpyavyāpakabhāve siddhe yatra vyāpyābhyupagamo vyāpakābhyupagamanāṇtarīyakaḥ pradarśyate ity evaṃlakṣaṇam, tena prameyavyavasthā pramāṇapravṛttyā vyāptā pramāṇato bhavatā pradarśanīyā; anyathā pramāṇapravṛttim antareṇāpi prameyavyavasthā syāt | tataś ca kathaṃ paralokādisādhakapramāṇaparyanuyoge 'pi paralokavyavasthā na bhavet ? vyāpyavyāpakabhāvagrāhakapramāṇābhyupagame ca kathaṃ kāryahetoḥ svabhāvahetor vā paralokādiprasādhakatvena pravartamānasya pratikṣepaḥ vyāptiprasādhakapramāṇasadbhāve 'numānapravṛtter anāyāsasiddhatvāt ? pramāṇābhāve tannibandhanāyāḥ prameyavyavasthāyā apy abhāva iti prasaṅgaviparyayaḥ; sa ca vyāpakābhāve vyāpyasyāpy abhāvaḥ ity evambhūtavyāpakānupalabdhisamudbhūtānumānasvarūpaḥ | etad api prasaṅgaviparyayarūpam anumānaṃ pramāṇato vyāpyavyāpakabhāvasiddhau pravartata iti vyāptiprasādhakasya pramāṇasya tatprasādalabhyasya cānumānasya prāmāṇye svavācaiva bhavatā dattaḥ svahasta iti nānumānādiprāmāṇyapratipādane 'smābhiḥ prayasyate | ato yad uktam sarvatra paryanuyogaparāṇy eva sūtrāṇi bṛhaspateḥ iti, tad abhidheyaśūnyam iva lakṣyate uktanyāyāt |
 yat tūktam pratyakṣāṃ sannihitaviṣayatvena cakṣurādiprabhavaṃ paralokādigrāhakatvena na pravartate tan na siddhasādhanam | yac coktam nāpy atīndriyaṃ yogipratyakṣam, paralokavat tasyāsiddheḥ iti, tad vismaraṇaśīlasya bhavato vacanam, atīndriyārthapravṛttipravaṇasya yogipratyakṣasyānantaram eva pratipāditatvāt | yat punar idam ucyate nāpi pratyakṣāpūrvakam anumānaṃ tadabhave pravartate tad asaṅgatam, pratyakṣeṇa hi sambandhagrahaṇapūrvaṃ parokṣe pāvakādau yathānumānaṃ pravartamānam upakabhyate sa eva nyāyaḥ paralokasādhane 'py anumānasya kim ity adṛṣṭo duṣṭo vā | tathā hi – yat kāryaṃ tat kāryāntarodbhūtam, yathā paṭādilakṣaṇaṃ kāryam, kāryaṃ cedaṃ janma iti bhavaty ato hetoḥ paralokasiddhiḥ | tathā hi -
  nityaṃ sattvam asattvaṃ vāhetor anyānapekṣaṇāt |
  apekṣāto hi bhāvānāṃ kādācitkatvasambhavaḥ || XE "[PV I 35=37]"[PV I 35=37] iti |
 na tāvat kāryatvam ihajanmano na siddham, akāryatve hetunirapekṣasya nityaṃ sattvāsattvaprasaṅgāt | atha svabhāvata eva kādācitkatvaṃ padārthānāṃ abhaviṣyati, na hi kāryakāraṇabhāvapūrvakatvaṃ pratyakṣata upalabdhaṃ yena tadabhāvān nivarteta, pratyakṣataḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvasyaivāsiddheḥ | yady evaṃ bāhyenāpy arthena saha kāryakāraṇabhāvasyāsiddheḥ svasaṃvedanamātratve sati advaitam. vicāratas tasyāpy abhāve sarvaśūnyatvam iti sakalavyavahārocchedaprasaktiḥ | tasmād yathā pratyakṣeṇa bāhyārthapratibaddhatvam ātmanaḥ pratīyate – anyathehalokasyāpy aprasiddheḥ, pratyakṣataḥ tajjanyasvabhāvatvānavagame tasya tadgrāhakatvāsambhavāt; tathā cehalokasādhanārtham aṅgīkartavyaṃ pratyakṣaṃ svārthenātmanaḥ pratibandhasādhakam – tathā paralokasādhanārtham api tad eva sādhanam iti siddhaḥ paraloko 'anumānataḥ  yathā ca bāhyārthapratibaddhatvaṃ pratyakṣasya kādācitkatvena sādhyate, dhūmasyāpi vahnipratibaddhatvam, tathehajanmano 'pi kādācitkatvena janmāntarapratibaddhatvam api | tato 'nala – bāhyārthavat paraloke 'pi siddham anumānam |
 athehajanmādibhūtamātāpitṛsāmagrīmātrād apy utpatteḥ kādācitkatvaṃ yuktam evehajanmanaḥ; nanv evaṃ pradeśāsamanantarapratyayamātrasāmagrīviśeṣād eva dhūmapratyakṣasaṃvedanayoḥ kādācitkatvam iti na sidhyati vahnibāhyārthapratītir iti sakalavyavahārābhāvaḥ | athākāraviśeṣād evānanyathātvasambhavino 'nalabāhyārthasiddhis tarhīhajanmano 'pi prajñāmedhādyākāraviśeṣata eva mātāpitṛvyatiriktanijajanmāntarasiddiḥ | tathā, yathākāraviśeṣa evāyaṃ taimirikādijñānavyāvṛttaḥ pratyakṣasya bāhyārtham antareṇa na bhavatīti niścīyate – anyathā bāhyārthāsiddher bauddhābhimatasaṃvedanādvaitam eveti punar api vyavahārābhāvaḥ – tathehajanmādibhūtaprajñāviśeṣād ihajanmaviśeṣākāro nijajanmāntarapratibaddha iti niścīyatām anumānataḥ | atha pratyakṣam eva savikalpakaṃ paramārthataḥ pratipattuḥ tataḥ paraṃ punar vastu dharmaiḥ [ŚV 4, 120] ityādi mīmāṃsakādiprasiddhaṃ sādhakaṃ vahnibāhyarthapūrvakatvasya dhūmajāgratpurovṛttistambhādipratyayasya, atrābhyupagame paralokavādinaḥ svapakṣam anāyāsasiddham eva manyante; na hi dṛṣṭe 'nupapannam iti nyāyāt |
 S. 75
 yatheva hi niścayarūpā mātāpitṛjanmapratibaddhatvasiddis tathaivehajanmasaṃskāravyāvṛttād ihajanmaprajñādyākāraviśeṣān nijajanmāntarapratibaddhatvasiddhir api pratyakṣaniścitā syād iti na paralokakṣatiḥ | na ca niścayapratyayo 'nabhyāsadaśāyām anumānatām atikrāmati, pūrvarūpasādharmyāt tat tathāprasādhitaṃ nānumeyatām atipatati XE "[PVin I 36,5f]"[PVin I 36,5f] iti nyāvāt anvayavyatirekapakṣadharmatānusaraṇasyānabhyāsadaśāyām upalabdheḥ; abhyāsadaśāyāṃ ca pakṣadharmatvādyanusaraṇasyānyatrāpy asaṃvedanāt siddam anumānapratitatvaṃ paralokasya |
 athetaretarāśrayadoṣād anumānaṃ nāsty evaivaṃvidhe viṣaye ity ucyeta; nanv evaṃ sati sarvabhedābhāvato vyavahāroccheda iti taducchedam anabhyupagacchatā vyavahārārthināvaśyam anumānam abhyupagantavyam | etena pratyakṣapūrvakatvābhāve 'py anumānasya prāmāṇyaṃ pratipāditam | na cānumānapūrvakatve 'pītaretarāśrayadoṣānuṣaṅgaḥ, tasyaivetaretarāśrayadoṣasya vyavahārapravṛttito nirākaraṇāt |
 yad apy uktam anumānapūrvakatve 'navasthāprasaṅgān nānumānapravṛttiḥ iti, tad apy asaṅgatam; evaṃ hi sati pratyakṣagṛhīte 'py arthe vipratipattivi-ṣaye nānumānapravṛttim antareṇa tannirāsa iti bāhye 'rthe pratyakṣasyāvyāp-ārāt punar apy advaitāpatteḥ śūnyatāpatter vā vyavahāroccheda iti vyavahārabalāt saivānavasthā parihriyata iti | abhyupagamavādena caitad uktam anyathā bāhyārthavyavasthāpanāya pratyakṣaṃ pravartate | tathā, pradarśitahetor vyāptiprasādhanārthaṃ keṣāṃcid matena nirvikalpam, anyeṣāṃ tu savikalpakaṃ cakṣurādikaraṇavyāpārajanyam, apareṣāṃ mānasam, keṣāṃcid vyāvṛttigrahaṇopayogi jñānam, anyeṣāṃ pratyakṣānupalambhabalodbhūtāliṅgajohākhyaṃ parokṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ tatra vyāpriyata iti katham anumānena pratibandhagrahaṇe 'navasthetaretarāśrayadoṣaprasaktiḥ paralokavādinaḥ prati bhavatā preryeta ?
 !!!20
 yad apy uktam sarvam apy anumānam asmān prati pramāṇatvenāsiddham ityādi, tad apy asaṅgatam; yataḥ kim anumānamātrasyāprānāṇyaṃ bhavatā pratipādayitum abhipretam anumānam apramānam ityādigranthena, atha tāntrikalakṣāṇakṣepaḥ, atīndriyārthānumānapratikṣ_po vā ? na tāvad anumānamātrapratiṣedho yuktaḥ, lokavyavahārocchedaprasaṅgāt | yataḥ pratiyanti kovidāḥ kasyacid arthasya darśane niyamataḥ kiñcid arthāntaraṃ na tu sarvasmāt sarvasyāvagamaḥ | uktaṃ cānayena
  svagṛhān nirgato bhūyo na tadāgantum arhati [   ]
 athaḥ kiñcid dṛṣṭvā kasyacid avagame nimittaṃ kalpanīyam | tac ca niyatasāhacaryam avinābhāvaśabdavācyaṃ naiyāyikādibhiḥ parikalpitam | tadavagamaś ca pratyakṣānupalambhasahāyamānasapratyakṣataḥ pratīyate | sāmānyadvāreṇa pratibandhāvagamād deśādivyabhicāro na bādhakaḥ, nāpi vyaktyānantyam, ubhayatrāpi sāmānyasyaikatvāt | sāmānyākṛṣṭāśeṣavyaktipratibhānāṃ ca mānase pratyakṣe yathā śatasaṅkhyāvaccedena śatam iti pratyaye viśeṣaṇākṛṣṭānāṃ pūrvagṛhītānāṃ śatasaṅkhyāviṣayapadārthānām | tathā hi – ete śatam iti pratyayo bhavaty eva | sāmānyasya ca sattvam anugatābādhitapratyayaviṣayatvena vyavasthāpitam | tad evaṃ niyatasāhacaryam arthāntaraṃ pratipādayad upalabdhaṃ sat pratipādayati | upalambhaś cāvaśyaṃ kvacit sthitasya, saiva pakṣadharmatā, tataḥ sambandhānusmṛtau tataḥ sādhyāvagamaḥ | yas tu pratibandhaṃ nopaiti tasyāpi kathaṃ na sarvasmāt sarvapratipattiḥ abhyupagame vāpratipanne 'pi sambandhe pratipattiprasaṅgaḥ ? pramātṛsaṃskārakārakāṇāṃ pūrvadarśanānām abhāvāt ity anuttaram, sambandhāpratipattau pramātṛsaṃskārānupapatteḥ | darśanajaḥ saṃskāro 'py anabhivyaktaḥ sattāmātreṇa na pratipattyupayogī; na ca smṛtim antareṇa tatsadbhāvo 'pi | na cānubhavapradhvaṃsanibandhanā smṛtiḥ kvacid viṣaye, saṃskāram antareṇa tadanupapatteḥ pradhvaṃsasya ca nirhetukatvāsambhavāt | yatrāpy abhyaste viṣaye vastvantaradarśanād avyavadhānena vastvantarapratipattis tatrāpi prāktanakramāśrayaṇena vastvantarāvagamaḥ | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ – ekatrānabhyastatvād antarāle smṛtisaṃvedanam, anyatrābhyāsād vidyamānāyā apy asaṃvittiḥ |
 S. 76
 kecit tu yogipratyakṣaṃ sambandhgrāhakam āhur vyāpteḥ sakalākṣepeṇāvagamāt tathā ca yatra yatreti deśakālavikṣiptānāṃ vyaktīnām anavabhāsānupapattiḥ, ata ekatra kṣeṇe yogitvaṃ pratibandhagrāhiṇaḥ | etat pūrvasmād aviśiṣṭam, tad loke arthāntaradarśanāt arthāntarasudṛḍhaptatītau tārkikāṇāṃ nimittacintāyāṃ pakṣadharmatvādyabhidhānam | ato na tāntrikalakṣaṇapratikṣepe 'pi |
 utpannapratītīnām astu prāmāṇyam, utpādyapratītīnāṃ tu atīndriyādṛṣṭaparalokasarvajñādyanumānānāṃ pratikṣepa iti cet, tad asat; yady anavagatasambandhān pratipattXṛn adhikṛtyaitad ucyate tadā dhūmādiṣv api tulyam | atha gṛhītāvinābhāvānām apy atīndriyaparalokādipratibhāsānutpatter evam ucyate, tad asat; ye hi kāryaviśeṣasya tadviśeṣeṇa gṛḥītāvinābhāvās te tasmāt paralokādyavagacchaty eva; ato na jñāyate kena viśeṣaṇātīndriyārthānumānapratikṣepaḥ ? sāhacaryāviśeṣe 'pi vyāpyagatā niyatatā prayojikā na vyāpakagatā, ataḥ samavyāptikānām api vyāpyamukhenaiva pratipattiḥ | niyatatāvagame cārthāntarapratipattau na bādhā, na pratibandhaḥ, ekasya rūpabhedānupapatteḥ  tato na viśeṣaviruddhasambhavaḥ nāpi viruddhāvyabhicāriṇa iti yad uktam viruddhānumānavirodhayoḥ sarvatra sambhavāt kvacic ca viruddhāvyabhicāriṇaḥ iti, etad apy apāstam | avinābhāvasambandhasya grahītum aśakyatvāt, avasthādeśakālādibhedāt ityadeś ca pūrvanītyānumānapramāṇatve 'nupapattiḥ |
 parokṣasyārthasya sāmānyākāreṇānyataḥ pratipattau lokapratītāyāṃ bauddhais tu kāryakāraṇabhāvādilakṣāṇaḥ pratibandhas tannimittatvena kalpitaḥ | tad uktam -
 kāryakāraṇabhāvād vā svabhāvād vā niyāmakāt |
 avinābhāvaniyamo 'darśanān na na darśanāt || XE "[PV I 31=33]"[PV I 31=33] ityādi |
 tathā -
 avaśyaṃbhāvaniyamaḥ kaḥ parasyānyathā paraiḥ |
 arthāntaranimitte vā dharme vāsasi rāgavat || XE "[PV I 32=34]"[PV I 32=34] iti ca |
 tathā hi – kvacit parvatādideśe dhūma upalabhyamāno yady agnim antareṇaiva syāt tadā pāvakadharmānuvṛttitas tasya tatkāryatvaṃ yan niścitaṃ viśiṣṭapratyakṣānupalambhābhyāṃ tad eva na syād ity ahetos tasyāsasttvāt kvacid apy upalambho na syāt, sarvadā sarvatra sarvākāreṇa vopalambhaḥ syāt; ahetoḥ sarvadā sattvāt| svabhāvaś ca yadi bhāvavyatirekeṇa syāt tato bhāvasya niḥsvabhāvatvāpatteḥ svabhāvasyāpy abhāvāpattiḥ | tatpratibandhasādhakaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ kāryahetor viśiṣṭapratyakṣānupalambhaśabdavācyaṃ pratyakṣam eva sarvajñasādhakahetupratibandhaniścayaprastāve pradarśitam | svabhāvahetos tu kasyacid viparyaye bādhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ vyāpakānupalabdhisvarūpam. kasyacit tu viśiṣṭaṃ pratykṣam abhyupagatam | sarvathā sāmānyadvāreṇa vyaktīnām atadrūpaparāvṛttavyaktirūpeṇa vā tāsāṃ pratibandho 'bhyupagantavyaḥ; anyathāpratibaddhād anyato 'nyapratipattāv atiprasaṅgāt | pratipandhaprasādhakaṃ ca pramāṇam avaśyam abhyupagamanīyam; anyathāgṛhītapratibandhatvād anyato 'nyapratipattāv api prasaṅgas tadavastha eva | yatra gṛhītapratibandho  'sāv artha upalabhyamāṇaḥ sādhyasiddhiṃ vidadhāti taddharmatā tasya pakṣadharmatvasvarūpā, tadgrāhakaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ pratyakṣam anumānaṃ vā | tad uktaṃ dharmakīrtiṇā -
 pakṣadharmatāniścayaḥ pratyakṣato 'numānato vā XE "[cf. HB 2,12f]"[cf. HB 2,12f]
 ato lokaprasiddhatāntrikalakṣaṇalakṣitānumānayor bhedābhāvād atīndriyaparalokādyarthasādhakatvam api tasyaiveti tatprāmāṇyānabhyupagame ihalokasyāpi abhyupagamābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | na ca kim atra nirvikalpakam, yogipratyakṣam, ūho vā pratibandhaniścāyakam pratibandho 'pi niyatasāhacaryalakṣāṇaḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvādir vā iti cintātropayoginī, dhūmād agnipratipattivat prajñāmedhādinijñānakāryaviśeṣān nijajanmāntaravijñānasvabhāvaparalokapratipattisiddheḥ | ato 'numānāprāmāṇyapratipādanāya pūrvapakṣavādinā yad yuktijālam upavyastaṃ tan nirastaṃ draṣṭavyam; pratipadam uccārya na dūṣyate granthagauravabhayāt |
 S. 77
 yad apy uktam paraloke pratyakṣasyāpravṛtter arthāpatter eveyam ihajamnānyathānupapattyā paralokasadbhāvaḥ iti, tad api na samyak; pūrvānusareṇa sarvasya niyatapratyayasya pravṛtter anumānatvapratipādanāt | avinābhāvasambandhasya grahītum aśakyatvān nātrānumānam iti cet; nanv evaṃ tad evādvaitaṃ śūnyatvaṃ vā kasya kena doṣābhidhānam | tasmāt saṃvyavahārakāriṇā pratyakṣena ūhena vā pratibandhasiddhir iti kathaṃ nānumānāt paralokasiddiḥ ?
 yad apy uktam mātāpitṛsāmagrīmātreṇehajanmasambhavān na tajjanmavyatiriktabhūtaparalokasādhanaṃ yuktam iti, tad api prativihitam eva; samanantarapratyayamātreṇa pratyayapratyakṣasya bhāvāt svapnādipratyayavan na pratyakṣād bāhyārthasiddhir api iti bauddhābhimatapakṣasiddhiprasaṅgo 'nastatvāt | yad api pratyapādi na sannihitamātraviṣayatvāt pratyakṣasya deśakālavyāptyā pratibandhagrahaṇasāmarthyam iti, tad api na kiñcit; evaṃ sati atisannihitaviṣayatvena pratyakṣasya svarūpamātra eva pravṛttiprasaṅga iti tad eva bauddhādiabhimataṃ svasaṃvedanamātraṃ sarvavyavahārocchedakāri prasaktam iti pratipāditatvāt | tasmāl lokavyavahārapravarttanakṣamasavikalpakapratyakṣabalād ūhākhyapramāṇād vā deśakālavyāptyā yathoktalakṣaṇasya hetoḥ pratibandhagrahaṇe pravṛttir anumānasyeti na vyāhatiḥ prakṛtasyeti etad api nirastam kecit prajñādayaḥ ityādi | na ca prajñāmedhādayaḥ śarīrasvabhāvāntargatāḥ ityādi codyaṃ yuktam, tadantargatatve 'pi parihārasambhavād anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ teṣāṃ mātāpitroḥ pitṛśarīrajanyatvasya pitṛśarīraṃ (?) tarhi hetubhedān na bhedo mātāpitṛśarīrād apatyaprajñādīnām | ayam aparo bṛhaspatimatānusāriṇa eva doṣo 'stu yaḥ kāryabhede 'pi kāraṇabhedaṃ necchati | asmākaṃ tu harṣaviṣādādyanekaviruddhadharmākrāntasya vijñānasyāntarmukhākāratayā vedyasya rūparasagandhasparśādiyugapadbhāvibālakumārayauvanavṛddhāvasthādyanekakramabhāviviruddhadharmābhyāsitataccharīrāder bāhyendriyaprabhavavijñānasamadhigamyād bhedaḥ siddha eva | viruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ, kāraṇabhedaś ca padārthānāṃ bhedakaḥ; sa ca jalānalayor iva śarīravijñānayor vidyata eveti kathaṃ na tayor bhedaḥ ? tadbhedād apy abhede brahmādvaitavādāpattes tadavastha eva pṛthivyāditatvacatuṣṭayābhāvāpattyā vyavahārocchedaḥ | athavā mātāpitṛpūrvajanmaikasāmagrījanyam etat kāryam, etan na (tan na) doṣo 'vyatiriktapakṣe 'pi vijñānaśarīrayoḥ | pūrvam apy uktam vilakṣanād apy anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ mātāpitṛśarīrād vijñānam upajāyatām; na hi kāraṇākāram eva sakalaṃ kāryam iti, tad apy asat; yato na hi kāraṇavilakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ na bhavatīpy ucyate, api tu tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhānāt tatkāryatvam | tathā hi – yad yadvikārānvayavyatirekānuvidhāyi tat tatkāryam iti vyavasthāpyate, yathā agurukarpūrorṇādidāhyadāhakapāvakagatasurabhigandhādyanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyī dhūmas tatkāryatayā vyavasthitaḥ, ekasantatyanupatitaśāstrasaṃskārādisaṃskṛtaprāktanavijñānadharmānvayavyatirekānuvidhāyi ca prajñāmedhādyuttaravijñānam iti kathaṃ na tatkāṛyam abhyupagamyate ? tad anabhyupagame dhūmāder api prasiddhavahnyādikāryasya tatkāryatvāprasiddhir iti punar api sakalavyavahārocchedaḥ |
 tasmād yasyaiva saṃskāraṃ niyamenānuvartate |
 tannāntarīyakaṃ cittam ataś cittasamāśritam || XE "[PV II 78 Abweichung]"[PV II 78 Abweichung]
 pratipāditaś ca pramāṇataḥ pratiniyataḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ sarvajñasādhane kusamayavisāsaṇaṃ iti padavyākhyāṃ kurvadbhir na punar ihocyate |
 yo 'pi śālūkadṛṣṭāntena vyabhicāraḥ yathā gomayād api śālūkaḥ, kaścit samānajātīyād api śālūkād eva tathā kecit prajñāmedhādayas tadabhyāsāt, kecit tu rasāyanopayogāt,
 S. 78
 apare mātāpitṛśukraśoṇitaviṣeśād eva iti, so 'pi na samyag; tatrāpi samānajātīyapūrvābhyāsasambhavāt; anyathā samāne 'pi rasāyanādyupayoge yamalakayoḥ kasyacit kvāpi prajñāmedhādikam iti pratiniyamo na syāt, rasāyanādyupayogasya sādhāraṇatvād iti | na ca prajñādīnāṃ janmādau rasāyanābhyāde ca viśeṣaḥ, śālūkagomayajanyasya tu śālūkādes tadanyasmād viśeṣo dṛśyate | kvacij jātismaraṇaṃ ca darśanam iti na yuktā dṛṣṭakāraṇād eva māṭāpitṛśarīrāt prajñāmedhādikāryaviśeṣotpattiḥ | na ca gomayaśālūkāder vyabhicāraviṣayatvena pratipāditasyātyantavailakṣaṇyam, rūparasagandhasparśavat pudgalapariṇāmatvena dvayor api availakṣaṇyāt | vijñānaśarīrayoś cāntarbahirmukhākāravijñānagrāhyatayā svaparasaṃvedyatayā svasaṃvedanabāhyakaraṇādijanyapratyayānubhūyamānatayā ca parasparānanuyāyyanekaviruddhadharmādhyāsato 'tyantavailakṣaṇyasya pratipāditatvād nopādānopādeyabhāvo yuktaḥ |
 yas tu śarīravṛddhyādeś caitanyavṛddhyādilakṣaṇa upādānopādeyabhāvadharmopalambhaḥ pratipādyate 'sau mahākāyasyāpi mātaṅgājagarādeś caitanyālpatvena vyabhicārīti na tadbhāvasādhakaḥ | yas tu śarīravikārāac caitanyavikāropalambhalakṣaṇas taddharmabhāvaḥ pratipādyate 'sāv api sāttvikasattvānām anyagatacittānāṃ vā chedādilakṣaṇaśarīravikārasadbhāve 'pi taccittavikārānupalabdher asiddhaḥ | dṛśyate ca sahakāriviśeṣād api jalabhūmyādilakṣaṇād bījopādānasyāṅkurāder viśeṣa iti sahakārikāraṇatve 'pi śarīrāder viśiṣṭāh-ārādyupayogādau yauvanāvastāyāṃ vā śāstrādisaṃskāropāttaviśeṣapūrvajñānopādānasya vijñānasya vivṛddhilakṣaṇo viśeṣo nāsambhavī |
 yad apy uktam anādimātāpitṛparamparāyāṃ tathābhūtasyāpi bodhasya vyavahitamātāpitṛgatasya sadbhāvāt tato vāsanāprabodhena yukta eva prajñāmedhādiviśeṣasya sambhavaḥ iti, tad apy ayuktam; anantarasyāpi mātāpitṛpāṇḍityasya prāyaḥ prabodhasambhavāt; tataś cakṣurādikaraṇajanitasya svarūpasaṃvedanasya cakṣurādijñānasya vā yugapat krameṇa cotpattau mayaivopalabdham etat iti pratyabhijñānaṃ santānāntaratadapatyajñānānām api syāt, na ca mātāpitṛjñānopalabdhes tadapatyādeḥ kasyacit pratyabhijñānam upalabhyate | anena ekasmād brahmaṇaḥ prajotpattiḥ pratyuktā, ekaprabhavatve hi sarvaprāṇināṃ parasparaṃ pratyabhijñāprasaṅgaḥ ekasantānodbhūtadarśanasparśanapratyayor iva |
 yad uktam āzmāno 'dṛṣṭer nātmānam āśritya paralokaḥ iti, tad ayuktam; tadadṛṣṭyasiddheḥ | tathā hi – dehendriyaviṣayādivyatirikto 'haṃpratyayapratyakṣopalabhya evātmā | na ca cakṣurādeḥ karaṇagrāmasyātīndriyātmaviṣāyatvena jñānajananāvyāparāt kathaṃ tajjanyapratyakṣajñānaviṣaya iti vaktuṃ yuktam, svasambedanapratyakṣagrāhyatvābhyupagamāt | tathā hi – upasaṃhṛtasakalendriyavyāpārasya andhakārasthitasya ca aham iti jñānaṃ sarvaprāṇinām upajāyamānaṃ svasaṃviditam anubhūyate, tatra ca śarīrādyanavabhāse 'pi tadvyatiriktam ātmasvarūpaṃ pratibhāti | na caitad jñānam anubhūyamānam apy apahnotuṃ śakyam, anubhūyamānasyāpy apalāpe sarvāpalāpaprasaṅgāt | nāpy etan notpadyate, kādācitkatvavirodhāt | nāpi bāhyendriyavyāpāraprabhavam, tadvyāpārābhāve 'py upajāyamāvatvāt | nāpi śabdaliṅgādinimittodbhūtam, tadabhāve 'py utpattidarśanāt | na cedaṃ bādhyatvenāpramāṇam, tatra bādhakasadbhāvasyāsiddheḥ | na cedaṃ savikalpakatvenāpramāṇam, savikalpakasyāpi jñānasya pramāṇatvena pratipādayiṣyamāṇatvāt | kadācic ca bāhyendryavyāpārakāle 'pi yadā ghaṭam ahaṃ jānāmi ity evam viṣayam avagacchati tadā svātmānam api | tathā hi – tatra yathā viṣayasyāvabhāsaḥ karmatayā tathātmano 'py anabhāsaḥ kartṛtayā | na ca śarīrādīnāṃ jñātṛtā, yathā hi śarīrād vyatiriktā ghaṭādayaḥ pratītikarmatayā pratibhānti – mama ghaṭādayaḥ, ahaṃ ghaṭādīnāṃ jñātā evaṃ mama śarīrādayaḥ, ahaṃ śarīrādīnāṃ jñātā ity evaṃ ca pratītikarmatvena ghaṭādibhis tulyatvān na śarīrādisaṅghātasya jñātṛtā | na ca jñātrapratibhāsam, tadapratibhāse hi mamaite bhāvāḥ pratibhānti nānyasya ity evaṃ pratibhāso na syāt tad avabhāsāpahnave ca ghaṭāder api kathaṃ pratītiḥ ? iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ – ekasya pratītikarmatā aparasya tatpratītikartṛtā na tv anavabhāsaḥ; ato liṅgādyanapekṣa ātmāvabhāso 'py astīti kathaṃ tasyādṛṣṭiḥ ?
 S. 79
 kathaṃ tasyādṛṣṭiḥ ? na cāsya pratyayasya bādhārahitasyāpūrvārthaviṣayasyākṣajaviṣayāvabhāsasyevāsandigdharūpasya niścitarūpatvena pratibhāsamānasya smṛtirūpatā aprāmāṇayaṃ na pratipādayituṃ yuktam | ato 'syām api pratītāv avabhāsamāvasyāparokṣataiva yuktā na pramāṇāntaragamyatā |
 yad apy atrāhuḥ – {asty ayam avabhāsaḥ kin tv asya pratykṣatā cintyā | pratyakṣaṃ hīndriyavyāpārajaṃ jñānam | tadā coktaṃ bhavadbhiḥ – indriyānāṃ satsamprayoge buddhijanma pratyakṣam [MS 1.1.4] pratyakṣaviṣayatvāt tadarthasya pratyakṣatā na tu sākṣāt, anindriyajatvena | tatra ghaṭāder bāhyendriyajñānaviṣayatvena sarvalokapratītādhyakṣatā na tv evam ātmanaḥ |
 athaivam ucyeta – nātmano ghaṭāditulyā pratyakṣatā, ghaṭāder hi indriyajajñānaviṣayatvena sā vyavasthāpyate, na tv ātmā kasyacit pramāṇasya viṣayaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi pratyakṣaḥ ? na jñānaviṣayatvāt pratyakṣaḥ api tv aparokṣātvena pratibhāsanāt pratyakṣa ucyate; tac ca kevalasya ghaṭādipratītyantargatasya vāparasādhanaṃ prāk pratipāditam, etad apy asat; yataḥ aparasādhanam iti ko 'rthaḥ – kiṃ cidrūpasya sattā, āhosvit svapratītau vyāpāraḥ ? yadi cidrūpasya sattaivātmaprakāśanam ucyate tadā dṛṣṭānto vaktavyaḥ | na cātrāśaṅkanīyam – aparokṣe dṛṣṭāntānveṣaṇaṃ na kartavyam, yatas tathāvidhe vivādaviṣaye suprasiddhaṃ dṛṣṭāntānveṣaṇaṃ dṛśyate | na ca dīpādi dṛṣṭāntaḥ, tatra hi sajātīyālokānapekṣatvena svapratītau svaprakāśakatvaṃ vyavasthāpitaṃ kaiścit na tv indriyāgrāhyatvam; tad agrāhyatve svaprakāśāḥ pradīpādayaḥ iti cakṣuṣmatābhivāndhānām api tatpratītiprasaṅgaḥ; tasmān na svaprakāśāḥ pradīpādayaḥ | yat tu ālokāntaranirapekṣatvaṃ tat kasyacid viṣayasya kācit sāmagrī prakāśikā iti naikatra dṛṣṭatvenānyatrāpi prasaktiś codyate | atha dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ, so 'py ayuktaḥ; adarśanād eva – na hi kaścit padārthaḥ kartṛūpaḥ karaṇarūpo vā svātmani karmaṇīva savyāpāro dṛṣṭaḥ | kathaṃ tarhy anumeyatve 'py ātmapratītiḥ pramātrantarābhāvāt  ? ekasyaivaliṅgādikaraṇam apekṣyāvasthābhedena bhede sati adoṣāḥ | kiṃ ca, pramāṇaviṣayatve 'py aparokṣatety asya bhāṣitasya ko 'rthaḥ ? jñātṛtayā svarūpeṇāvabhāsanam iti cet, ghaṭāsyo 'pi kiṃ pararūpatayā pratītiviṣayāḥ ? ato yad yasya rūpaṃ tat pramāṇaviṣayatve 'py avasīyate iti na jñānāviṣayatā pramātuḥ | tathā hi – tasya jñātṛtā pramātṛtātmasvarūpatā, ghaṭādeḥ prameyatāṃ jñeyatā ghaṭādirūpatā; ato yathā tasya svarūpeṇāvabhāsanān nāpratyakṣatā tadvad ātmano 'pi | abhyupagamanīyaṃ caitat, anyathātmādisvasaṃvedanasya pratyakṣasyāpi pratyakṣādilakṣaṇavyatiriktaṃ lakṣaṇāntaraṃ vaktavyam; tathā ca pramāṇeyattāvyāghātaḥ, kenacit pratyakṣādilakṣaṇenātmādiviṣayasya svasaṃvedanasyāsaṅgrahāt | ito 'py uktaṃ pramātṛvat phale 'pi saṃvedanābhyupagamaprasaṅgāt | tathābhyupagamād adoṣa iti cet, tathā coktam -
 saṃvittiḥ saṃvittitayaiva saṃvedyā na saṃvedyatayā [   ] iti |
 etat prāk pratikṣiptam, na svarūpāvabhāse pramāṇāviṣayatā | kiṃ ca, evaṃ kalpyamāne bodhadvayam āntaram svasaṃvidrūpaṃ ca kalpitaṃ syāt, tathā cāyuktam, ekasmād eva viṣayāvabhāsasiddheḥ kiṃ dvayakalpanayā ? athocyate – kalpanā hy anavabhāsamānasya, bodhadvaye tu ghaṭādivad avabhāso 'stīti na kalpanā | yadīdṛśāḥ pratibhāsāḥ pramāṇatvena vyavasthāpyante tadā ghaṭaṃ aham cakṣuṣā paśyāmi iti karaṇapratītir api pramātṛphalapratītivat kalpanīyā |
 yāpi kaiścit karaṇapratītiḥ pratakṣatvenoktā sāpi nātīva saṅgacchate | tathā hi ghaṭam ahaṃ cakṣuṣā paśyāmi ity asyām avagatau kiṃ golakasya cakṣuṣṭvam , āhosvit tadavyatiriktasya ? golakasya cakṣuṣṭve na kaścid andhaḥ syāt | tadvyatiriktasya ca raśmer anaghyupagamaḥ; abhyupagame vā na pratītiviṣayaḥ, kevalaṃ śabdamātram uccārayati ghaṭapratītikāle | evaṃ ca pramātṛphalaviṣayaṃ śabdoccāraṇamātram avasīyate, na ca tayoḥ pratītigocaratā karaṇasyeva tathā hi – indriyavyāpāre sati śarīrād vyavacchinnasya viṣayasyaiva kevalasyāvabhāsanam iti nyāyavidaḥ pratipannāḥ |
 80
 kiṃ tasyāvabhāsanam iti paryanuyoge mūkatvaṃ parihāram āhuḥ, vyapadeṣṭum aśakyatvāt | ataḥ pramātravabhāsānupapattiḥ |
 nanu aham iti pratyayaḥ sarvalokasākṣiko naivāpahnotuṃ śakyaḥ | anapahnave saviṣayaḥ, nirviṣayo vā ? nirviṣayatā pratyayānām abādhitarūpāṇāṃ kathaṃ ? saviṣayatve 'pi pramātrapratibhāse kiṃviṣayo 'yaṃ pratyayāt ? na pratyayāpahnavaḥ na cāsya nirviṣayatā kin tu dehādivyatirikto viṣayatvenāvabhāsamāna ātmāsya na viṣayaḥ na ca jñātṛtvenāvabhāsamāna ity ucyate – kas tarhi viṣayaḥ ? śarīram iti brūmaḥ | tathā hi kṛśo 'ham sthūlo 'ham gauro 'ham iti śarīrādyālambanaiḥ pratyayaiṛ asya samānādhikaraṇatāvasīyate |
 nanv evaṃ sukhyādipratyayair apy ahaṅkārasya samānādhikaraṇatā – sukhy ahaṃ duḥkhy aham iti vā ato na dehaviṣayatā | yac codyate gauro 'ham ityādisāmānādhikaraṇyadarśanāc charīrālambanatvam iti, tatrāpy etad vicāryam – gaurādīnāṃ śarīrādivyatiriktānām anahaṅkārāspadatvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tadvaccharīrādigatānāṃ api yuktaṃ vyavasthāpayitum | tathā ca vārttikakṛtoktam – na hy asya draṣṭur yad etad mama gauraṃ rūpaṃ so 'ham iti bhavati pratyayaḥ, kevalaṃ matublopaṃ kṛtvaivaṃ nirdarśati [NV 341,23] ayam arthaḥ – śarīre 'haṅkāra aupacāriko na tāttvikaḥ, yathānyasmiṃs tatkāryakāriṇyatyantanikaṭe 'haṅkāṛo gauṇaḥ yo 'yam so 'ham iti, evaṃ śarīre 'pi | ato nimittād ayam etasmiṃs tūbhaysampratipanne 'nātmarūpe idaṃtāpratyayaviṣaye 'haṅkāras tata eva śarīre 'pi | ātmaviṣayas tv ahaṅkāro naupacārikaḥ, idaṃpratyayāsambhinnāhaṃpratyayapratibhāsitvāt pramātā śarīrādivyatiriktaḥ |
 etad eva kathaṃ ? mamedaṃ śarīram iti pratyayopādānāt mamāyam ātmā iti pratyayābhāvāc ca | nanu mamāyam ātmā iti kiṃ na bhavati pratyayaḥ ? na bhavatīti bhrūmaḥ | kathaṃ tarhy evam ucyate ? kevalaṃ śabda uccāryate na tu pratyayasya sambhavaḥ | atrāpi mamapratyayapratibhāsasyādarśanāt śabdoccāraṇamātraṃ kena vāryate | kim idānīṃ sukhādiyogaḥ śarīrasyeṣyate ? naivam, sukhādiyogābhāvāt mithyāpratyayo 'yaṃ sukhy aham iti na tv etadalambanaḥ | ato vyavasthitam – jñātṛpratibhāsādarśanāt pratibhāse vā śarīrasya jñātṛtvenāvabhāsanān na dehādivyatiriktasyāhaṃpratyayaviṣayatā; śarīrasya ca jñātṛtvenāvabhāsamānasyāpi pramāṇasiddhā buddhiyoganiṣedhān mithyāpratyayālambanatā; na tu tasyācaitanye 'nyaḥ kaścid jñātā pratyakṣapramāṇaviṣayaḥ sidhyatītyādi} tad apy asaṅgatam; yato bhavatu jaiminīyānāṃ satsamprayoge puruṣasyendriyāṇāṃ buddhijanma pratyakṣam [MS 1.1.4] itilakṣaṇalakṣitendriyaprataykṣavādinām aham ity anabhāsapratyayasyānindriyajatvenātrāpratyakṣātvadoṣo nāsmākaṃ jinamattānusāriṇām, na hy asmākam indriyajam eva pratyakṣaṃ kiṃ tu yad yatra viśadaṃ jñānam indriyānindriyanimittaṃ tat tatra pratyakṣam ity abhyupagamāt, tad indriyānindriyanimittam [Tattvārtha 1.14] iti vācakamukhyavacanāt | tena yathā pratyakṣaviṣayatvena ghaṭādeḥ pratyakṣatā tathātmano 'pi dvasaṃvedanādhyakṣatāyāṃ ko virodhaḥ ? ata eva yad ucyate ghaṭāder bhinnajñānagrāhyatvena pratyakṣatā vyavasthāpyate; ātmanasvaparokṣatvena pratibhāsanāt pratyakṣatvam | tac ca kevalasya ghaṭādipratītyantargatasya vāparasādhanaṃ prāk pratipāditam ity atra aparasādhanam iti ko 'rthaḥ – kiṃ cidrūpasya sattā, ahosvit dvapratītau vyāpātaḥ ? iti pakṣadvayam utthāpya prathamapakṣe cidrūpasya sattaivātmaprakāśanaṃ yas ucyate tadā dṛṣṭānto vaktavyaḥ iti, tan nirastam; adhyakṣapratīte 'rtha dṛṣṭāntānveṣaṇasyāyuktatvāt |
 atha vivādagocare 'dhyakṣapratīte 'pi dṛṣṭāntānveṣaṇaṃ loke suprasiddham iti so  'trāpi vaktavyas tadāsyeva pradīpādilakṣaṇo dṛṣṭānto 'pi jñānasya prakāśaṃ prati sajātīyāparān apekṣaṇe sādhye | tathā hi – yathā pradīpādyāloko na svapratipattāv ālokāntaram apekṣate tathā jñānam api svapratipattau na samānajātīyajñāpekṣam | etāvan mātreṇālokasya dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ na punas tasyāpi jñānatvam āsādyate | yena indriyāgrāhyatvāc cakṣuṣmatām ivāndhānām api tatpratītiprasaṅgaḥ iti preryate | na hi dṛṣṭānte sādhyadharmidharmāḥ sarve 'pi āsañjayituṃ yuktāḥ, anyathā ghaṭe 'pi śabhadharmāḥ śabdatvādayaḥ prasajyerann iti tasyāpi śrotragrāhyatvaprasaṅgaḥ | na ca sādharmyadṛṣṭāntam antareṇa pramāṇapratītasyāpy arthasyāprasiddir iti śakyaṃ vaktum, anyathā jīvaccharīrasyāpi sātmakatve sādhye tadvat tatprasiddhadṛṣṭāntasyābhāvāt prāṇādimattvādes tatsiddhir va syāt |
 atha sādharmyadṛṣṭāntābhāve 'pi dṛṣṭavaidharmyadṛṣṭāntasya ghaṭādeḥ sadbhāvāt kevalavyatirekibalāt tatra tatsiddis tarhi yatra svaprakāśakatvaṃ nāsti tatrārthaprakāśakatvam api nāsti,
 S. 81
 yathā ghaṭādāv iti vyatirekadṛṣṭāntasadbhāvād arthaprakāśakatvalakṣaṇād dhetoḥ svaprakāśakatvaṃ vijñānasya kim iti na siddim āsādayati ? yat tūktam kasyacid arthasya kācit sāmagrī, tena prakāśaḥ prakāśāntaranirapekṣa eva svagrāhiṇi jñāne pratibhāti tad yuktam eva, yathā hi svasāmagrīta upajāyamānāḥ pradīpālokādayo na samānajātīyam alokāntaraṃ svagrāhiṇi jñāne pratibhāsamānā apekṣante tatrā svasāmagrīta upajāyamānaṃ vijñānaṃ svārthaprakāśasvabhāvaṃ svapratipattau na jñānāntaram apekṣate; pratiniyatatvāt svakāraṇāyattajanmanāṃ bhāvaśaktīnām | yat tu pradīpālokādikaṃ sajātīyālokāntaranirapekṣām api svapratipattau jñānam apekṣate tat tasyājñānarūpatvāt jñānasya ca tadviparyayasvabhāvatvād yuktyuktam iti naikatra sṛṣṭaḥ svabhāvo 'nyatrāsañjayituṃ yuktaḥ iti pūrvapakṣavaco niḥsāratayā vyavasthitam |
 athālokasya tadantaranirapekṣā pratipattir upalabdheti na taddṛṣṭāntabalād jñānasyāpi jñānāntaranirapekṣā pratipattiḥ, adṛṣṭatvāt svātmani kriyāvirodhāc ca; nanv evam upalabhyamāne 'pi vastuni yady adṛṣṭatvam virodhaś cocyeta tadā svātmavad ghaṭāder api bāhyasya na grāhakaṃ jñānam, adṛṣṭatvāt jaḍasya prakāśayogāc cety api vadataḥ saugatasya na vaktravakratā samupajāyate | {tathā hy asāv apy evaṃ vaktuṃ samarthaḥ – jaḍaṃ vastu na svataḥ prakāśate, vijñānavat, jaḍatvahāniprasaṅgāt | nāpi parataḥ prakāśamāṇam, nīlasukhādivyatiriktasya vijñānasyāsaṃvedanenāsattvāt |
 atha nīlasya prakāśaḥ iti prakāśamāṇanīlādivyatiriktas tatprakāśaḥ, anyathā bhedenāsyāṃ pratipattau saṃvedanasya tatpratibhāso na syāt; nanu na nīlatadvedanayoḥ pṛthagavabhāsaḥ pratyakṣasambhavī, prakāśaviviktasya nīlāder anubhavāt tadvivekena ca bodhasyāpratibhāsanāt | na cādhyakṣato vivekenāpratīyamānayor nīlatatsaṃvidor bhedo yuktaḥ, vivekādarśanasya bhedaviparyayāśrayatvāt; nīlatatsvarūpavat | athāpi kalpanā nīlatatsaṃvidor bhedam ullikhati – nīlasyānubhavaḥ iti; nanv abhedo 'pi bhedollekho sṛṣṭo yathā – śilāputrakasya vapuḥ nīlasya vā svarūpam iti | atha tatra pratyakṣārūḍo 'bhedo bādhaka iti na bhedollekhaḥ satyaḥ sa tarhi nīlasaṃvidor api pratyakṣārūḍho 'bhedo 'stīti na bhedakalpanā satyā | tad evaṃ nīlādikaṃ sukhādikaṃ ca svaprakāśavapuḥ pratibhātīti sthitam, tadvyatiriktasya prakāśasyāpratibhāsanenābhāvāt |
 bhavatu vā vyatirikto bodhas tathāpi na tadgrāhyā nīlādayo yuktāḥ | tathā hi – tulyakālo vā bodhas teṣāṃ prakāśakaḥ, bhinnakālo vā ? tulyakālp 'pi parokṣaḥ, svasaṃvidito vā ? na tāvat parokṣaḥ, yataḥ apratyakṣopalambhasya nārthādṛṣṭiḥ prasidhyati [   ]ityādinā svasaṃviditatvaṃ jñānasya prasādhyanta etatpakṣāṃ nirākariṣyāmāḥ – nāpi jñānāntaravedyaḥ, anavasthādidūṣaṇasyātra pakṣe pradarśayiṣyamāṇatvāt | svasaṃvedanapakṣe tu yathāntarnilīno bodhaḥ svasaṃviditaḥ pratibhāti tathā tatkāle svaprakāśavapuṣo nīlādayo bahirdeśasambandhitayā pratibhāti iti samānakālayor nīlatatsaṃvedanayoḥ svatantrayoḥ pratibhāsanāt savyetaragoviṣāṇayor iva na vedyavedakabhāvaḥ | samānakālasyāpi bodhasya nīlaṃ prati grāhakatve nīlasyāpi taṃ prati grāhakatāprasaṅgaḥ |
 samānakālapratibhāsāviśeṣe 'pi buddhir nīlādīnāṃ grahaṇam uparacayatīti grāhikā, nīlādayas tu grāhyāḥ, naitad api yuktam; yato nīlabodhavyatiriktā na grahaṇakriyā pratibhāti | tathā hi – bodhaḥ sukhāspadībhūto hṛdi, bahiḥ sphuṭam udbhāsamānatanuś ca nīlādir ābhāti na tv aparā grahaṇakriyā pratibhāsaviṣayaḥ | tadanavabhāse ca na tayā vyāpyamānatayā nīlādeḥ karmatā yuktā | bhavatu vā nīlabodhavyatiriktā kriyā tathāpi kiṃ tasyā api svataḥ pratītiḥ, yad vānyataḥ ? tatra yadi svato grahaṇakriyā pratibhāti tathā sati bodhaḥ nīlam grahaṇakriyā ceti trayaṃ svarūpanimagnam ekakālaṃ pratibhātīti na kartṛkarmakriyāvyavahṛtiḥ | athānyato grahaṇakriyā pratibhāti; nanu tatrāpy aparāgrahaṇakriyā upeyā – anyathā tasyā grāhyatāsiddheḥ – punas tatrāpy aparā karmatānibandhanaṃ kriyā upeyety anavasthā tan na grahaṇakriyāparāsti, tatsvarūpānavabhāsanāt | tataś cāntaḥsaṃvedanam bahirnīlādikaṃ ca svaprakāśam eveti |
 S. 82
  svasaṃvittamātravādaḥ sādhīyān yadi tarhy antarnilīno bodho nīlāder na bodhakaḥ kin tu svaprakāśa evāsau; tathā sati nīlam ahaṃ vedmi iti kamakartṛbhāvābhiniveśī pratyayo na bhavet, viṣayasya karmakartṛbhāvasyābhāvāt | nanu viṣayam antareṇāpi pratayo dṛṣṭa eva, yathā śuktikāyāṃ rajatāvagamaḥ | atha bādhakodayāt punar bhrāntir asau, nīlādau tu karmatāder na bādhāstīti satyatā | nanv atrāpi bodhanīlādeḥ svarūpasaṃsaktasya dvayasya svātantryopalambho 'sti bādhakaḥ karmakartṛbhāvollekhasya | atha kim asyā bhrānter nibandhanam ? na hi bhrāntir api virbījā bhavati; nanu pūrvabhrāntir evottarakarmakartṛbhāvāvagater nibandhanam; pūrvabhrāntikarmatāder api aparā pūrvabhrāntir ity anādibhrāntiparamparā, karmatādir na tattvam |
 atha vā nīlam iti pratītis tāvan mātrādhyavasāyinī pṛthak, aham ity api matir antarullekham udvahantī bhinnā, vedmi ity api pratītir aparaiva; tataś ca prasparāsaṃsaktapratītitritayaṃ kramavat pratibhāti na karmakartṛbhāvaḥ, tulyakālayos tasyāyogāt bhinnakālayor apy anavabhāsanān na karmatādigatiḥ kathañcit sambhavinī |
 athāpi darśanāt prāk sann api nīlātmā na bhāti tadudaye ca bhātīti karmatā tasya, naitad api sādhīyaḥ; yataḥ prāgbhāvo 'rthasya na siddhaḥ | darśanena svakālāavadher arthasya grahaṇād darśanakāle hi nīlam ābhāti na tu tataḥ prāk, tat kathaṃ pūrvabhāvo 'rthasya sidhyeta tasya darśanasya pūrvakāle virahāt ? na ca tatkāle darśanaṃ prāg arthasannidhiṃ vyanakti, sarvadā tatpratibhāsaprasaṅgāt | athānyena darśanena prāgarthaḥ pratīyate, nanu taddarśanād api prāk sadbhāvo 'rthasyānyenāvaseya ity anavasthā | tasmāt sarvasya nīlāder darśanakāle pratibhāsanān na tatpūrvaṃ sattā sidhyati |
 athāpi pūrvadṛṣṭaṃ paśyāmi iti vyavasāyāt prāgarthaḥ sidhyati, pragarthasattāṃ vinā dṛśyamānasysa pūrvadṛṣṭena ekatvagater ayogāt | kena punar ekatvaṃ tayor gamyate – kim idānīntanadarśanena, pūrvadarśanena vā ? na tāvat pūrvadarśanena, tatra tatkālāvadher evārthasya pratibhāsanāt | na hi tena svapratibhāsino 'rthasya vartamānakāladarśanavyāptir avasīyate, tatkāle sāmpratikadarśanāder abhāvāt | na cāsat pratibhāti, darśanasya vitathatvaprasaṅgāt | nāpīdānīntanadarśanena pūrvadarśanādivyāptinīlāder avasīyate, taddarśanakāle pūrvadṛkkālasyāstamayāt | na cāstamitapūrvadarśānādisaṃsparśam avatarati pratyakṣam, vitathatvaprasaṅgād eva | tasmād apāstatatpūrvadṛgādiyogaṃ sarvaṃ vastu dṛśā gṛhyate, pūrvadṛṣṭatāṃ tu smṛtir ullikhati tad apāstam, dṛṣṭollekhābhāvāt | na va sa evāyam iti pratītir ekā, saḥ iti smṛtirūpam, ayam iti tu dṛśaḥ svārūpam; tatparokṣāparokṣākāratvān naikasvabhāvau pratyayau, tat kutas tattvasiddiḥ ?
 athānumānāt prāgbhāvo 'rthasya sidhyati prāk sattāṃ vinā paścāddarśanāyogād iti, tad apy asat; yataḥ paścādddarśanasya prāksattāyāḥ sambandho na siddhaḥ, prāksattāyāḥ kathañcid apy asiddheḥ | na cāsiddhayā sattayā vyāptaṃ paścāddarśanaṃ sidhyati yena tatas tatsiddhiḥ | ata yadi pragartham antareṇa darśanam udayam āsādayati tathā sati niyāmakābhāvāt sarvatra sarvadā sarvākāraṃ tad bhavet, nāyam api doṣaḥ, niyatavāsanāprabodhena saṃvedananiyamāt | tathā hi – svapnāvasthāyāṃ vāsanābalāc darśanasya deśakālākāraniyamo dṛṣṭa iti jāgraddṛśāyām api tat evāsau yuktaḥ | arthasya tu na sattā siddhā nāpi tadbhedāt saṃvittiniyama iti tan na tataḥ saṃvidvaicitryam, tasmān na kathañcid api nīlādeḥ prāk sattāsiddhiḥ |
 atha pūrvasattāvirahe kiṃ pramāṇam ? nanv anupalabdhir eva pramāṇam – yadi nīlaṃ pūrvakālasambandhisvarūpaṃ syāt tenaiva rūpeṇopalabhyeta, na ca tathā darśanakālabhavaḥ sarvadā pratibhāsanāt | yac ca yenaiva rūpeṇa pratibhāti tat tenaiva rūpeṇāsti, yathā nīlaṃ nīlarūpatayāvabhāsamānaṃ tathaiva sat na pītādirūpatayā, sarvaṃ copalabhyamānaṃ rūpaṃ vartamānakālatayaiva pratibhāti na pūrvāditayā, tan na pūrvaṃ sattārthasya |
 S. 83
 atha nīlaṃ taddarśanaviratāv api paradṛśi pratibhātīti sādhāranatayā grāhyam | vijñānaṇ tv asādhāraṇatayā prakāśakam, naitad api yuktam; yato nīkasya na sādhāraṇatayā siddhaḥ pratibhāsaḥ, pratyakṣeṇa svapratibhāsitāyā evāvagateḥ | na hi nīlaṃ paradṛśi pratibhātīty atra pramāṇam asti, paradṛśo 'nadhigame nīlādes tadvedyatānadhigateḥ |
 athānumānena nīlādīnāṃ sādhāraṇatā pratīyate – yathaiva hi svasantāne nīladarśanāt tadādānārthā pravṛttis tathāparasantāne 'pi pravṛttidarśanāt tadviṣayaṃ darśanam anumīyate, naitad apy asti; anumānena svaparadarśanabhṛto nīlāder ekatāsiddheḥ; tad dhi sadṛśavyavahāradarśanād upajāyamānaṃ svadṛṣṭasadṛśatāṃ paradṛṣṭasya pratipādayet, yathāparadhūmadarśanāt pūrvasadṛśaṃ dahanam adhigantum īśo na tu tam eva pūrvasṛṣṭam, sāmānyenānvayaparicchedāt | tan nānumānato 'pi grāhyākārasyaikatā |
 nanu bhedo 'py asya na siddha eva | pratibhāsabhede sati katham asiddhaḥ parapratibhāsaparihāreṇa svapratibhāsān svapratibhāsaparihāreṇa ca parapratibhāsān vivekasvabhāvān vyatirecayati anyathā tasyāyogāt ? tataḥ svaparadṛṣṭasya nīlādeḥ pratibhāsabhedād vyavahāre tulye 'pi bheda eva; itarathā romāñcanikarasadṛśakāryadarśanāt sukhāder api svaparasantānabhuvas tattvaṃ bhavet |
 athāpi santānabhedāt sukhāder bhedaḥ; nanu santānabhedo 'pi kim anyabhedāt ? tathā ced anavasthā | atha tasya svarūpabhedād bhedaḥ sukhāder api tarhi sa evāstu, anyathā bhedāsiddheḥ | na hy anyabhedād anyad bhinnam, atiprasaṅgāt | nīlāder api svaparapratibhāsinaḥ pratibhāsabhedo 'stīti naikatā |
 atha deśaikatvād ekatvam, nanu deśasyāpi svaparadṛṣṭasyānantaroktanyāyād naikatā yuktā | tasmād grāhakākāravat pratipuruṣam udbhāsamānaṃ nīlādikam api bhinnam eva | tac caikakālopalambhād grāhakavat svaprakāśam |
 atha grāhakākāraś cidrūpatvād vedako nīlākāras tu jaḍatvād grāhyaḥ, atrocyate – kim idaṃ bodhasya cidrūpatvam ? yady aparokṣaṃ svarūpadṃ nīlāder api tarhi tad astīti na jaḍatā | atha nīlāder aparikṣasvarūpam anyasmād bhavatīti grāhyam, nanu bodhasyāpi sarvarūpam indriyāder bhavatīti grāhyaṃ syāt | atha yad indriyādikāryaṃ na tad vedyam, nīlādikam api tarhi nayanādikāryam astu na tu grāhyam |
 athāpi bodho bodhasvarūpatayā nityo nīlādikas tu prakāśyarūpatayānitya iti grāhyaḥ, tad apy asat; stambhāder nayanādibalād udeti rūpam aparokṣatvam, tad anityaḥ stambhādir bhavatu, grāhyas tu katham ? na hi yad yasmād utpadyate tat tasya vedyam, atiprasaṅgāt | tasmād aparokṣasvarūpāḥ stambhādayaḥ svaprakāśāḥ bodhas tu nityo 'nityo vā tatkāle kevalam udbhāti na tu vedakaḥ, dvayor api parasparaṃ grāhyagrāhakatāpatteḥ |
 atha nīlonmukhatvād bodho grāhakaḥ, kim idaṃ tadunmukhavtaṃ nāma bodhasya ? yadi nīlakāle sattā sā nīlasyāpi tatkāle samastīti nīlam api bodhasya vedakaṃ syāt | athānyad unmukhatvam tat tarhi svarūpanimagnaṃ cakāsat tṛtīyaṃ svarūpaṃ bhavet | tathā hi – tasya tadunmukhatvaṃ tadvyāpāraḥ, sa ca vyāpāro yadi nīle vyāpriyate tadā tatrāpy aparo vyāpāra ity anavasthā | atha na vyāpriyate na tadbalād bodhasya grāhakatvaṃ nīlādes tu grāhyatvam | atha vyāpārasyāparavyāpāravyartirekeṇāpi nīlaṃ prati vyāpṛtirūpatā, tasya tadrūpatvāt; nanu nīlasyāpi svaṃ svarūpaṃ vidyata iti bodhaṃ prati grahaṇavyāpṛtiḥ syāt |
 kiñ ca, bodhena yadi nīlaṃ prati grahaṇakriyā janyate sā nīlād bhinnā, abhinnā vā ? bhinnā cet, na tayā tasya grāhyatvam, bhinnatvād eva | athābhinnā tarhi nīlāder jñānarūpatā, jñānajanyatvād uttarajñānakṣaṇavat | atha jñānasyaivaṃbhūtā śaktir yena tasya nīlaṃ prati grāhakatā nīlādes tu taṃ prati grāhyatā | nanu bodhasya grāhakatve nīlādes tu grāhyatve siddhe śaktiparikalpanā yuktā, śakteḥ kāryānumeyatvāt; tadasiddhau tu tatparikalpanam ayuktam, itaretarāśrayaprasaṅgāt |
 S. 84
 tathā hi – bodhasya śaktiviśeṣasiddher nīlaṃ prati grāhakatvasiddhiḥ, tatsiddheś ca tacchaktisiddhir iti vaktam itaretarāśrayatvam | tan na bodhasya nīlaṃ prati grāhakatvasiddhiḥ, tasmād vyatirikte 'pi bodhe 'bhyupagate sahopalambhaniyamāt svasaṃvedanam eva yuktam | paramārthatas tu sukhādayo nīlādayaś cāparokṣā ity etāvad eva bhāti, nirākāras tu bodhaḥ svapne 'pi nopalabhyata iti na tasya sadbhāva iti kathaṃ tasyārthagrāhakatvām | ata eva te pramāṇayanti – iha khalu yadi pratibhāti tad eva sadvyavahṛtipatham avatarati, yathā hṛdi prakāśamānavapuḥ sukham, na tatkāle pīḍānudbhāsamānā samasti, vijñaptir eva ca nīlādirūpatayā sakalatanubhṛtām ābhātīti svabhāvahetuḥ | tad evam arthagrāhakatvasyāpy asiddheḥ jaḍasya prakāśaviruddhatvāc ca nārthagrāhakatvam api bauddhadṛṣṭyā yuktam} |
 atha bahirdeśasambaddhasya jaḍasyāpi nīlāder anubhavān na nīlādiprakāśasya tadgrāhakatvam asiddham, nāpy anubhūyamāne stambhādike jaḍe prakāśaviṣayatvavirodhodbhāvanaṃ yuktisaṅgatam; pratyakṣasiddhasvabhāve vastuni tadviruddhasvabhāvāvedakasyānumānasya pratyakṣabādhitakarmanirdeśānantaraprayuktakālātyayāpadiṣṭatvadoṣaduṣṭahetuprabhavatvenānumānābhāsatvāt | na ca pratyakṣāsiddhe svabhāve virodhaḥ sidhyati; anyathā jñānasyāpi jñānatvavirodhaprāptiḥ | nanv evaṃ nīlādisaṃvedanasyāpi hṛdi svasaṃvedanaviṣayatayānubhavān na svasaṃviditatvam asiddham, nāpi svātmani kriyāvirodhodbhāvanaṃ yuktiyukam; anubhūyamāne virodhāsiddheḥ | asvasaṃvedanajñānasādhakatvenopanyasya mānasya ca hetoḥ pratyakṣānirākṛtapakṣaviṣayatvena na sādhyasādhakatvam ity api samānam |
 kiṃ ca, svasaṃviditajñānānabhyupagame pratīyate 'yam artho bahirdeśasambandhitayā ity atra pratīter vyavasthāpikāyā apratītatvāvyavasthitau vyavasthāpy asyārthasya na vyavasthitiḥ syāt; na hi svayamavyavasthitaṃ kharaviṣāṇādi kasyacid vyavasthāpakam upalabdham | atha pratīter asaṃviditatve 'pi ekārthasamavetānantapratītivyavasthāpitatvena nāvyavasthitatvaṃ tarhi tadekārthasamavetānantarapratīter api aparatathābhūtapratītyavyavasthāpitatve nārthavyavasthāpanapratītivyavasthāpakatvam iti punar api tathābhūtāparā pratītiḥ pratītivyavasthāpikābhyupagantavyety anavasthā | atha pratītivyavasthāpikā pratītiḥ svasaṃviditatvena svayam eva vyavasthiteti nāyam doṣas tarhy arthavyavashāpikāpi pratītis tathā kiṃ nābhyupagamyate nyāyasya samānatvāt ? atha pratītir apratītāpi pratītyantarvyavasthāpikā tarhi prathamapratītir apy anavasthitāpy arthavyavasthāpikā bhaviṣyastīti nāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇā viśeṣye buddhiḥ [   ] iti vacaḥ kathaṃ na pariplavet ?  pratīto'rthaḥ iti viśeṣyapratipattau pratītiviśeṣaṇānavagame 'pi viśeṣyapratipattyabhyupagamāt |
 api ca, yadi tadekārthasamavetajñānāntaragrāhyaṃ jñānam arthagrāhakam abhyupagamyate tadā pūrvapūrvajñānopalambhanasvabhāvānām uttarottarajñānānām anavaratam utpatter viśayāntarasañcāro jñānānāṃ na syāt, viṣayāntarasannidhāne 'pi pūrvajñānalakṣaṇasya tadekārthasamavetasyāntaraṅgatvenātisannihitatarasya viṣayasya sadbhāvāt | yas tv āha – viṣayopalambhanimittamātrapratipattau pratītiviśeṣaṇasyārthasya siddhatvād nānavasthā tad etad eva na saṅgacchate, svasaṃvedanajñānānabhyupagamāt; etac ca pratipāditam |
 api ca, pramāṇasamplavavādinā naiyāyikena pratyakṣaśābdajñānayor ekaviṣayatvam abhyupagatam, tathā cādhyakṣajñānavat śābde 'pi tasyaivānyūnānatiriktasya viṣasyasyādhigame na pratipattibheda iti adhyakṣavac chābdam api spaṣṭapratibhāsaṃ syāt | athaikaviṣayatve saty api indriyasambandhābhāvāc chabdaviṣaye pratipattibhedaḥ; nanv akṣair api viṣayasvarūpam udbhāsanīyam, tac ca yadi śābdenāpi pradarśyate tathā sati indriyasambandhābhāve 'pi kim iti na spaṣṭāvabhāsaḥ śābdasya ? na hi viṣayabhedam antareṇa jñānānabhāsabhedo yuktaḥ; anyathā jñānāvabhāsabhedād viṣayabhedavyavasthā na syāt | na hi bahir api tadavabhāsabhedasaṃvedanavyatirekeṇānyad bhedavyavasthānibandhanam utpaśyāmaḥ | anyac ca pratyakṣe 'pi sākṣād indriyasambandho 'stīti na svarūpeṇa jñātuṃ śakyaḥ – tasyātīndriyatvāt – kin tu svarūpapratibhāsāt kāryāt; tac cāvikalaṃ yadi śābde 'pi vastusvarūpaṃ pratibhāti tadā tata evendriyasambandhas tatrāpi kiṃ nābhyupagamyate ?
 S. 85
 atha tatra spaṣṭapratibhāsābhāvan nāsāv anumīyate; nanu tadabhāvas tadakṣasaṅgativirahāt, tadabhāvaś ca sphuṭapratibhāsābhāvād iti so 'yam itaretarāśrayadoṣaḥ | tasmād viṣayabhedanibandhana eva jñānapratibhāsabhedāvasāyo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ; sa caikaviṣayatve śābdādhyakṣajñānayor na saṅgacchate |
 atha śābde vasturūpāvabhāse 'pi na sakalatadgataviśeṣāvabhāsa ity aspaṣṭapratibhāsaṃ tat; nanv evaṃ pratyakṣāvabhāsino viśeṣasyārthakriyākṣam asya tatrāpratibhāsanāt tad eva bhinnaviṣayatvaṃ śābdādhyakṣayoh jñānātmabhūtau; nanv evam akṣasambaddhe viṣaye pratibhāsamāne tatkālaḥ spaṣṭṛtvāvabhāso jñānāvabhāsa iti prāptam; viśiṣṭasāmagrījanyasya jñānasya viśadatvāt, tadavabhāsavyatirekeṇa tu akṣasambaddhanīlapratibhāsakāle 'nyasya bhavadabhyupagamena vaiśadyapratibhāsanimittasyāsambhavāt |
 atha ca bhavatu viśadajñānapratibhāsanimitta eva tatra vaiśasyapratibhāsavyavahāras tathāpi na svasaṃviditatajjñānasiddhiḥ, tadekārthasamavetajñānāntaravedyatve 'pi tadvyavahārasya sambhavād; ekakālāvabhāsavyavahāras tu laghuvṛttitvān manasaḥ kramānupalakṣaṇanimittaḥ utpalapatraśatavyatibhedavat | nanv evaṃ saty aṅgulipañcakasyaikajñānāvabhāso 'pi kramāvabhāse saty api tata eva kramapratibhāsānupalakṣaṇakṛta iti sadasaddharmaḥ sarvaḥ kasyacid ekajñānapratyakṣaḥ, prameyatvāt, pañcāṅgulivat iti sarvajñasādhakaprayoge dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatāprasaktiḥ | tathā, samastasadasaddharmagrāhakeṇa sarvavijñānena jñānātmā gṛhyate, uta neti ? yadi na gṛhyate tadā tasya prameyatve sati tenaiva prameyatvalakṣaṇo hetur vyabhicārī, aprameyatve tasya bhāgāsiddho hetuḥ | atha sarvajñajñānena sarvapadārthagrāhiṇā ātmāpi gṛhyata iti nānaikāntikaḥ; nanv evaṃ sati yatheśvarajñānaṃ jñānatve 'py ātmānaṃ svayaṃ gṛhṇāti na ca tatra svātmani kriyāvirodhaḥ tathāsadādijñānam apy evaṃ bhaviṣyatīti na kaścid virodhaḥ kiṃ ca, evam abhyupagame jñānmaṃ jñānāntaragrāhyam, prameyatvāt, ghaṭavat ity atra prayoge īśvarajñānasya prameyatve saty api jānāntaragrāhyatvābhāvāt tenaivānaikāntikaḥ prameyatvāt iti hetuḥ | tasmāt jñānasya jñānāntaragṛāhyatve 'nekadoṣasambhavāt svasaṃviditaṃ jñānam abhyupagantavyam |
 jñānasvarūpaś cātmā, anyathā bhinnajñānasadbhāvād ākāśasyeva tasya jñātṛtvaṃ na syāt | na cākāśavyatirekeṇa jñānam ātmany eva samavetam iti tasyaiva jñātṛtvaṃ nākāśāder iti vaktuṃ yuktam, samavāyasya niṣetsyamānatvāt | jñānasya ca svasaṃviditatve siddhe ātmano 'pi tadavyatiriktasya tat siddham iti kathaṃ na svasaṃvedanapratyakṣāsiddhatvam ātmanaḥ ? tan na prathamapakṣasya duṣṭatvam |
 dvitīyapakṣe 'pi yad uktam na hi kaścit padārthaḥ kartṛrūpaḥ karaṇarūpo vā svātmani karmaṇīva savyāpāro dṛṣṭaḥ iti, tad apy asaṅgatam; bhinnavyāpāravyatirekeṇāpi ātmanaḥ kartuḥ pramāṇasya ca jñānasya svasaṃviditatvapratipādanāt | ekasyaiva ca liṅgādikaraṇam apekṣyāvasthābhedena yathā pramātṛtvaṃ prameyatvaṃ ca bhavadbhir aviruddhatvenābhyupagamyate tathaikadāpy ekasyātmanaḥ anekadharmasadbhāvāt pramātṛpramāṇatvaprameyatvāny aviruddhāni kiṃ nābhyupagamyante tattaddharmayogāt tattatsvabhāvatvasya pramānaniścitatvenāvirodhāt | yac coktam pramāṇāviṣayatve 'py aparokṣatety asya bhāṣitasya ko 'rthaḥ ityādi, tad apy asāraṃ; jñātṛtayā pramāṇatvena ca svarūpāvabhāsanasya pratipāditatvāt | na ca ghādeḥ svarūpasya bhinnajñānagrāhyatvāt pramātuḥ pramāṇasya ca svarūpaṃ abhinnajñānagrāhyam, tayoś cidrūpatvena ghaṭādes tu tadviparyayeṇa svarūpasysa siddhatvāt | na ca pramāṇapramāṭṛsvarūpagrāhakasya pratyakṣasya tallakṣaṇenāsaṅgrahaḥ; tatsaṅgrāhakasya lakṣaṇasya pradarśitatvāt | yad api ghaṭam ahaṃ cakṣuṣā paśyāmi ity anenātiprasaṅgāpādanaṃ kṛtam, tad apy asaṅgatam; na hi cakṣuṣo jaḍarūpasyāsvasaṃviditatve pramātṛpramityor api cidrūpayor asvasaṃviditatvaṃ yuktam; anyasvabhāvatvānupapatteḥ | yat tūktam indriyavyāpāre sati śarīrād vyavacchinnasya viṣayasyaiva kevalasyāvabhāsanam iti, tad atyantam asaṅgatam; viṣayasyeva tadavabhāsasaṃvedanasyāpi vyavasthāpitatvāt tadabhāve viṣayāvabhāsa eva na syād ity asya ca |
 S. 86
 ataḥ pramātravabhāsa upapanna eva |
 na ca kṛśo 'haṃ sthūlo 'ham iti śarīrasāmānādhikaraṇyenāsya pratyayasyopapattes tadālambanatā, cakṣurādikaraṇavyāpārābhāve śarīrasyāgrahaṇe 'pi aham iti pratyayasya sukhādisamānādhikaraṇatvena parisphuṭapratibhāsaviṣayatvenotpattidarśanād na śarīrālambanatvam asya vyavasthāpayituṃ yuktam | na ca kṛśo 'ham iti prayayasya bhrāntatve jñānavān aham iti jñānasāmānādhikaraṇyenopajāyamānasyāpi pratyayasya bhrāntatvaṃ yuktam; anyathā agnir māṇavakaḥ iti māṇavake 'gnipratyayasyopacaritaviṣayasya bhrāntatve 'gnāv api tatpratyayasyopacaritatvena bhrāntatvaṃ syāt | ata tatra pāṭavapiṅgalatvādilakṣaṇasyopacāranimittasya sadbhāvād bhavati tatropacaritaḥ pratyayaḥ na cātropacāranibandhanaṃ kiñcid asti, tad apy asaṅgatam; saṃsāryātmanaḥ śarīrādivyatirikte 'py atyantopakārake svabhṛtyādāv upacaritas tannimittaḥ yo 'yaṃ bhṛtyaḥ so 'ham iti pratyayaḥ |
 na ca sukhādisāmānadhikaraṇyenopajāyamānasyaivāhaṃpratyayasyopacaritaviṣayateti vaktuṃ śakyam, agnāv agnipratyayavad abādhitatvenāskhaladrūpatvena cāsyātra mukhyatvāt; gauratvādes tu pudgaladharmatvena bāhyendriyagrāhyatayā antarmukhākārānindriyāhaṃpratyayaviṣayatvāsambhavāt | na ca gauratvādirūpāśrayabhūtasya pratikṣaṇaviśarārutvenābhyupagamaviṣayasya śarīrasya ya evāhaṃ prāg mitraṃ dṛṣṭavān sa evāhaṃ varṣapañcakādivyavadhānena spṛśāmi iti sthirālambanatvenānubhūyamānpratyayaviṣayatvaṃ yuktam; anyathā rūpaviṣayatvenānubhūyamānasya tasya rasādyālambanatvaṃ syāt | na ca sukhādivivarttātmakātmālambanatve kiñcid bādhakam utpaśyāmaḥ yena tadviṣayatvenāsya bhrāntatvaṃ syāt | nāpi tatra tasya skhaladrūpatā yena vāhīke gopratyayasyevopacaritatvakalpanā yuktimatī syāt | tasmād abādhitāskhaladrūpāhaṃpratyayagrāhyatvād ātmano nāsiddhiḥ | śeṣas tu pūrvapakṣagrantho niḥsāratayā na pratisamādhānam arhatīty upekṣitaḥ ||
 na cātra bauddhamatānusāriṇaitad vaktuṃ yujyate – ahaṃpratyayasya savikalpakatvenāpratyakṣatvena na tadgrāhyatvam ātmana iti, savikalpakasyaivapratyakṣasya pramāṇatvena vyavasthāpayiṣyamāṇatvāt; pratyakṣaviṣayatve 'pi vipratipattisambhave 'numānasyāvatāraḥ | na ca siddhe ātmana ekatve tatpratibaddho 'nusandhānapratyayaḥ sidhyati, tatsiddhau ca tatas tasyaikatvam itītaretarāśrayadoṣāvatāraḥ; ya evāhaṃ ghaṭam adrākṣaṃ sa evedānīṃ taṃ spṛśāmi iti pratyayāt pratyabhijñāpratakṣasvarūpād ātmana ekatvasiddheḥ |
 na cātraitat preryam – draṣṭṭarūpamātmanaḥ spraṣṭṭarūpānupraveśena pratibhāsate, āhosvid ananupraveśena ? yady anupraveśena tadā draṣṭṭarūpasya spraṣṭṭārūpe 'nupraveśāt spraṣṭṭarūpataiveti na draṣṭṭarūpatā; tathā ca ahaṃ draṣṭā spṛśāmi iti kuta ekaṃ pratyabhijñānaṃ yatas tad ekatvasiddhiḥ ? athānanupraveśena tadā darśanasparśanāvabhāsayor bhedāt kuta ekaṃ pratyabhijñānam ? na hi pratibhāsabhede saty apy ekatvam,  anyathā ghaṭapaṭapratibhāsayor api tat syāt | atha pratibhāsasyaivātra bhedo na punas tadviṣayasyātmanaḥ, kutaḥ punas tasyābhedaḥ ? na tāvat pratibhāsābhedāt, tasya bhinnatve vyavasthāpitatvāt | nāpi svataḥ, svato 'dyāpi vivādaviṣayatvāt  atha darśanasparśanāvasthābhede 'pi cidrūpasya tadavasthātur abhinnatvān nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, tad apy asaṅgatam; yato darśanāvasthāpratibhāsena tatsambaddham evāvasthātṛrūpaṃ gṛhītaṃ na sparśanajñānasambandhi, tatra tadavasthāyā anutpannatvenāpratibhāsanāt; tadapratibhāsane ca tadvyāptitvenāvasthātur apy apratibhāsanāt | nāpi sparśanapratibhāsena darśanāvasthāvyāptir avasthātur avagamyate, sparśanajñāne darśanasya vinaṣṭatvenāpratibhāsavāt; pratibhāsane cānādyavasthāparamparāpratibhāsaprasaṅgaḥ | na ca prāgavasthāpratibhāsane tadavasthāvyāptir avasthātur avagantuṃ śakyā | yac ca yena rūpeṇa pratibhāti tat tenaiva sad ity abhyupagantavyam, yathā nīlaṃ nīlarūpatayā pratibhāsamānaṃ tenaica rūpeṇābhyupagamyate | darśanasparśanajñānābhyāṃ ca svasambandhitvam evāvasthātur gṛhyate iti tadrūpa evāsāv abhyupagantavya iti kuto 'vasthātṛsiddhiḥ ?
 yato nīlapratibhāse 'py evaṃ vaktuṃ śakyam – kim ekanīlajñānaparamāṇvavabhāso 'paratannīlajñānaparamaṇvavabhāsānupraveśena pratibhāti, utānanupraveśena ?
 87
 yady anupraveśena tadaikatannīlajñānaparamāṇvavabhāsānām anupraveśān nīlajñānasaṃvedanasyaikaparamāṇurūpatvam; tasya cānanubhavāt kuto nīlajñānasaṃvedanasiddhiḥ ? athānanupraveśena tadā nīlajñānaparamāṇvavabhāsānām ayaḥśalākākalpānāṃ pratibhāsanāt kutaḥ sthūlam ekanīlajñānasaṃvedanaṃ pratinīlajñānaparamāṇvavabhāsaṃ bhinnatvāt ? atha svasaṃvedanāvabhāsabhede saty api na tatpratibhāsasya nīlajñānasya bhedaḥ; nanu kuto nīlajñānasyābhedaḥ kiṃ tatsvasaṃvedanābhedāt, svato vā ? yadi svasaṃvedanābhedāt, tad ayuktam; tadbhedasya vyavasthāpitatvāt | atha svata eva tadabhedaḥ, tad apy ayuktam; tasyādyāpy asiddhatvāt |
 tathā, yadi darśanāvasthāyāṃ sparśanāvasthā na pratibhātīti tadavasthāvyāptir darśanajñānenāvasthātur na grahītuṃ śakyā; nanv evaṃ tadapratibhāsane tena tadavyāptir api kathaṃ graḥituṃ śakyā ? tadapratibhāsane tata idam avasthātṛrūpaṃ vyāvṛttam ity etad api prahītum aśakyam eva | na ca tadviviktapratibhāsād eva tadavyāptir gṛhītaiveti vaktuṃ yuktam, tadapratibhāsane tadviviktasyaivāgrahaṇāt | na ca tadavyāptis  tasya svarūpam eva iti darśanajñānena tatsvarūpagrāhiṇā tadabhinnasvarūpā tadavyāptir api gṛhītaiveti yuktam, tadvyāptāv apy asya sarvasya samānatvāt | na cābādhitaikapratyayaviṣayasyātmana ekatvam asiddham | na cāsyaikatvādhyavasāyasya kiñcid bādhakam asti, tadbādhakatvena sambhāvyamānasya pramāṇasya yathāsthānaṃ niṣetsyamānatvāt |
 bhavatu vānusandhānapratyayalakṣaṇād dhetos tadekatvasiddhis tathāpi netatretarāśrayadoṣaḥ, yato naikatvapratibaddham anusandhānam anvayidṛṣṭāntadvāreṇa niścīyate – yenāyaṃ doṣaḥ syāt – api tv anekatve 'nusandhānasyāsambhavāt tato vyāvṛttam anusandhānaṃ tadekatvena vyāpyata ity ekasantāne smaraṇādyanusandhānadarśanād anumānato 'pi tatsiddhiḥ | na ca bhede darśanasmaraṇādijñānānām anusandhānaṃ sambhavati; anyathā devadattānubhūte 'rthe yajñadattasya smaraṇādyanusandhānaṃ syāt |
 atha devadattayajñadattayor ekasantānābhāvaṃ nānusandhānam, yatra tv ekaḥ santānas tatra pūrvāparajñānayor atyantabhede 'pi bhavaty evānusandhānam; nanu santānasya yadi santānibhyo bhedaḥ ekatvaṃ ca tadā śabdāntareṇa sa evātmābhihito yatpratibaddham anusandhānam | atha santānibhyo 'bhinnaḥ santānas tadā pūrvottarajñānakṣaṇānāṃ santāniśabdavācyānāṃ devadattayajñadattajñānavad atyantabhedāt tadabhinnasya santānasyāpi bheda iti kuto 'nusandhānanimittatvam ? athaikasantatipatitānāṃ pūrvottarajñānasantānināṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvād bhede 'py ekasantānatvam tannibandhanaś cānusandhānapratyayo yuktaḥ, na punar devadattayajñadattajñānayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāva atas tannibandhanasantānābhāvanimittas tatrānusandhānābhāvaḥ; nanu devadattajñānaṃ yajñadattena yadā vyāpāravyāhārādiliṅgabalād anumīyate tadā tad yajñadattānumānajanakaṃ bhavatīti kāryakāraṇabhāvanimittaikasantānanibandhanānusandhānaprasaktiḥ syāt |
 atha svasantatāv upādānopādeyabhāvena jñānānāṃ janyajanakabhāvaḥ bhinnasantatau tu sahakāribhāvena tadbhāva iti nāyaṃ doṣaḥ; nanu kiṃ punar idam upādānatvaṃ yadabhāvād bhinnasantāne 'nusandhānābhāvaḥ ? yat svasantatinivṛttau kāryaṃ janayati tad upādānakāraṇam, yathā mṛtpiṇḍaḥ svayaṃ nivartamāno ghaṭam utpādayatīti sa ghaṭotpattāv upādānakāraṇaṃ; atha vāparam – anekasmād utpadyamāne kārye svagataviśeṣādhāyakaṃ tat, na tv evaṃ nimittakāraṇam | nanu pratikṣaṇaviśarāruṣv ekasvabhāvapaurvāparyāvasthitajñānasyabhāveṣu kṣaṇeṣūpādānopādeyabhāva eva na vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyaḥ | tathā hi – uttarajñānaṃ janayat pūrvajñāmaṃ kiṃ naṣṭaṃ janayati, utānaṣṭam, ubhayarūpam, anubhayarūpam vā ? na tāvan naṣṭam, cirataraanaṣṭasyevānantaranaṣṭasyāpy avidyamānatvenotpādakatvavirodhāt | nāpy anaṣṭam, kṣaṇabhaṅgabhaṅgaprasaṅgāt | nāpy ubhayarūpam, ekasvabhāvasya vuruddhobhayarūpāsambhavāt | nāpy anubhayarūpam, anyonyavyavacchedarūpāṇām ekaniṣedhasya tadaparavidhānanāntarīyakatvenānubhayarūpatāyā ayogāt |
 atha yadi vyāpārayogāt kāraṇaṃ kāryotpādakam abhyupagamyeta tadā syād ayaṃ doṣaḥ – yad uta naṣṭasya vyāpārāsambhavāt kathaṃ kāryotpādakatvam, yadā tu prāgbhāvamātram eva kāraṇasya kāryotpādakatvaṃ tadā kuta etaddoṣāvasaraḥ ? nanv etasminn abhyupagame prāgbhāvino 'nekasmād upajāyamāne kārye kuto 'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ -
 88
 idam atropādānakāraṇam idaṃ ca sahakārikāraṇam iti, dvayor api kāreṇānuvihitānvayavyatirekatvāt ? atha saty apy anvayavyatirekānuvidhāne ekasyopādānatvena janakatvam aparasyānyatheti; nanv etad evopādānabhāvena janakatvaṃ kasyacid rūpasyānanugame prāgbhāvitvamātreṇa duravaseyam | athābhihitam evopādānakāraṇatvasya lakṣaṇaṃ tadavagamāt kathaṃ tad duravaseyam | satyam, uktam na tu kasyacid rūpasyānanugame tat sambhavati | nāpy avasāntuṃ śakyam |
 tathā hi – yat svagataviśeṣādhāyakatvam upādānatvam uktaṃ tat kiṃ svagatakatipayaviśeṣādhāyakatvam, āhosvit sakalaviśeṣādhāyakatvam iti ? tatra yadi prathamaḥ pakṣaḥ, sa na yuktaḥ; sarvajñajñāne svākārārpakasyāsmadādivijñānasya taṃ pratyupādānabhāvaprasaṅgāt | tathā, rūpasyāpi rūpajñānaṃ pratyupādānabhāvaprasaktiḥ, tasyāpi svagatakatipayaviśeṣādhāyakatvāt; anyathā nirākārasya bodhasya sarvān praty aviśeṣād rūpasyaivāyaṃ grāhako na rasādeḥ iti tataḥ pratikarmavyavasthā na syāt | rūpopādānatve ca jñānasya paralokāya datto jalāñjaliḥ syāt | kiñ ca, katipayaviśeṣādhāyakatvenopādānatve ekasyaiva jñānakṣaṇasya tatkāryānugatavyāvṛttānekadharmasambandhitvābhyupagame viruddhadharmādhyāso 'bhyupahato bhavati; tathā ca yathā yugapadbhāvyanekaviruddhadharmādhyāse 'py ekaṃ vijñānaṃ tathā kramabhāvyanekataddharmayoge kim ity ekaṃ nābhyupagamyeta ?
 atha sakalaviśeṣādhāyakatvena, na tarhi nirvikalpakāt savikalpakotpattiḥ | na ca nirvikalpakayor apy upādānopādeyatvenābhyupagatayos tadbhāvaḥ syāt, tathā ca kuto rūpākārāt samanantarapratyayāṭ kadācid rasādyākārasyāpy upādeyatvenābhimatasyotpattiḥ ?
 atha vijñānasantānabahutvābhyupagamād nāyaṃ doṣaḥ – tena sarvasya svasadṛśasyotpattiḥ – tarhy asmin darśane ekasminn api santāne pramātṛnānātvaprasaṅgaḥ; tathā ca gavāśvadarśanayor bhinnasantānavarttinor ekena dṛṣṭe 'rthe 'parasyānusandhānaṃ na syāt, devadattayajñadattasantānagatayor ivānyenānubhūte 'nyasya | dṛśyate ca
 gām ahaṃ jñātavān pūrvam aśvaṃ jānāmy ahaṃ punaḥ [ŚV ātma 122]
 kiñ ca, sakalasvagataviśeṣādhāyakatve sarvātmanopādeyajñānakṣaṇe tasyopayogād anupayuktasyāparasvabhāvasyābhāvād yogivijñānam rūpādikaṃ caikasāmagryantargataṃ prati na sahakāritvaṃ tasyeti sahakārikāraṇābhāve nopādeyakṣaṇavyatiriktakāryāntarotpādaḥ |
 atha yeṣāṃ kāraṇam eva kāryatayā pariṇamati  teṣāṃ bhavatv ayaṃ diṣo na tv asmākaṃ prāgbhāvamātraṃ kāraṇatvam abhyupagacchatām | nanv atrāpi mate yena svarūpeṇavijñānam upādeyaṃ vijñānāntaraṃ janayati kiṃ tenaiva rūpam ekasāmagryantargatam, uta svabhāvāntareṇa ? tatra yadi tenaiva tadā rūpam api jñānam upādeyabhūtaṃ syāt, tatsvabhāvajanyatvāt; taduttarajñānakṣaṇavat | atha svabhāvāntareṇa tadopādānābhimataṃ jñānaṃ dvisvabhāvam āsajyates | yathā copādānasahakārisvabhāvarūpadvayayogas tathā trailokyāntargatānyajanyakāryāntarāpekṣayā tasyājanakatvam api svabhāvaḥ; tataś caikatvaṃ jñānakṣaṇasya yathopādānasahakāryajanakatvānekaviruddhadharmādhyāsitasyābhyupagamyate tathā harṣaviṣādādyanekavivarttātmanas tatsantānasyāpy abhyupagantavyam | athopādānasahakāryajanakatvādayo dharmās tatra kalpanāśilpakalpitāḥ; ekatvaṃ tu tasya svasaṃvedanādhyakṣasiddham iti na tais tad apanīyata iti matis tarhy ātmano 'py ekatvaṃ santānaśabdābhidheyatayā prasiddhasya svasaṃvedanādhyakṣasiddhasya kramavad dharṣaviṣādādikāryadarśanānumīyamānatadapekṣajanakatvājanakatvadharmāpaneyaṃ na syāt | tan na svagatasakaladharmādhāyakatvam upādānatvaṃ bhavadabhyupagamena saṅgatam | nāpi santānanuvṛttyā kāryotpādakatvasvabhāvam, tathābhyupagame jñānasantānanuvṛtteḥ paralokābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ |
 atha samanantarapratyayatvam upādānatvam ucyate | tathā hi – samas tulyaḥ, anantaro 'vyavahitaḥ, pratyayoḥ janakaḥ |
 89
 na caitad bhinnasantānād iti na tatrānusandhānasambhavaḥ | nanv atrāpi samatvaṃ kāryeṇa yady upādānatvaṃ pratyayasya tadā vaktavyam – kiṃ sarvathā samatvam, utaikadeśena ? yadi sarvathā tad asat, kāryakāraṇayoḥ sarvathā tulyatve yathā kāraṇasya prāgbhāvitvaṃ tathā kāryasyāpi syāt; tathā ca kāryakāraṇayor ekakālatvād na kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ, na hy ekakālayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ savyetaragoviṣāṇavat | tathā, kāraṇābhimatasyāpi svakāraṇakālatā, tasyāpi svakāraṇakālateti sakalasantānaśūnyam idānīṃ samastaṃ jagat syāt – ata kathañcit samānatā, tathā sati yogijñānasyāpy asmadādijñānālambanasya tadākāratvenaikasantānatvaṃ syāt ityādi dūṣaṇaṃ pūrvoktam eva | athānantaratvam upādānatvam, nanu kṣaṇikaikāntapakṣe sarvajagatkṣaṇānantaraṃ vivakṣitakṣaṇe jagad jāyata iti sarveṣām upādānatvam ity ekasantānatvaṃ jagataḥ | deśānantaryaṃ tatrānupayogi, deśavyavahitasyāpīhajanmamaraṇacittasya bhāvijanmacittopādānatvābhyupagamāt | pratyayatvaṃ tu nopādānatvam, sahakāritve 'pi pratyayatvasya bhāvāt  tan na samanantarapratyayatvam apy upādānatvam | na ca pratikṣaṇaviśarārūṣu bhāveṣu kathañcid ekānvayam antareṇa janakatvam api saṅgacchate kim utopādānādivibhāgaḥ iti kṣaṇabhaṅgabhaṅgapratipādanāvasare 'bhidhāsyāmaḥ |
 atra kecit tulye 'pi janakatve svaparasantānagatayor vijñānayor upādānatve kāraṇam āhuḥ – svasantatau cetitaṃ jñānaṃ jñānāntarajanakam, na tv evaṃ parasantatau; ato janakatvavyatiriktasyopādānakāraṇatve nimittasya sambhavād itthaṃbhūtād dhetuphalabhāvād vyavasthā | asyāpi vyavasthānimittatvam ayuktam, na hi jñānam asaṃveditaṃ vyavasthāṃ labhate | saṃvedanaṃ hi jñānānāṇ svata eveṣyate, tac ca svasantatipatite iva parasantatipatite 'pi tulyam | jñānāntaravedyatvaṃ tu na śākyair abhyupagamyate jñānasya niyamata iti nāyam apy atiprasaṅgaparihātaḥ | na ca svasantatāv api svasasṃveditajñānapūrvakatā jñānasya siddhā, mūrcchādyavasthottarakālabhāvijñānasya tathātvānavagamāt; yato vijñānapūrvakatve 'pi tatra vipratipannā vādinaḥ kutaḥ punaḥ samviditajñānapūrvakatvam ? tatraitat syāt – vijñānapūrvakatvasyānumānena niścayāt kathaṃ vipratipattiḥ ? tac ca darśitam tajjātīyāt tajjātīyotpattiḥ iti, etad asat; atajjātīyād api bhāvānām utpattidarśanāt, yathā dhūmādeḥ |
 ye 'py atrāhuḥ – sadṛśatādṛśabhedena bhāvānāṃ vijātīyotpattyasambhavād etad dūṣaṇam teṣām api sadṛśatādṛśaviveko nārvāgdṛkpramātṛgocaraḥ, kāryanirūpaṇāyām api tayor viveko durlabhas tasmād ayam aparihāraḥ | yaiḥ punar ucyate – sarvasya samānajātīyād upādānād utpattiḥ, ādyasyāpi dhūmakṣāṇasyopādānatvena vyavasthāpitāḥ kāṣṭhāntargatā aṇavaḥ tatrāpi sajātīyatvaṃ na vidmaḥ | rūpādīnāṃ hi rūpādipūrvakatvena vā sajātīyatvam, dhūmatvopalakṣitāvayavapūrvakatvena vā ? prācye vikalpe nedānīṃ vijātīyād utpattir gor apy aśvād upajāyamānasya | uttaravikalpe 'pi kāṣṭhāntargatānām avayavānāṃ dhumatvaṃ laukikam, pāribhāṣikaṃ vā ? paribhāṣāyās tāvad ayam aviṣayaḥ – loke 'pi tadākāravyavasthitānām avayavānāṃ naiva dhūmatvavyavahāraḥ | tārkikeṇāpi lokaprasiddhavyavahārānusaraṇaṃ yuktaṃ kartum | tasmān na sajātīyād utpattiḥ |
 yac cātrocyate – tasyām avasthāyāṃ vijñānābhāve tadavasthātaḥ pracyutasyottarakālam īdṛśī saṃvittir na bhavet na mayā kiñcid api cetitam smṛtir hīyam anubhavapūrvikā, ato yenānubhavena satā na kiñcic cetyate tasyām avasthāyāṃ tasyāvaśyaṃ sadbhāvo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ etat suvyāhṛtam, na kiñcic cetitaṃ mayā iti bruvatā vastvavedanaṃ vocyeta, svarūpāvedanaṃ vā ? vastvavedane sakalapratiṣedho na yuktaḥ | svarūpāvedanaṃ tu svasaṃvedanābhyupagame dūrotsāritam | tasmād idānīm eva manovyāpārāt tadavasthābhāvī sarvān avagamaḥ saṃvedyate |
 astu vā tasyām avasthāyāṃ vijñānaṃ tathāpi janakatvātiriktavyāpāraviśeṣābhāvaḥ, samanantarapratyayatve janakatvātirikte 'bhyupagamyamāne tayos tāttvikabhedaprasaṅgaḥ; tathā ca yad evaikasyāṃ jñānasantatau samanantarapratyayatvaṃ tad eva parasantatāv avalambanatvena janakatvam ity etan na syāt | atha janakatvasamanantaratvādayo dharmāḥ kālpanikāḥ, akalpitaṃ tu yat svarūpaṃ tat tāttvikam, tac ca bodharūpam; kim idānīṃ sāṃvṛtād rūpād bhāvānām utpattiḥ ? nety ucyate, kathaṃ vā kālpanikatvam ? atha janakatvātiriktasya samanantarapratyayanasyaivam ucyate kathaṃ tannibandhanā vyavasthā ?
 90
