 tathā hi – ekasyāṃ santatau parasantatigatena vijñānena tulye 'pi janakatve samanantarapratyayatvena jananaviśeṣam aṅgīkṛtyaikasantānavyavasthā kriyate, yadā tu vyavasthānibandhanasyāpi sāṃvṛtatvaṃ tatas tatkṛtāyā vyavasthāyāḥ paramārthasattvaṃ durbhaṇam iti | ayam api saugatānāṃ doṣo na jainānām, yato jñānapūrvakatvaṃ jñānasya, svasaṃvedajanaṃ ca jñānasya svarūpam ity etat prāk pratisādhitam |
 yac coktam atatjjātīyād api bhāvānāṃ utpattidarśanād, yathā dhūmādeḥ ity api na saṅgatam, yato nāsmābhir atajjātīyotpattir nābhyupagamyate – vilakṣaṇād api pāvakāt dhūmotpattidarśanāt – kin tu kāraṇagatadharmānuvidhānaṃ kāryatvābhyupagamanibandhaṃ; tac ca jñānasya pradarśitaṃ prāk | na ca kāyavijñānayor ivānaladhūmayoḥ sarvathā vailakṣaṇyam, pudgalavikāratvena dvayor api sādṛśyāt | sarvathā sādṛśye ca kāryakāraṇabhāvābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ ekatvaprāpteḥ | yat tu sadṛśatādṛśavivekaḥ kāryanirūpaṇāyām api durlabhaḥ tatra yaḥ kāryadarśanād api vivekaṃ nāvadhārayituṃ kṣamas tasyānumānavyavahāre 'nadhikāra eva | tad uktam -
 suvivecitaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇam na vyabhicarati XE "[PVin II 37,15 ~ PVSV 23,26]"[PVin II 37,15 ~ PVSV 23,26], atas tadavadhāraṇe yatno vidheyaḥ []
 ata eva rūpādīnāṃ hi rūpādipūrvakatvena vā [] ityādi anabhimatopālambhamātram, kathañcit sādṛśyasya kāryakāraṇayor darśitatvāt; tasya ca prakṛte pramāṇasiddhatvāt |
 yac ca suptamūrcchatādyavasthāsu vijñānābhāvena tatpūrvakatvam uttarajñānasya na sambhavati ity abhyadhāyi [], tad asat; tadavasthāyāṃ vijñānābhāvagrāhakapramāṇāsambhavāt | tathā hi – na tāvat supta eva tadavasthāyāṃ vijānābhāvaṃ vetti, tadā vijñānānabhyupagamāt; tadavagame ca tasyaiva jñānatvād na tadavasthāyāṃ tadabhāvaḥ | nāpi pārśvastito 'nyas tadabhāvaṃ vetti, kāraṇavyāpakasvabhāvānupalabdhānāṃ viruddhavidher vātra viṣaye 'vyāpārāt anyasya tadabhāvāvabhasatvāyogāt | na cābhāvavat tadbhāvasyāpi tasyām avasthāyām apratipattiḥ, svātmani svasaṃviditavijñānāvinābhūtatvena niścitasya prāṇāp-ānaśarīroṣṇatākāraviśeṣādes tadavasthāyām upalabhyamānaliṅgasya sadbhāvenānumānapratītyutpatteḥ | jāgradavasthāyāṃ api parasantanipatitacetovṛtter asmadādibhir yathoktaliṅgadarśanodbhūtānumānam antareṇāpratipatteḥ | na kiñcic cetitaṃ mayā iti smaraṇād uttarakālabhāvinas tadavasthāyām anubhavānummāne kiṃ vastvasaṃvedanam, svarūpāsaṃvedanaṃ vā ityādi yad dūṣaṇam abhihitam, tad apy asāram; jāgradavasthābhāvisvasaṃviditagacchattṛṇasparśajñānāśvavikalpasamayagodarśanādiṣūttarakālabhāvi na mayā kiñcid pralakṣitam iti smaraṇaliṅgabalodbhūtānumānaviṣayeṣv apy asya samānatvāt | na ca svasaṃveditavijñānavādino 'trāpi samāno doṣa iti vaktuṃ yuktam, yasya yāvatī mātrā [   ] iti svasaṃveditajñānasyābhyupagamāt |
 yac ca samanantarasahakāritvādyanekadharmayuktatvam ekakṣaṇe jñānasyāsajyate iti pratipāditam tadabhyupagamyamānatvenādūṣaṇam | ataḥ paurvāparyavyavasthitaharṣaviṣādādyanekaparyāyavyāpyekātmavyatirekeṇa jñānayoḥ svasantāne 'py anusandhānanimittopādānopādeyabhāvāsambhavād na parasantānavad anusandhānapratyayaḥ syāt, dṛśyate ca; ato 'nekatvavyāvṛttād anusandhānapratyayād api liṅgād ātmasiddhiḥ |
 athāpi syād gamakatvaṃ hi hetoḥ svasādhyāvinābhāvagrahaṇapūrvakam, tadgrahaṇaṃ ca dharmyantare, na cātraikakartṛkatvena sādhyadharmivyatirikte dharmyantare pratisandhānasya vyāptiḥ yena pratisandhānād ekaḥ kartānumīyate | atha brūṣe kṣaṇikatāsādhakasya sattākhyasya hetor yathā dharmyantare vyāptyagrahaṇe 'pi gamakatā tadvad asyāpi, etad acāru; tasya hi kṣaṇikatāyāṃ prāk pratyakṣeṇa niścayān niścayaviṣayeṇa ca vyāpter darśanād vipakṣāt pracyāvitasya bādhakapramāṇena sādhyadharmiṇi yad avasthānaṃ tad eva svasādhyena vyāptigrahaṇam | ata evāsya hetoḥ sādhyadharmiṇy eva vyāptiniścayam icchanti | nanu vyāptisādhyaniścayayor niyamena paurvāparyam abhyupagantavyam, vyāptiniścayasya sādhyapratipattyaṅgatvāt; atra tu sāghyadharmiṇi vyāptiniścayābhyupagame sādhyapratipattikālo 'nyo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ; na cāsāv anyo 'nubhūyatve; asty etat kāryahetor api;
 91
 asya tu bādhakāt pramāṇād vipakṣāt pracyutasya yad eva sādhyadharmiṇi svasādhyavyāptatayā grahaṇaṃ tad eva sādhyagrahaṇam | na cāsyaivaṃ dvairūpyam, yato vipakṣād vyāvṛttir evānvayam ākṣipati | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ – kasyacid hetor vyāptiviṣayapradarśanāya dharmiviśeṣaḥ pradarśayate, asya tu yat sat tat kṣaṇikam iti dharmiviśeṣāpradarśane 'pi dharmimātrākṣepeṇa vyāptipradarśanam | tac ca sattvaṃ kvacid vyavasthitam upalabhyamānaṃ kṣaṇikatāpratipattyaṅgam, ataḥ pakṣadharmatāpy atrāsti, na cātraivam | 
 atrāpy enam eva nyāyaṃ kecid āhuḥ | katham ? tatra hi vyāpakasya kramayaugapadyasya nivṛttyā vipakṣāt tannivṛttiḥ; atrāpi pramātṛniyatatāyā vyāpikāyā abhāvād vipakṣāt pratisandhānalakṣaṇasya hetor nivṛttiḥ | atha tatra bādhakapramāṇasya vyāptiḥ pratyakṣeṇa niścīyate – kramayaugapadyābhyām arthakriyākaraṇasya pratyakṣeṇa niścayāt – atra tu katham ? atrāpi pramātṛniyamapūrvakatvena svasantāna eva pratisandhānasya vyātpiniścayāt kathaṃ na tulyatā ?
 atha brūyāt pramātṛniyatatā ity asya bhāṣitasya ko 'rthaḥ ? yadi paraṃ bhaṅgyantareṇaikakartṛkatvaṃ sādhyaṃ vyapadiśyate tasya pratyakṣeṇa niścayābhyupagame kathaṃ bādhakapramāṇāvaseyatā vyāpteḥ ? naitat, pramātṛniyatatāgraḥaṇaṃ naikakartṛkatvagrahaṇam; sarve eva hi bhāvā deśādiniyatatayāvasīyamānā vyavahāragocaratām upayānti, pramātur apy avasāya evam eva dṛśyate – idānīm atrāham | evaṃ deśādyasaṃsargavat pramātrantarāsaṃsargo 'pi niścīyate | tathā hi – deśakālanibandhnaniyamavad vyatiriktapadārthāsaṃsargasvabhāvaniyatapratibhāso 'pi ghaṭāder iva, atraikatvānekatvaniścayābhāvaḥ | pūrvapākṣikamate tasya nānākartṛkeṣu santānāntareṣu vyāpakasyābhāvād vipakṣāt pracyutasya pratisandhānasya kvacidupalabhyamāṇasyaikakartṛkatvena vyāptiḥ | yathā kramayaugapadyābhyām arthakriyākaraṇadarśane naivaṃ niścayaḥ – kiṃ kṣaṇikaiḥ kramayaugapadyābhyāṃ sā kriyate, āhosvid anyathā iti, atha ca pratyakṣena bādhakasya vyāptyavasāye paścād vyāpakānupalabdhyā mūlahetor vyāptisiddhiḥ evaṃ ekakartṛkatvānavasāye 'pi pramātṛniyatatayā pratisandhānasya svasantatau vyāptiniścaye saty uttarakālaṃ vipakṣe vyāpakasya pramātṛniyatatvasyābhāvād ekakartṛkatvena pratisandhānasya vyāptisiddhiḥ | evam anabhyupagame aham anyo vā iti pramātraniścaye prameyāniścayād andhamūkaṃ jagat syāt | aupacārikasya pramātṛniyatatayā pratibhāsaviṣayatve 'nātmapratyakṣatvaṃ doṣaḥ |
 tatraitat syāt – asty ayaṃ pramātṛniyamaniścayaḥ; sa tu svasantatau kim ekakartṛkatvakṛtaḥ, utsvin nimittāntarakṛto yuktaḥ ? tac caikasyāṃ santatau hetuphalabhāvalakṣaṇaṃ prāk pradarśitam | satyam, pradarśitaṃ na tu sādhitam | tathā hi – tatkṛtaḥ pramātṛniyamo nānyakṛta iti naitāvat pratyakṣasya viṣayaḥ; na ca pramānāntarasyāpi | tad dhi asmin viṣaye ucyamānam anumānam ucyeta, tad api pratyakṣaniṣedhān niṣiddham | na ca kṣaṇikatvavyavasthāpane hetuphalabhāvakṛto niyama ity abhyupagantuṃ yuktam, tasyopariṣṭāt niṣetsyamānatvāt | na cāta eva dōṣād ekakartṛkatvakṛto 'pi na niyama iti vaktuṃ śakyam  svasaṃvedanapratyakṣasiddhatvasya tatpūrvakānumānasiddhatvasya cātmani prāg vyavasthāpitatvāt | abhyupagamavādena tu kṣaṇikatvavyavasthāpakasattvahetutulyatvam anusandhānapratyayahetoḥ pradārśitam; na tu kṣaṇikatvavad ātmaikatvasya pratyakṣāsiddhatvam yenānumānāt tatprasiddhyabhyupagame itaretarāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ preryete | ato 'dhyakṣānumānapramāṇasiddhatvāt paralokinaḥ paralokino 'bhāvāt paralokābhāvaḥ iti sūtraṃ niḥsāratayā vyavasthitam |
 yad apy ucyate śarīrāntargataṃ saṃvedanaṃ kathaṃ śarīrāntarasañcāri; jīvatas tāvann aśarīrāntarasañcāro dṛṣṭaḥ; parasmin maraṇasamaye bhaviṣyatīti duranvayam etat tad api na yuktam; yataḥ kumāraśarīrāntargatāḥ pāṇḍityādivikalpā vṛddhāvasthāśarīrasañcāriṇo dṛśyante jīvata eva, capalatādiśarīrāvasthāviśeṣāḥ vāgvikārāś ca; tat kathaṃ na jīvataḥ śarīrāntarasañcāraḥ ? athaikam evedaṃ śarīraṃ bālakumārādibhedabhinnam, janmāntaraśarīraṃ tu mātāpitrantarśukraśoṇitaprabhavam śarīrāntaraprabhavam, etad apy ayuktam; bālakumāraśarīrasyāpi bhedāt; yathā ca bālakumāraśarīracapalatābhedas tarūṇādiśarīrasañcārī upalabhyate tathā nijajanmāntaraśarīraprabhavaṣ capalatādibhedaḥ parabhavabhāvijanmaśarīrasañcārī bhaviṣyatīti tato na  mātāpitṛśukaśoṇitānvayi janmādiśarīram api tu svasantānaśarīrānvayam eva, vṛddhādiśarīravat;
 92
 anyathā mātāpitṛśarīracapalatādivilakṣaṇaśarīraceṣṭāvan na syāt | athehajanmabālakumārādyavasthābhede 'pi pratyabhijñānād ekatvaṃ siddhaṃ śarīrasya tadavasthāvyāpakasya tena na taddṛṣṭāntabalād atyantabhinne janmāntaraśarīrādau jñānasañcāro yuktaḥ, tad asat; pūrvottarajanmaśarīrajñānasañcārakāriṇaḥ kārmaṇaśarīrasyāta eva kathañcid ekatvasiddheḥ | tathā hi – jñānaṃ tāvad ihajanmādāv anyanijajanmajñānaprabhavaṃ prasādhitam, tasya cehajanmabālakumārādyavasthābhedeṣu tad evedaṃ śarīram ity abādhitapratyabhijñāpratyayāvagataikarūpānvayiṣu sañcāradarśanāt pūrvottarajanmāvasthāsv api tathābhūtānugāmirūpasamanvitāsu tasya sañcāro 'numīyate | na cāsmadādīndriyasaṃvedyarūpādyāśrayasyaudārikaśarīrasya janmāntaraśarīrādyavasthānugamaḥ sambhavati, tasya tadaiva ca dāhādinā dhvaṃsopalabdheḥ | ato janmadvayāvasthāvyāpakasyoṣmādidharmānugatasya kārmaṇaśarīrasya vijñānasañcārakāriṇaḥ sadbhāvaḥ siddhaḥ | pūrvottarajanmāvasthāvyāpakasyāvasthātus tadavasthābhyaḥ kathañcid bhedād mātāpitṛśarīravilakṣaṇanijaśarīrāvasthācapalatādyanuvidhāne uttarāvasthāyāḥ kathaṃ nāvasthātṛdharmānuvidhānam ?
 tasmād yasyaiva saṃskāraṃ niyamenānuvartate | XE "[PV II 78ab]"[PV II 78ab]
 śarīraṃ pūrvadehasya tat tadanvayi yuktimat || [   ]
 atha pūrvāparayoḥ pratyakṣāsyāpravṛttena kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ, anumānasiddhāv itaretarāśrayadośaḥ iti, tad api prativihitam, evaṃ hi sarvaśūnyatvam āyātam iti kasya dūṣaṇaṃ sādhanaṃ vā kena pramāṇena ? ihalokasyāpy abhāvaprasakter iti pratipāditatvāt |
 atha kāryaviśeṣasya viśiṣṭakāraṇapūrvakatvasiddhau yathoktaprakāreṇa bhavatu pūrvajanmasiddhiḥ, bhāviparalokasiddhis tu kathaṃ bhāvini pramāṇābhāvāt ? tatrāpi kāryaviśeṣād eveti brūmaḥ | tathā hi – kāryaviśeṣo viṣiṣṭaṃ sattvam eva, tac ca na sattāsambhandalakṣaṇam; tasya niṣetsyamānatvāt | nāpy arthakriyākāritvalakṣaṇam, santativyavacchede tasyābhāvaprasaṅgāt | tathā hi śabdabuddhipradīpādisantānānāṃ yady ucchedo 'bhyupagamyate tadā tatsantaticaramakṣaṇasyāparakṣaṇājananād asattvam, tadasattve ca pūrvapūrvakṣaṇānām arthakriyājananād asattvam iti sakalasantatyabhāvaḥ | atha santatyantakṣaṇaḥ sajātīyakṣaṇāntarājanane 'pi sarvajñasantāne svagrāhijñānajanakatvena sann iti nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, tad asat; svasantatipatitopādeyakṣaṇājanakatve parasantānavarttisvagrāhijñānajanakatvasyāpy asambhavāt | na hy upādānakāraṇatvābhāve sahakārikāraṇatvaṃ kvacid apy upalabdham; tatsadbhāve vā ekasāmagryadhīnasya rūpāder asatas tatsamānakālabhāvino 'vyabhicāriṇī pratipattir na syāt, rūpakṣaṇasya svopādeyakṣaṇāntarājanane 'pi rasasantatau sahakārikāraṇatvena rasakṣaṇajanakatvābhyupagamāt; tatsadbhāve 'pi tatsamānakālabhāvino rūpāder abhāvāt | tan nopādānakāraṇatvābhāve sahakāritvasyāpi sambhava iti svasantatyucchedābhyupagame 'rthakriyālakṣaṇasya sattvasyāsambhava iti utpādavyayadhrauvyalakṣaṇam eva sattvam abhyupagantavyam iti kāryaviśeṣalakṣaṇād dhetor yathoktaprakāreṇātītakālavad anāgatakālasambandhitvam apy ātmanaḥ siddham |
 yad apy uktam yady āgamasiddhatvaṃ ātmanaḥ, tasya vā pratiniyatakarmaphalasambandhas tatsiddho 'bhyupagamyate tadānumānavaiyarthyam iti, tad api mūrkheśvaraceṣṭitam; na hi vyartham iti nijakāraṇasāmagrībalāyātaṃ vastu pratikṣeptuṃ yuktam; na hi āgamasiddhāḥ padārthāḥ iti pratyakṣasyāpi pratikṣepo yuktaḥ | yad api pratyakṣānumānānuṣaye cārthe āgamaprāmāṇyavādibhis tasya prāmāṇyam abhyupagamyate – tad uktam -
 āmnāyasya kriyārthatvād ānarthakyam atadarthānām [MS 1.2.1] iti,
 tad apy ayuktam; yato yathā pratyakṣapratīte 'py arthe vipratipattiviṣaye 'numānam api pravṛttim āsādayatīti pratipāditaṃ tathā pratyakṣānumānapratipanne 'py ātmalakṣaṇe 'rthe tasya vā pratiniyatakarmaphalasambandhalakṣaṇe kim ity āgamasya pravṛttir nābhyupagamasya viṣayaḥ ?

 na cāgamasya tatrāprāmāṇyam iti vaktuṃ yuktam, sarvajñapraṇītatvena tatprāmāṇyasya vyavasthāpitatvāt | pratiniyatakarmaphalasambandhapratipādakaś cāgamaḥ -
 bahvārambhaparigrahatvaṃ ca nārakasya [Tattvā. A. 9.19]
 ityādinā vācakamukhyena sūtrīkṛto 'syaivānumānaviṣayatvam pratipādayitukāmena | yathā ca karmaphalasambandho 'py ātmano'numānād avasīyate tathā yathāsthānam ihaiva pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ | ātmasvarūpapratipādakaḥ pratiniyatakarmaphalasambandhapratipādakaś cāgamaḥ -

 puvviṃ ducciṇṇāṇaṃ duppaḍikaṃtāṇaṃ kaḍāṇaṃ kammānam [   ]
 ityādikaḥ suptasiddha eva | tad evaṃ pratyakṣānumānāgamapramāṇaprasiddhatvād nārakatiryagnarāmaraparyāyānubhūtasvabhāvasyātmanaḥ, na bhavaśabdavyutpattiḥ arthābhāvāt ḍitthādiśabdavyutpattitulyā iti sthitam ||

 [pūrvapakṣaḥ – jagataḥ īśvarakṛtatvasthāpanam]
 
 atrāhur nayāyikāḥ – kleśakarmavipākāśayāparāmṛṣṭapuruṣābhyupagame nityaṃ sattvam asattvaṃ vā [] iti dūṣaṇam abhyadhāyi tatra tan nityasattvapratipādane nāsmākaṃ kācit kṣatiḥ, pramāṇato nityajñānādidharmakalāpānvitasya tasyābhyupagamāt |
 nanu yuktam etad yadi tathābhūtapuruṣasadbhāvapratipādakaṃ kiñcit pramāṇaṃ syāt, tac ca nāsti | tathā hi – pratyakṣaṃ tathāvidhapuruṣasadbhāvāvedakam asmadādīnām | asmadvilakṣaṇayogibhis tasyāvasāya ity atrāpi na kiñcit pramāṇam asti – yadā na tatsvarūpagrahaṇe pratyakṣapramāṇapravṛttis tadā tadgatadharmāṇāṃ nityajñānādīnāṃ sadbhāvavārttaiva na sambhavati |
 nānumānam api yuktam etatsvarūpāvedakam, pratyakṣaniṣedhe tatpūrvakasya tasyāpi niṣedhāt | sāmānyatodṛṣṭasyāpi nātra viṣaye pravṛttiḥ, liṅgasya kasyacit tatpratipādakasyābhāvāt kāryatvasya pṛthipṛthivyādisaṃsthānasyātyantavailakṣaṇyāt saṃsthānaśabdavācyatvena cātiprasaktidarśitā
 vastubhedaprasiddhasya śabhdasāmyād abhedinaḥ XE "[PV II 12ab]"[PV II 12ab]
 ityādinā | tasmād nānumānaṃ tatsādhanāyālam |
 napyāgamaḥ, nityasyātra darśane 'nabhyupagamāt; abhyupagame vā kāryārthapratipādakasya siddhe vastuny avyāpṛteḥ | nāpīśvarapūrvakasya prāmāṇyam, itaretarāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāt | anīśvarapūrvakasyāpi sambhāvyamānadoṣatvena pramāṇatānupapatteḥ | tasyānyeśvarapūrvakatve tasyāpi siddhiḥ kuta iti vaktavyam, tadasiddhau na tasya prāmāṇyam, anekeśvaraprasaṅgadoṣaś ca | bhavatu, ko doṣaḥ yata ekasyāpi sādhane vayam atīvotsukāḥ kiṃ punar bahūnām iti cet, na kaścit pramāṇābhāvaṃ muktvā | tan nāgamato 'pi tatpratipattiḥ | evaṃ svarūpāsiddhau kathaṃ tasya kāraṇatā ?
 atrāhuḥ – yad uktam na tatra pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam tad evam eva | yad api sarvaprakārasyāgamasya na tatsvarūpāvedane vyāpṛtiḥ tatrocyate – āgamāvyāpāre 'pi tatsvarūpasādhakam anumānaṃ vidyate | āgamasyāpi siddhe 'rthe liṅgadarśananyāyena yathā vyāpṛtis tathā pratipādayiṣyāṃaḥ | pratyakṣapūrvakānumānaniṣedhe siddhasādhanam, sāmānyatodṛṣṭānumānasya tatra vyāpārābhyupagamāt | nanv anumānapramāṇatāyām ayaṃ vicāro yuktārambhaḥ, tasyaiva tu prāmāṇyaṃ nānumanyante cārvākā iti, etac cānuddhoṣyam; anumānapramāṇyasya vyavasthāpitatvāt |
 yat tūktam pṛthivyādigatasya kāryatvasyāpratipatter na tasmād īśvarāvagamaḥ tatra pṛṭhivyādīnāṃ bauddhaiḥ kāryatvam abhyupagataṃ te katham evaṃ vadeyuḥ | ye 'pi cārvākādyāḥ pṛthivyādīnāṃ kāryatvaṃ necchanti teṣāṃ api viśiṣṭasaṃsthānayuktānāṃ katham akāryatā ?
 94
 sarvaṃ saṃsthānavat, kāryam, tac ca puruṣapūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam | ye 'py āhuḥ – saṃsthānaśabdavācyatvaṃ kevalaṃ ghaṭādibhiḥ samānaṃ pṛthivyādīnām na tatvato 'rthaḥ kaścid dvayor anugataḥ samāno vidyate | teṣām api na kevalam atrānugatārthābhāvaḥ kintu dhūmādāv api pūrvāparavyaktigato naiva kaścid anugato 'rthaḥ samāno 'sti |
 atha tatra vastudarśanāyāt akalpanānimittam uktam, atra tathābhūtasya pratibhāsasyābhāvān nānugatārthakalpanā | tathā hi – kasyacid ghaṭādeḥ kriyamāṇasya viśiṣṭāṃ racanāṃ kartṛpūrvikāṃ dṛṣṭvādṛṣṭakartṛkasyāpi ghaṭaprāsādādes tasya racanāviśeṣasya kartṛpūrvakatvapratipattiḥ, pṛthivyādes tu saṃsthānaṃ kadācid api kartṛpūrvakaṃ nāvagatam; nāpi tādṛśaṃ dharmyantare dṛṣṭakartṛka iva paṭādau; tat pṛthivyādigatasya saṃsthānasya vailakṣaṇaāt tato na tataḥ kartṛpūrvakatvapratipattiḥ; evaṃ hetor asiddatvena naitat sādhanam, ayuktam etat; yato yaty anavagatasambandhān pratipattṛXn adhikṛtya hetor asiddhatvan ucyate tadā dhūmādiṣv api tulyam | atha gṛhītāvinābhāvānām api kāryatvadarśanāt tan vādiṣu īśvarādikṛtatvapratibhāsānutpatter evam ucyate, tad asat; ye hi kāryatvāder buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvena gṛhītāvinābhāvās te tasmād īśvarādipūrvakatvaṃ teṣām avagacchanty eva | tasmād vyutpannānām asty eva pṛthivyādisaṃsthānavattvakāryatvāder hetor dharmidharmatāvagamaḥ, avyutpannānāṃ tu prasiddhānumāne dhūmādāv api nāsti |
 api ca, bhavatu prāsādādisaṃsthānebhyaḥ pṛthivyādisaṃsthānasya vailakṣaṇyaṃ tathāpi karyatvaṃ śākyādibhiḥ pṛthivyādīṇām iśyate, kāryaṃ ca kartṛkaraṇādipūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam; ataḥ kāryatvād buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvānumānam | atha kartṛpūrvakasya kāryatvasysa saṃsthānavattvasya ca tadvailakṣaṇyān na tataḥ sādhyāvagamaḥ | ata evādhiṣṭhātṛbhāvābhāvānuvṛttimad yat saṃsthānaṃ taddarśanāt kartradarśino 'pi tatpratipattir yuktety asya dūṣaṇasya kāryatve 'pi samānatvāt kathaṃ gamakatā ? yady evam anumkānocchedaprasaṅgaḥ; dhūmādikam api yathāvidham agnyādisāmagrībhāvābhāvānuvṛttimat tathāvidham eva yadi parvatopari bhavet syāt tato vahnyādyavagamaḥ | athādhūmavyāṛttaṃ tathāvidham eva dhūmādi tarhi kāryatvādy api tathāvidhaṃ pṛthivyādigataṃ kiṃ neṣyate ? atha pṛthivyādigatakāryatvādidarśanāt kartradarśināṃ tadapratipattiḥ, evaṃ śikharyādigatavahnyādyadarśināṃ dhūmadibhyo 'pi tadapratipattir astu | na cātra śabdasāmāṇyaṃ vastvanugamo nāstīti vaktuṃ yuktam, dhūmādāv api śabdasāmānyasya vaktum śakyatvāt | tan na śākyadṛṣṭyā kāryatvāder asiddhatā |
 nāpi cārvākamīmāṃsakadṛṣṭyā, teṣām api saṃsthānavad avaśyaṃ kāryaṃ ghaṭādivat pṛthivyādi svāvayavasaṃyogair ārabdham avaśyaṃtayā viśleṣād vināśam anubhaviṣyati; evaṃ vināśād vā sambhāvitāt kāryatvānumānam racanāsvabhāvatvād vā | yathoktam bhāṣyakṛtā -
 yeṣām apy anavagatotpattīnāṃ bhāvānāṃ rūpam upalabhyate teṣāṃ tantuvyatiṣaṅgajanitaṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadvyatiṣaṅgavimocanāt tadvināśād vā vinaṅkṣyatīty anumīyate [   ]
 anena saṃsthānavato 'nupalabhyamānotpatteḥ samavāyasaṃvāyikāraṇavināśād vināśam āha | tathā, pṛthivyādeḥ saṃsthānavato 'dṛṣṭajanmano rūpadarśanād nāśasambhāvanā bhaviṣyati; sambhāvitāc ca nāśāt kāryatvānumitau kartṛpratipattiḥ | yathoktaṃ nyāyavidbhiḥ
 tattvadarśanaṃ pratyakṣato 'numānato vā [   ]
 kāryatvavināśitvayoś ca samavyāptikatvād ekenāparasyānumānam iṣṭam tena yatrāpy ubhau dharmau [ŚV anu 9] ity atra | ato jaiminīyānāṃ na kāryatvāder asiddhatā |
 nāpi cārvākamate 'siddhatvam; teṣaṃ racanāvattvenāvaśyambhāvinī kāryatāpratipattir adṛṣṭotpattīnām api kṣityādīnām, anyathā vedaracanāyā api kartṛdarśanābhāvān na kāryatā, yatas tatrāpy etāvac chakyaṃ vaktum – na racanātvena vedaracanāyāḥ kāryatvānumānam, kartṛbhāvābhābānuvidhāyinī taddarśanāl laukiky eva racanā tatpūrvikāstu mā bhūd vaidikī | atha tayor viśeṣānupalambhāl laukikīva vaidiky api kartṛpūvikā tarhi prāsādādisaṃsthānavat pṛthivyādisaṃsthānavattvasyāpi tadrūpatāstu, viśeṣānupalakṣanāt | tan na hetor asiddhatā |
 95
 {mā bhūd asiddhatvaṃ tathāpy asmāt sādhyasiddhir na yuktā, na hi kevalāt pakṣadharmatvād vyāptiśūnyāt sādhyāvagamaḥ | nanu kiṃ ghaṭādau kartṛkarmakaraṇapūrvakatvena kāryatvāder vyāptyanavagamaḥ | asty evaṃ ghaṭagate kāryatve pratipattis tathāpi navyāptiḥ, sā hi sakalākṣepeṇa gṛhyate, atra tu vyāptigrahaṇakāla eva keṣāñcit kāryāṇām akartṛpūrvakānāṃ kāryatvadarśanān na sarvaṃ kāryaṃ kartṛpūrvakam, yathā vaneṣu vanaspatīnāṃ | atha tatra na kartrabhāvaniścayaḥ kiṃ tu kartragrahaṇam, tac ca vidyamāne 'pi kartari bhavatīti kathaṃ sādhyābhāve hetor darśanam ? kva punar vidyamānakartṛkāṇāṃ tadapratipattiḥ | yathā ghaṭādīnām anavagatotpattīnāṃ | yuktā tatra kartur apratipattiḥ, utpādakālānavagamāt; tatkāle ca tasya tatra sannidhānam, anyadāsya sannidhānābhāvād agrahaṇam; vanagateṣu ca sthāvareṣūpalabhyamānajanmasu kartṛsadbhāve tadavagamo 'vaśyaṃabhāvī, yathopalabhyamānajanmani ghaṭādau; ata upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasysa kartus teṣv abhāvaniścayāt tatra vyāptigrahaṇakāla eva kāryatvāder hetor darśanān na kartṛpūrvakatvena vyāptiḥ |
 itaś ca dṛṣṭānyadṛṣṭaparikalpanāsambhavāt – dṛṣṭāntāṃ kṣityādīnāṃ kāraṇatvatyāgaḥ adṛṣṭasya ca kartuḥ kāraṇatvakalpanā na yuktimatī | atha na kṣityādeḥ kāraṇatvanirākaraṇaṃ kartṛkalpanāyām api, tatsadbhāve 'pi tasyāparakāraṇatvakĺpteḥ, tad asat; yato yad yasyānvayavyatirekānuvidhāyi tat tasysa kāraṇam itarat kāryam; kṣityādīnāṃ tv anvayavyatirekāv anuvidhatte tatrākṛṣṭajātaṃ vanaspatyādi nāparasya, katham ato vyatiriktaṃ karaṇaṃ bhavet ? evam api kāraṇatvakalpanāyāṃ doṣa uktaḥ caitrasya vraṇarohaṇe [PV II 22b] ityādinā | tasmāt pakṣadharmatve 'pi vyāptyabhāvād agamakatvaṃ hetoḥ |
 atha teṣaṃ pakṣe 'ntarbhāvān na tair vyabhicāraḥ, tad asat; tāttvikaṃ vipakṣatvaṃ katham icchākalpitena pakṣatvenāpodyeta ? vyāptau siddhāyāṃ sādhyatadabhāvayor agrahaṇi vādīcchāparikalpitaṃ pakṣatvaṃ kathyate | grahaṇakāle vyabhicārāśaṅkāyāṃ niścaye nā vyabhicāraviṣayasya pakṣe 'ntarbhāvena gamakatvakalpane na kaścid dhetur vyabhicārī bhavet | tasmān neśvarasiddhau kaścid hetur avyabhicāry asti} |
 atrāhuḥ – nākṛṣṭajātaiḥ sthāvarādibhir vyabhicāraḥ vyāptyabhāvo vā, sādhyābhāve vartamāno hetur vyabhicārī ucyate, teṣaṃ tu kartragrahaṇam na sakartṛkatvābhāvaniścayaḥ | nanūktam upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatve kartur abhāvaniścasya tatra yuktaḥ naitad yuktam; upalabdhilakṣanaprāptatāyāḥ kartus teṣv anabhyupagamāt | yat tūktam kṣityādyanvayavyatirekānuvidhānadarśanāt teṣām tad vyatiriktasya kāraṇatvakalpane 'tiprasaṅgadoṣaḥ iti, etasyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ dharmādharmayor api na kāraṇatā bhavet | na ca tayor akāraṇataiva, tayoḥ kāraṇatvaprasādhanāt – na hi kiñcij jagaty asti yat kasyacin na sukhasādhanaṃ duḥkhasādhanaṃ vā | na ca tatsādhanasyādṛṣṭanirapekṣasyotpattiḥ | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ – śarīrādeḥ pratiniyatādṛṣṭākṣiptatvaṃ prāyeṇa, sarvopabhogyānāṃ tu sādhāraṇādṛṣṭākṣiptatvam ? etat sarvādibhir abhyupagamād apratyākhyeyam; yuktiś ca pradarśitaiva | cārvākair apy etad abhyupagantavyam, tān prati pūrvam etatsiddhau pramāṇasyoktatvāt | pramāṇasiddaṃ tu na kasyacin na siddham | athādṛṣṭasya kāryeṇānvayavyatirekānanuvidhāne 'pi nākāraṇatā, na tarhi vaktavayam bhūmyādivyatirekeṇa sthāvarādinā kāryeṇeśvarasyānvayavyatirekānanuvidhānād akāraṇatvam | atha jagadvaicitryam adṛṣṭasya kāraṇatvaṃ vinā nopapadyate iti tat kalpyate, sarvān utpattimataḥ prati bhūmyādeḥ sādhāraṇatvād ato 'dṛṣṭākhyavicitrakāraṇakṛtaṃ kāryavaicitryam; evam adṛṣṭasya kāraṇatvakalpanāyāṃ īśvasrasyāpi kāraṇatvapratikṣepo na yuktaḥ; yathā kāraṇagataṃ vaicitryaṃ vinā kāryagataṃ vaicitryaṃ nopapadyate iti tat parikalpyate tathā cetanaṃ kartāraṃ vinā kāryasvarūpānupapattir iti kim iti tasya nābhyupagamaḥ ? na cākṛṣṭajāteṣu sthāvarādiṣu tasyāgrahaṇena pratikṣepaḥ, anupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatvād adṛṣṭavat | na ca sarvā kāraṇasāmagryupalabdhilakṣanaprāptā | ata eva dṛśyamāneṣv api kāraṇeṣu kāraṇatvam apratyakṣam, kāryeṇaiva tasyopalambhāt |
 96
 sahakārisattā dṛśyamānasya kāranatā, keṣāñcit sahakāriṇāṃ dṛśyatve 'py adṛṣṭādeḥ sahakāriṇaḥ kāryeṇaiva pratipattiḥ; evaṃ īśvarasya kāraṇatve 'pi na tatsvarūpagrahaṇaṃ pratyakṣeṇeti sthitam | tato 'nupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatvāt kartur upalabhyamānajanmasu sthāvareṣu dhetor vṛttidarśanān na vyāptyabhāvah yato niścitavipakṣavṛttir hetur vyabhicārī |
 nanu niścitavipakṣavṛttir yathā vyabhicārī tathā sandigdhavyatireko 'pi ukteṣu sthāvareṣu kartragrahaṇaṃ kiṃ kartrabhāvāt, āhosvid vidyamānatve 'pi tasyāgrahaṇam anupalabdhilakṣanaprāptatvena ? evaṃ sandigdhavyatirekatve na kaścid dhetur gamakaḥ | dhumāder api sakalavyaktyākṣepeṇa vyāptyupalambhakāle na sarvā vahnivyaktayo dṛśyāḥ; tāsu cādṛśyāsu dhūmavyaktīnāṃ dṛśyatve sandigdhavyatirekāśaṅkā na nivartate yatra vahner adarśane dhūmadarśanaṃ tatra kiṃ vahner adarśanam abhāvāt, āhosvid anupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatvāt iti na niścayaḥ – ato dhūmo 'pi sandigdhavyatirekatvān na gamakaḥ |
 atha dhūmaḥ kāryaṃ hutabhujaḥ, tasya tadabhāve svarūpānupapatter adṛṣṭatve 'py analasya sadbhāvakalpanā; nanu tat kāryam atropalabhyamānaṃ kim iti kāraṇam antareṇa kalpyate ? atha dṛṣṭaśakteḥ kāraṇasya kalpanāstu mā bhūd buddhimataḥ, vahnyāder dhūmādīn prati kathaṃ dṛṣṭaśaktatā ? pratyakṣānupalambhābhyām iti cet, buddhimato 'pi tābhyāṃ kāraṇatvakĺptau vahnyādibhis tulyatā; yathā vahnyādisāmagryā dhūmādir janyamāno dṛṣṭaḥ sa tām antareṇa kadācid api na bhavati – svarūpahāniprasaṅgāt – tadvat sarvam utpattimat kartṛkarmakaraṇapūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam, tasya sakṛd api tathādarśanāt tajjanyatāsvabhāvaḥ, tasyaivaṃ svabhāvaniścitāv anyatamābhāve 'pi kathaṃ bhavaḥ ?
 kiṃ ca, anupalabhyamānakartṛkeṣu sthāvareṣu kartur anupalambhaḥ śarīrādyabhāvāt na tv asattvāt, yatra śarīrasya kartṛtā tatra kulālādeḥ pratyakṣeṇaivopalambhaḥ, atra tu caitanyamātreṇopādānādyadhiṣṭhānāt kathaṃ pratyakṣavyāpṛtiḥ ? nāpy etad vaktavyam śarīrādyabhāvāt tarhi kartṛtāpi na yuktā, kāryasya śarīreṇa saha vyabhicāradarśanāt – yathā svaśarīrasya pravṛttinivṛttī sarvaś cetanaḥ karoti tena tasya vyabhicāraḥ | atha śarīre eva dṛṣṭatvāt karotu nānyatra, tan na; yataḥ kāryaṃ śarūreṇa ninā karotīti naḥ sādhyam; tat svaśarīragatam anyaśarīragataṃ veti nānena kiñcit | etenaitad api parākṛtaṃ yad āhur eke
 acetanaḥ kathaṃ bhāvas tadicchām anuvartate [   ]
 acetanasya śarīrāder ātmecchānuvarttitvadarśanāt ? na cācetanasya tadicchānanuvarttino 'pi prayatnapreryatvaṃ parikāra iti vaktavyam, yata īṣvarasyāpi prayatnasadbhāve na kācit kṣatiḥ | na ca śarīrābhāvāt kathaṃ prayatna iti vaktum yuktam, śarīrāntarābhāve 'pi śarīrasya prayatnapreryatvadarśanāt | tat kartuḥ śarīrābhāvād akṛṣṭotpattiṣu sthāvareṣv agrahaṇam, na tatrādarśanena hetor vyabhicāraḥ | ye 'pi pratyakṣānupalambhasādhanaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvam āhus teṣām api kasyacit kāryakāraṇabhāvasya tatsādhanatve yathendriyānām adṛṣṭasyas ca tau vinā kāraṇatvaṃ siddhis tatheśvarasyāpi | ato na vyāptyabhāvaḥ |
 ata eva na satpratipakṣatāpi, naikasmin sādhyānvite hetau sthite dvitīyasya tathāvidhasya tatrāvakāśaḥ; vastuno dvairūpyāsambhavāt |
 nāpi bādhaḥ, abuddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvasya pramāṇenāgrahaṇāt; sādhyābhāve hetor abhāvaḥ svasādhyavyāptatvād eva siddhaḥ |
 nāpi dharmyasiddhatā, kāryakāraṇasaṅghātasya pṛthivyāder bhūtagrāmasya ca pramāṇena siddhatvāt | tadāśrayatvena hetor yathā pramāṇenopalambhas tathā pūrvaṃ pradarśitam | ato 'smādīśvarāvagame na tatsiddhau pramāṇābhāvaḥ |
 nāpi hetor viśeṣaviruddhatā, tadviruddhatve hetor viśeṣaṇe 'bhyupagamyamāne na kaścid dhetur anuruddo bhavet, prasiddhānumāne 'pi viśeṣaviruddhādīnāṃ sulabhatvāt | yathāyaṃ dhūmo dahanaṃ sādhayati tathaitaddeśāvacchinnavahnyabhāvam api sādhayati | na hi pūrvadhūmasyaitaddeśāvacchinnena vahninā vyāptiḥ | evaṃ kālādyavacchedena hetor viruddhatā vaktavyā | atha deśakāladīn vihāya vahnimātreṇa hetor vyāpter na viruddhatā tarhi tadvat kāryamātrasya buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvena vyāpter yady api dṛṣṭānte 'nīśvaro 'sarvajñaḥ kṛtrimajñānasambandhī saśarīraḥ kṣityādyupaviṣṭaḥ kartā tathāpi pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇānāṃ dharmiviśeṣarūpānāṃ vyabhicārāt tadviparyayasādhakatve 'pi na viruddhatā |
 97
 viruddo hi hetuḥ sādhyaviparyayakāritvād bhavati | na caiteṣāṃ sādhyatā, buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvamātrasyāsmād dhetoḥ sādhyatveneṣṭatvāt | yathā ca viśeṣaviruddhinām adūṣaṇatvaṃ tathā
 siddhāntam abhyupetya tadvirodhī viruddhaḥ [NS 1.2.6]
 ity atra sūtre nirṇītam |
 itaṣ caitad adūṣaṇam – pūrvasmād dhetoḥ svasādhyasiddhāv uttareṇa pūrvasiddhasyaiva sādhyasya kiṃ viśeṣaḥ sādhyate, uta pūrvahetoḥ svasādhyasiddhipratibandhaḥ kriyate ? na tāvat pūrvo vikalpaḥ, yadi nāma tatrāpareṇa hetunā viśeṣādhānāṃ kṛtaṃ kiṃ tāvatā pūrvasya hetoḥ sādhyasiddhivighātaḥ – yathā kṛtakatvena śabdasyānityatvasiddhau hetvantareṇa guṇatvasiddhāv api na pūrvasya kṣatis tadvad atrāpi | athottaro vikalpas tathāpi svasādhyasiddhipratibandho vyāptyabhāvapradarśanenea kriyate; vyāpyabhāvaś ca heturūpāṇām anyatamābhāvena | na ca dharmiviśeṣaviparyayodbhāvanena kasyacid api rūpasyābhāvaḥ kathyate | na ca heturūpābhāvāsiddhāv agamakatvam | tan na viśeṣaviruddhatā |
 viśeṣās tu dharmiṇaḥ svarūpasiddhāv uttarakālaṃ pramāṇāntarapratipādyā na tu pūrvahetubalād abhyupagamyante | tac ca pramāṇāntaram āgamaḥ pūrvahetor hetvantaraṃ ca | tac cānvayavyatirekipūrvakakevalavyatirekisaṃjñam | yathā gandhādyupalabdhyā tatsādhanakaraṇamātraprasiddhau prasaktapratiṣedhe karaṇaviśeṣasiddhiḥ kevalavyatirekinimittā tathehāpi kāryatvāt buddhimatkāraṇamātraprasiddhau prasaktapratiṣedhāt kāraṇaviśeṣasiddhiḥ kevalavyatirekinimittā | tathā hi – kāryatvād buddhimatkāraṇamātrasiddhau prasaktānāṃ kṛtrimajñānaśarīrasambaddhatvādīnāṃ dharmāṇāṃ pramānāntareṇa bādhopapattau viśiṣṭabuddhimatkāraṇasiddhir vyatirekibalāt iti kecit |
 anye manyante – yatrānvayavyatirekiṇo hetor na viśeṣasiddhis tatra tatpūrvakāt kevalavyatirekiṇo viśeṣasiddir bhavatu, yathā ghrāṇādiṣu, atra tu pūrvasmād dhetor viśeṣasiddhau na hetvantaraparikalpanā | yathā dhūmasya vahninānvayavyatirekasiddhau atra deśe vahniḥ iti pakṣadharmatvabalāt pratipattir nānvayād vyatirekād vā, tayor hy etaddeśāvacchinnena vahnināsambhavāt – yady api vyāptikāle sakalākṣepeṇa taddeśasyāpy ākṣepaḥ anyathātra vyāpter asambhavāt, tathāpi vyāptigrahaṇavelāyāṃ sāmānyarūpatayā tadākṣepaḥ na viśeṣarūpeṇeti viśeṣāvagamo nānvayavyatirekanimmittaḥ, api tu pakṣadharmatvakṛtaḥ | ata eva pratyutpannakāraṇajanyāṃ smṛtim anumānam āhuḥ | pratyutpannaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ pakṣadharmatvam eva – tathā kāryatvāder buddhimatkāraṇamātreṇa vyāptisiddhāv api kāraṇaviśeṣapratipattiḥ pakṣadharmatvasāmarthyāt | ya itthaṃbhūtasya pṛthivyādeḥ kartā niyamenāsāv akṛtrimajñānasambandhī śarīrarahitaḥ sarvajña ekaḥ iti, evaṃ yadā pakṣadharmatvabalād viśeṣasiddhis tadā na viśeṣaviruddhādīnām avakāśaḥ |
 anvayasāmarthyād api viśeṣasiddhim anye manyante | tathā, dhūmamātrasya vahnimātreṇa vyāptiḥ evaṃ dhūmaviśeṣasya vahniviśeṣaṇa iti dhūmaviśeṣapratipattau na vahnimātreṇānvayānusmṛtiḥ kintu vahniviśeṣeṇa, evaṃviśiṣṭakāryatvadarśanān na kāraṇamātrānusmṛtiḥ kintu tathāvidhakāryaviśeṣajanakakāraṇaviśeṣānusmṛtiḥ | tadanusmṛtāv atrānvayasāmarthyād eva karaṇaviśeṣapratipattir iti na viśeṣaviruddhāvakāśaḥ |
 eteṣāṃ pakṣānāṃ yuktāyuktatvaṃ sūrayo vicārayiṣyantīti nāsmākam atra nirbandhaḥ; sarvathā viśeṣaviruddhasyādūṣaṇatvam asmābhiḥ pratipādyate tadviruddhalakṣaṇaparyālocanayā | prasaktānāṃ ca viśeṣāṇāṃ pramāṇāntarabādhayā, anvayavyatirekimūlakevalavyatirekibalād vā, pakṣadharmatvasāmarthyena vā, kāryaviśeṣasya kāraṇaviśeṣānvitatvena vā nātra prayatyate; sarvathā prastutahetau na vyāptyasiddhiḥ |
 98
 prasaktānāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ pramāṇāntarabādhayā viśeṣaviruddhatānavakāśa ity uktaṃ tatra katamasya prasaktasya viśeṣasya kena pramāṇena nirākṛtiḥ | śarīrasambandhasya tāvad vyāptyabhāvena, śarīrāntararahitasyāpy ātmanaḥ svaśarīradhāraṇapareṇakriyāsu yathā | athātmanaḥ prayatnavattvād dhāraṇādikriyāsu śarīrādyādhārāsu kartṛtvaṃ yuktam neśvarasya, tadrahitatvāt; tathā ca bhavatāṃ mukhyaṃ kartṛlakṣaṇam -
 jñānacikīrṣāprayatnānāṃ samavāyaḥ kartṛtā [   ] iti |
 keneśvarasya tadrahitatvāt prayatnapratiṣedhaḥ kṛtaḥ | ātmamanaḥsaṃyogajanyatvāt prayatnasya īśvarasya tadasambhavāt kāraṇābhāvāt tanniṣedhaḥ | buddhis tarhīśvare kathaṃ tasyā api manaḥsaṃyogajanyataiva ? sāpi mā bhūt kā naḥ kṣatiḥ ? nanu tadasattaiva na tv anyā kācit | sāpi bhavatu, tadabhāve kasya niśeṣaḥ śarīrādisaṃyogalakṣaṇaḥ sādhyate ? ata evānyair uktam -
 nātīndriyārthapratiṣedho viśeṣasya kasyacit sādhanena nirākaraṇena vā kāryaḥ – tadabhāve viśeṣasādhanasyas tannirākaraṇahetor vāśrayāsiddhatvāt – kintv atīndriyam artham abhyupagacchaṃs tatsiddhau pramāṇam praṣṭavyaḥ | sa cet tatsiddhau prayogakaṃ hetuṃ darśayati om iti kṛtvāsau pratipattavyaḥ | atha na darśayati pramāṇābhāvād evāsau nāsti, na tu viśeṣābhāvat [   ] tasmād jñānacikīrṣāprayatnānāṃ samavāyo 'stīśvare, te tu jñānādayo 'smadādijñānādibhyo vilakṣaṇāḥ, vailakṣaṇyaṃ ca nityatvādidharmayogāt | tan neśvaraśarīrasya kartṛviśeṣasya vyāptyabhāvāt siddhiḥ |
 nāpy asarvajñatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ kulālādiṣu dṛṣṭas tatra sādhyate, tatsiddāv api viśeṣaviruddhasya vyāptyabhāva eva; na hy asarvavidā kartrā kulālādinā kiñcit kāryaṃ kriyate | nanu kulālādeḥ sarvavittve cedānīṃ kaścid asarvavit, evam eva – yad yaḥ karoti sa tasyopādānādikāraṇakalāpam prayojanaṃ ca jānāti; anyathā tatkriyāyogāt | sarvajñatvaṃ ca prakṛtakāryatannimittāpekṣam, ataḥ kulālādir yathā kartā svakāryasya sarvaṃ jānāty upādānādi evam īśvare 'pi sarvakartā sarvasya karaṇaprayojanam vivādaviṣayasya sarvasyopādānakāraṇādi ca kartṛtvād eva jānāti; ataḥ katham asāv asarvavit ?
 anye tv āhuḥ – kṣetrajñānāṃ niyatārthaviṣayagrahaṇaṃ sarvavidadhiṣṭhitānām. yathā pratiniyataśabdādiviṣayagrāhakāṇām indriyāṇām aniyataviṣayasarvavidadhiṣṭhitānāṃ jīvaccharīre | tathā ca indriyavṛttyucchedalakṣanaṃ kecid maraṇam āhuś cetanānadhiṣṭhitānām | asti ca kṣetrajñānāṃ pratiniyataviṣayagrahaṇam tenāpy aniyataviṣayasarvavidadhiṣṭhitena bhāvyam | yo 'sau kṣetrajñādhiṣṭhāyako 'nityataviṣayaḥ sa sasrvavid īśvaraḥ | nanv evaṃ tasyaiva sakalakṣetrajñādhiṣṭhāyakatvāt kim antargaḍusthānīyaiḥ kṣetrajñaiḥ kṛtyam ? na kiñcit pramāṇasiddhatāṃ muktvā | nanv evam aniṣṭhā – yathendriyādhiṣṭāyakaḥ kṣetrajñas tadadhiṣṭhāyakaś ceśvaraḥ – evam anyo 'pi tadadhiṣṭhāyako 'stu. bhavatv aniṣṭhā yadi tatsādhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ kiñcid asti; na tv aniṣṭhāsādhakaṃ kiñcit pramāṇam utpaśyāmaḥ tāvat evānumānasiddatvāt |
 āgamo 'py asmin vastuni vidyate – tathā ca bhagavān vyāsaḥ -
 dvāv imau puruṣau loke kṣaraś cākṣara eva ca |
 kṣaraḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni kūṭastho 'kṣara ucyate ||
 uttamaḥ puruṣas tv anyaḥ paramātmety udāhṛtaḥ |
 yo lokatrayam āviśya bibharty avyaya īśvaraḥ || [BhG 15.16f.]
 tathā śrutiś ca tatpratipādikā upalabhyate -
 < NOTEREF _Ref400347598 1viśvataś cakṣur uta viśvato mukho viśvato bāhur uta viśvatas pāt |
 saṃ bāhubhyāṃ dhamati saṃ patatrair dyāvābhumī janayan deva eka āste [ŚvetU 3.3] ityādi |
 99
 na ca svarūpapratipādakānām aprāmāṇyam, pramānajanakatvasya sadbhāvāt | tathā hi – pramājanakatvena pramāṇasya prāmāṇyaṃ na pravṛttijanakatvena tac cehāsty eva | pravṛttinirvṛttī tu puruṣasya sukhaduḥkhasādhanatvādhyavasāye samarthasyārthitvād bhavata iti | atha vidhāv aṅgatvād amīṣāṃ prāmāṇyaṃ na svarūpārthatvād iti cet, tad asat; svārthapratipādakatvena vidhyaṅgatvāt  tathā hi – stuteḥ svārthapratipādakatvena pravartakatvam, nindāyās tu nivartakatvam iti | anyathā hi tadarthāparijñāne vihitapratiṣiddheṣv aviśeṣeṇa pravṛttir nivṛttir vā syāt | tathā vidhivākyasyāpi svārthapratipādanadvāreṇaiva puruṣaprerakatvaṃ dṛṣṭam evaṃ svarūpapareṣv api vākyeṣu syāt, vākyasvarūpatāyā aviśeṣāt viśeṣahetoś cābhāvād iti | tathā, svarūpārthānāṃ aprāmāṇye medhyā āpaḥ, darbhāḥ pavitram, amedhyam aśuci ity evaṃ svarūpāparijñāne vidhyaṅgatāyām apy aviśeṣeṇa pravṛttinivṛttiprasaṅgaḥ, na caitad asti; medhyeṣv eva pravartate amedhyeṣu ca nivartata ity upalambhāt | tad evaṃ svarūpārthebhyo vākyebhyo 'rthasvarūpāvabodhe sati iṣṭe pravṛttidarśanāt aniṣṭe ca nivṛtter iti jñāyate – svarūpārthānāṃ pramājanakatvena pravṛttau nivṛttau vā vidhisahakāritvam iti, aparijñānāt tu pravṛttāv atiprasaṅgaḥ | atha dvarūpārthānāṃ prāmāṇye grāvāṇaḥ plavante ity evamādīnām api yathārthatā syāt, na; mukhye bādhakopapatteḥ | yatra hi mukhye bādhakaṃ pramāṇam asti tatropacārakalpanā, tadabhāve tu prāmāṇyam eva | na ceśvarasadbhāvapratipādaneṣu kiñcid asti bādhakam iti> svarūpe prāmāṇyam abhyupagantavyam ity āgamād api siddhaprāmāṇyāt tadavagamaḥ | īśvarasya ca sattāmātreṇa svaviṣayagrahaṇapravṛttānāṃ kṣetrajñānām adhiṣṭāyakatā yathā sphaṭikādīnām upadhānākaragrahaṇapravṛttāṇāṃ savitṛprakāśaḥ | yathā teṣāṃ sāvitraṃ prakāśam vinā nopadhānākāragrahaṇasāmarthyaṃ tatheśvaraṃ vinā kṣetravidāṃ na svaviṣayagrahaṇasāmarthyam ity asti bhagavaṇ īśvaraḥ sarvavit | [   ]
 itaś cāsau sarvavit – jñānasya sannihitasadarthaprakāśakatvam nāma svabhāvah, tasyānyathābhāvaḥ kutaścid doṣasadbhāvāt | etat tāvad rūpaṃ cakṣurādyāśrayāṇāṃ jñānānām | yat punaś cakṣuranāśritaṃ na ca rāgādimalāvṛtaṃ tasya viṣayaprakāśanasvabhāvasya viṣayeṣu kim iti prakāśanasāmarthyavighātaḥ yathā dīpāder pavarakāntargatasysa ? nanu rāgāder āvaraṇasya kathaṃ tatrābhāvo 'vagataḥ tatpratipādakapramāṇābhāvāt ? pramāṇasyābhāve saṃśayo 'stu rāgādīnām na tv abhāvaḥ | viparyāsakāraṇā rāgādayaḥ, eṣāṃ kāraṇābhāve kathaṃ tatra bhāvaḥ ? viparyāsaś cādharmanimittaḥ, na ca bhagavaty adharmaḥ, tatsadbhāve vā itthaṃvidhasyāsmadādibhiś cintayitum apy aśakyasya kāryasya kathaṃ tasmād utpādaḥ anekādṛṣṭakalpanāprasaṅgāt ? kiñca, rāgādaya iṣṭāniṣṭasādhaneṣu viṣayeṣūpajāyamānā dṛṣṭāḥ | na ca bhagavataḥ kaścid iṣṭāniṣṭasādhano doṣayaḥ, avāptakāmatvāt |
 yā tu pravṛttiḥ śarīrādisarge sā kaiścit krīḍārtham uktā; sā cāvaptaprayojanānām eva bhavati na tv anyeṣām | ato yad uktaṃ vārttikakṛtā -
 kṛīḍā hi ratim avindatām; na ca ratyarthī bhagavān, duḥkhābhāvāt [NV 462]
 tat pratikṣiptam | na hi duḥkhitāḥ krīḍāsu pravartante, tasmāt krīḍārthā pravṛttiḥ |
 anye manyante – kārūṇyād bhagavataḥ pravṛttiḥ | nanv evaṃ kevalaḥ sukharūpaḥ prāṇisargo 'stu, naivam; nirapekṣasya kartṛtve 'yaṃ doṣaḥ, sāpekṣatve tu katham ekarūpaḥ sargaḥ ? yasya yathāvidhaḥ karmāśayaḥ puṇyarūpo 'puṇyarūpo vā tasya tathāvidhaphalopabhogāya tatsādhanān śarīrādīṃs tathāvidhāṃs tatsāpekṣaḥ sṛjati [   ] iti | na ceśvaratvavyāghātaḥ sāpekṣatve 'pi, yathā savitṛprakāśasya sphaṭikādyapekṣasya, yathā nā karaṇādhiṣṭāyakasya kṣetrajñasya, sāpekṣatve 'pi teṣu tasyeśvaratā tadvad atrāpi neśvaratāvisaṃ ghātaḥ [   ] iti kecit |
 anye manyante – yathā prabhuḥ sevābhedānurodhena phalabhedaprado nāprabhus tatheśvaro 'pi karmāśayāpekṣaḥ phalaṃ janayatīti anīśvaraḥ iti na yujyate vaktum [   ]
 bhāṣyakāraḥ kārūṇyapreritasya pravṛttim āha | tannimittāyām api pravṛttau na vārttikakārīyaṃ dūṣaṇam -
 100
 saṃsṛjeta śubham evaikam anukampāprayojitaḥ [ŚV sambandhā. 52] ity evamādi, yataḥ karmāśayānāṃ kuśalākuśalarūpānāṃ phalopabhogaṃ vinā na kṣaya iti bhagavān anagacchaṃs tadupabhogāya prāṇisargaṃ karoti | upabhohgaḥ karmaphalasya śarīrādikṛtaḥ, kasyacit tu aśubhasya karmaṇaḥ prāyaścittāt prakṣayaḥ | tatrāpi svalpena duḥkhopabhogena dīrghakāladuḥkhapradaṃ karma kṣīyate, na tu phalam adattvā karmakṣayah | yeṣām api mataṃ samyagjñānād viparyāsanivṛttau tajjanyakleśakṣaye karmāśayānāṃ sadbhāve 'pi sahakāryabhāvān na śarīrādyākṣepakatā, tatrāpi kuśalaṃ karma samādhiṃ nāntareṇa na tattvajñānotpattiḥ; tayos tu sañcaye pravṛttasya yamaniyamānuṣṭhāne 'nekavidhaduḥkhotpattiḥ, ataḥ kathaṃ kevalasukhirūpaḥ prāṇisargaḥ ? nārakatiryagādisargo 'pi akṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ tatra atyaduḥkhānubhave punar viśiṣṭasthānāvāptāv abhyudayahetur iti siddhaṃ duḥkhiprāṇisṛṣṭāv api karuṇayā pravartanam | tan nāsarvajñatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ |
 nāpi kṛtrimajñānasambandhitvam, tajjñānasya pratyarthaniyamābhāvāt | yad jñānam anityaṃ tat śarīrādisāpekṣaṃ pratyarthaniyatam tajjjānasya tu śasrīrādyabhāve kutaḥ pratyarthaniyatatā ? bhavatu tajjñānaṃ pratiniyataviṣayam na tasya pratiniyataviṣayave 'smākaṃ pakṣakṣatiḥ | kathaṃ na kṣatiḥ ? tasya tathāvidhatve yugapatsthāvarānutpādaprasaṅgaḥ, tadanutpāde ca kartṛtvāsiddhiḥ, tadasiddhau kasya kṛtrimajjānasambandhitāviśeṣaḥ ? atha yugapatkāryānyathānupapattyā pratyarthaniyatām anekāṃ buddhim īśvare pratipadyeta tatrāpi santānena vā tathābhūtā buddhayaḥ, yugapad vā bhaveyuḥ ? prācye vikalpe punar api yugapatkāryānutpādaprasaṅgaḥ | yugapadutpattau vā buddhīnāṃ śarīrādiyogas tasyaiṣitavyah , sa ca pūrvaṃ pratikṣiptaḥ | atha kāryasya bahutvamahattvābhyām bahavo buddhimantaḥ kartāro bhavantu, na tv ekaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvaśaktiyuktaḥ; nanv etasminn api pakṣe īśvarānekatvaprasaṅgaḥ, bhavatu, ko doṣaḥ ? vyāhatakāmānāṃ svatantrāṇām ekasminn arthe 'pravṛttiḥ | atha tanmadhye 'nyeṣām ekāyattatā tadā sa eveśvaraḥ, anye punas tadadhīnā anīśvarāḥ | atha sthapatyādīnāṃ mahāprasādādikaraṇe yathaikamatyaṃ tadvad atrāpi, naitad evam; tatra kasyacid abhiprāyeṇa niyamitānām aikamatyam; na tv atra bahūnāṃ niyāmakaḥ kaścid asti | sadbhāve vā sa eveśvaraḥ | evaṃ yasya yasya viśeṣasya sādhanāya vā nirākṛtaye vā pramānam ucyate tasya tasya pūrvoktena nyāyena nirākaraṇaṃ kartavyam | tan na viśeṣaviruddhatā īśvarasādhakasya |
 prasaṅgaviparyayayor apy anutpattiḥ | prasaṅgasya vyāptyabhāvāt, tanmūlatvāt tadviparyayasya; tatheṣṭavihātakṛtaś ca | yac ca nityatvād akartṛkatvam ucyate śākyais tad api kṣaṇabhaṅgabhaṅge pratikṣiptam | yad api vyāpāraṃ vinā na kartṛtvaṃ tad api jñānacikīrṣāprayatnalakṣaṇasya vyāpārasyoktatvān nirākṛtam |
 vārttikakāreṇāparaṃ pramāṇadvayam upanyastaṃ tatsiddhaye -
 mahābhūtādivyaktaṃ cetanādhiṣṭhitaṃ prāṇināṃ sukhaduḥkhanimittam, rūpādimattvāt, turyādivat | tathā, pṛthivyādīni mahābhūtāni buddhimatkāraṇādhiṣṭhitāni svāsu dhāraṇādyāsu kriyāsu pravartante, anityatvāt, vāsyādivat [NV 467] iti |   {zur folgenden Stelle cf. TSP 52,16ff}
 aviddhakarṇas tu tatsiddhaye idaṃ pramāṇadvayam āha -
 dvīndriyagrāhyāgrāhyaṃ vimatyadhikaraṇabhāvāpannaṃ buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakam, svārambhakāvayavasanniveśaviśiṣṭatvāt, ghaṭādivat; vaidhyarmyeṇa paramāṇavaḥ [   ] iti | tatra dvābhyāṃ darśanasparśanendriyābhyāṃ grāhyaṃ mahadanekadravyavattvarūpādyupalabdhikāraṇopetaṃ pṛthivyudakajvalanasaṃjñakaṃ trividhaṃ dravyaṃ dvīndriyagrāhyam | agrāhyaṃ vāyvādi, yasmād mahattvam anekadravyavattvaṇ rūpasamavāyādiś copalabdhikāraṇam iṣyate, tac ca vāyyādau nāsti | yathoktam -
  mahatyanekadravyavattvād rūpāc copalabdhiḥ  [VS 4.1.6.]
  rūpasaṃskārābhāvād vāyāv anupalabdhiḥ [VS 4.1.7] 
 rūpasaṃskāro rūpasamavāyaḥ, dvyaṇukādīnāṃ tv anupalabdhir amahattvād iti | anye tu vāyor api sparśanendriyapratyakṣagrāhyatvam icchanti |
 101
 dvīndriyagrāhyatvāpekṣayā tu rūpasamavāyābhāvād anupalabdhir ity uktam | tatra sāmānyena dvīndriyagrāhyāgrāhyasya buddhimatkāranapūrvakatvasādhane siddhasādhyatādoṣah, ghaṭādiṣu ubhayasiddher vivādābhāvāt; abhyupetabādhā ca, aṇvākāśādīnāṃ tathānabhyupagamāt, teṣāṃ ca nityatvāt pratyakṣādibādhā | tatas tadarthaṃ vimatyadhikaraṇabhāvāpannagrahaṇam | vividhā matir vimatir vipratipattir iti yāvat, tasyā adhikaraṇabhāvāpannaṃ vivādaspadībhūtam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati śarīrendriyabhuvanādaya evātra pakṣīkṛtā iti nāṇvādiprasaṅgaḥ | kāraṇamātrapūrvakatve 'pi sādhye siddhasādhyatā ma bhūd iti buddhimatkāraṇagrahaṇam | sāṅkhyam prati matubarthānupapatter na siddhasādhyatā avyatiriktā hi buddhiḥ pradhānāt sāṅkhyair ucyate, na ca tenaiva tad eva tadvad bhavati | svārambhakāṇām avayavānāṃ sanniveśaḥ – pracayātmakaḥ saṃyogaḥ – tena viśiṣṭaṃ vyavacchinnaṃ tadbhāvas tasmāt | avayavasanniveśaviśiṣṭatvaṃ gotvādibhir vyabicārīty ataḥ svārambhakagrahaṇam | gotvādīni tu dravyārambhakāvayavasanniveśena viśiṣyante na tu svārambhakāvayavasanniveśeneti | tena yo 'sau buddhimān sa īśvara ity ekam | dvitīyaṃ tu tanubhuvanakaraṇopādānāni cetanācetanāni cetanādhiṣṭhitāni svakārayam arabhante iti pratijānīmahe, rūpādimattvāt, yad yad rūpādimat tat tat cetanādhiṣṭhitaṃ svakāryam ārabhate | yo 'sau cetanas tanubhuvanakaraṇopādānāder adhiṣṭātā sa bhagavān īśvara iti |
 uddyotakaras tu pramāṇayati -
 bhuvanahetavaḥ pradhānaparamāṇvadṛṣṭāḥ svakāryotpattāv atiśayabuddhimantam adhiṣṭhātāram apekṣante; sthitvā pravṛtteḥ, tantuturyādivat [NV 457] iti |
 praśastamatis tv āha -
 sargādau puruṣānāṃ vyavahāro 'nyopadeśapūrvakaḥ, uttarakālaṃ prabuddhānāṃ pratyarthaniyatatvāt, aprasiddhavāgvyavahārāṇāṃ kumārāṇām gavādiṣu pratyarthaniyato vāgvyāvahāro yathā mātrādyupadeśapūrvakaḥ [   ] iti | prabuddhānāṃ pratyarthaniyatatvād iti prabuddhānāṃ satāṃ pratyarthaṃ niyatatvād ity arthaḥ | yad upadeśapūrvakaś ca sa sargādau vyavahāraḥ sa īśvaraḥ pralayakālo 'py aluptajñānātiśaya iti siddham |
 tathāparāṇy apy uddyotakareṇa tatsiddhaye sādhanāny upanyastāni -
 buddhimatkāraṇādhiṣṭhitaṃ mahābhūtādikaṃ vyaktaṃ sukhaduḥkhanimittaṃ bhavati, acetanatvāt, kāryatvāt, vināśitavāt, rupādimattvāt, vāsyādivat [NV 459] iti |
 atha bhavatv asmād dhetukadambakād īśvarasysa sarvajagaddhetutvasiddhiḥ sarvajñatvaṃ tu kathaṃ tasya siddham yenāsau niḥśreyasābhyudayakāmānāṃ bhaktiviṣayatāṃ yāyāt? jagatkartṛtvasiddher veti brūmaḥ | tathā cāhuḥ praśastamatiprabhṛtayaḥ -
 kartuḥ kāryopādānopakaraṇaprayojanasampradānaparijñānāt [   ] iha hi yo yasya kartā bhavati sa tasyopādānādīni jānīte, yathā kulālaḥ kuṇḍādīnāṃ kartā, tadupādānaṃ mṛtpiṇḍam, upakaraṇāni cakrādīni, prayojanam udakāharaṇādi, kuṭumbinaṃ ca sampradānaṃ jānīta ity etat siddham; tatheśvaraḥ sakalabhuvanānāṃ kartā, sa tadupādāni paramāṇvādilakṣanāni, tadupakaranāni dharmadikkālādīni, vyavahāropakaraṇāni sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyalakṣāṇāni, prayojanam upabhogam, sampradānasaṃjñakāṃś ca pururṣān jānīta iti; ataḥ siddham asya sarvajñatvam iti | ata eva nātraitat preraṇīyam – sarvajñapūrvakatve  kṣityādīnāṃ sādhye sādhyavikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ, hetuś ca viruddhaḥ, asarvajñakartṛpūrvakatvena kumbhādeḥ kāryasya vyāptidarśanāt, kiñcijjñapūrvakatve sādhye 'bhyupetabādhā, kāraṇamātrapūrvakatve sādhye karmaṇā siddhasādhanam iti, yataḥ sāmānyena svakāryopādānopakaraṇasampradānābhijñakartṛpūrvakatvaṃ sādhyate tatra cāsty eva vastrādidṛṣṭāntaḥ | tasya hy upādānopakaraṇādyabhijñakartṛpūrvakatvaṃ sakalalokaprasiddhaṃ katham anyathā kartuṃ śakyate apahnotuṃ vā ?
 102
 na tu karmaṇā siddhasādhyatā, tasya sakalajagallakṣaṇakāryopādānādyanabhijñatvāt; tadabhijñatve vā tasyaiva bhagavataḥ karma iti nāmāntaraṃ kṛtaṃ syāt | śeṣaṃ tv atra cintitam eva | tad evaṃ sakaladoṣarahitād uktahetukalāpād jñānādyatiśayavadguṇayuktasya siddheḥ tasya ca śāsanapraṇetṛtvaṃ nānyeṣaṃ yoginām iti bhavajinānāṃ śāsanam iti ayuktam uktam iti sthitam iti pūrvapakṣaḥ ||
 
 [uttarapakṣaḥ – jagataḥ īśvarakṛtatvanirākaraṇam]
 
 atra pratividhīyate – yat tāvad uktam – sāmānyatodṛṣṭānumānasya tatra vyāpārābhyupagamāt pratyakṣapūrvakānumānaniṣedhe siddhasādhanam iti, tad asaṅgatam; sāmānyatodṛṣṭānumānasyāpi tatsādhakatvenāpravṛtteḥ | tathā hi – tanubhuvanakaraṇādikaṃ buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakam, kāryatvāt, ghaṭādivat ity atra dharmyasiddher āśrayāsiddhas tāvat kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetuḥ |
 {tathā hi – avayavirūpaṃ tāvat tanvādi avabhāsamānatanu na yuktam; deśādibhinnasya tanvādeḥ sthūlasyaikasyānupapatteḥ | na hy anekadeśādigatam ekaṃ bhavituṃ yuktam, viruddhadharmādhyāsasya bhekalakṣaṇatvāt – deśādibhedaya ca viruddhadharmarūpatvāt | tathāpy abhede sarvatra bhinnatvenābhyupagate ghaṭapaṭādāv api bhedoparatiprasaṅgāt; na hi binnatvenābhyupagate tatrāpy anyad bhedanibandhanam utpaśyāmaḥ | pratibhāsabhedāt tatra bheda iti cet, na; viruddhadharmādhyāsaṃ bhedakam antareṇa pratibhāsasyāpi bhedānupapatteḥ |
 atha avayavī eko na bhavati, viruddhadharmādhyāsitatvāt ity etat kiṃ svatantrasādhanam, uta prasaṅgasādhanam iti | na tāvat svatantrasādhanaṃ yuktam, avayavinaḥ pramāṇāsiddhatvena hetor āśrayāsiddhatvadoṣāt; pramāṇasiddhatve vā tatpratipādakapramāṇabādhitapakṣanirdeśānantaraprayuktatvena tasya kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvadoṣaduṣṭatvāt | na ca parasyāvayavī siddha iti nāśrayāsiddhatvadoṣa iti vaktuṃ yuktam, yataḥ parasya kiṃ pramāṇato 'sau siddhaḥ , utāpramāṇataḥ ? pramāṇataś cet tarhi bhavato 'pi kiṃ na siddhaḥ pramāṇasiddhasya sarvān pratyaviśeṣāt ? tathā ca tad eva kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvaṃ hetoḥ | athāpramāṇatas tadā na parasyāpi siddha iti punar apy āśrayāsiddhatvam | tan na prathamaḥ pakṣaḥ | nāpi dvitīyaḥ, yataḥ vyāpyābhyupagamo yatra vyāpakābhyupagamanāntarīyakaḥ pradarśyate tat prasaṅgasādhanam | na ca parasya bhedaviruddhadharmādhyāsayor vyāpyavyāpakabhāvaḥ siddhaḥ, deṣādibhedalakṣaṇaviruddhadharmādhyāsābhāve 'pi rūparasayor bhedābhyupagamāt | tadbhāve 'pi sāmānyādāv abhedasya pramānasiddhatvād iti na vaktavyam |
 yataḥ prathamaḥ pakṣas tāvad anabhyupagamād eva nirastaḥ | prasaṅgasādhanapakṣe tu yad dūṣaṇam abhihitam – deśabhedalakṣaṇaviruddhadharmādhyāsābhāve 'pi rūparasayor bhedaḥ iti, tad vyāpyavyāpakabhāvāparijñānaṃ sūcayati na punar vyāpyavyāpakabhāvābhāvam, yato deśabhede sati yady abhedah kaścit siddhaḥ syāt tadā vyāpakābhāve 'pi viruddhadharmādhyāsasya bhāvān na tasya tena vyāptiḥ syāt; yadā tu deśābhede 'pi rūparasayor bhedas tadā deśabhedo bhedavyāpako na syāt | na punar etāvatā bhedo viruddhadharmādhyāsavyāpako na syāt | yadi hi bhedavyāvṛttāv api deśādibhedo na vyāvarteta tadā vyāpakavyāvṛttāv api vyāpyasyāvyāvṛttena bhedena deśādiviruddhadharmādhyāso vyāpyeta; na caitat kvacid api siddham | yat tu sāmāṇyādāv abhedasya pramāṇataḥ siddher bhedavyāvṛttāv api na deṣādibhedalakṣaṇasya viruddhadharmādhyāsasya nivṛttiḥ iti, tad ayuktam; sāmānyādeḥ pramāṇato 'bhinnarūpasyāsiddheḥ | uktaṃ ca -
 yadi viruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ padārthānāṃ bhedako na syāt tadānyasya tadbhedakasyābhāvād viśvam ekaṃ syāt [   ] pratibhāsabhedasyāpi tam antareṇa bhedavyavasthāpakasyābhāvād iti vyāpyavyāpakabhāvasiddheḥ kathaṃ na prasaṅgasādhanasyātrāvakāśaḥ ?
 athaikatvapratibhāsād deśādibhede 'pi tanvāder ekatā, na; deśabhedena vyavasthitānām avayavānāṃ pratibhāsabhedena bhedāt; na hy ekarūpā bhāgā bhāsante, piṇḍasyāṇumātratāpatteḥ: tadvyatiriktasya cāparasya tanvādyavayavino dravyasyopalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasyānupalambhenāsattvāt | na ca tasyopalabdhilakṣanaprāptatā parair nābhyupagamyate |
  mahatyanekadravyavattvāt rūpāc copalabdhiḥ [VS 416]
 iti vacanāṭ | tat siddam anupalabdheḥ upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasya iti viśeṣaṇam | na ca madhyordhvādibhāgavyatiriktavapurbahirgrāhyākāratāṃ bibhrāṇas tanvādir dravyātmā darśane cakāstīty anupalabdhir api siddhā | na ca samānadeśatvād avayavino 'vayavebhyaḥ pṛthaganupalakṣaṇam iti vaktuṃ śakyam, samānadeśatvād iti vikalpānupapatteḥ | tathā hi – samānadeśatvam avayavāvayavinoḥ kiṃ pāribhāṣikam, laukikaṃ vā ? yadi  pāribhāṣikam, tadanudghoṣyam; paribhāṣāyā atrānadhikārāt | na ca tat tatra bhavadabhiprāyeṇa siddham | tathā hi – anya eva pāṇyādaya ārambhakā diśās tanvādyavayavino bhavadbhiḥ paribhāṣyante, anye ca pāṇyādīnāṃ tadavayavānām ārambhakā deśāḥ, ārabhyārambhakavādaniṣedhāt | tan na pāribhāṣikaṃ samānadeśatvam | nāpi laukikam, ākāśasysa lokaprasiddhasya samānadeśasyāsmān prati asiddhatvāt | prakāśadirūpasya ca deśasysa tatsiddhasya samānatve 'pi bhinnānāṃ vātātapādīnāṃ bhedenopalabdheḥ | tathā hi – samānadeśā api bhāvā vātātapādayo bhinnatnvavaḥ pṛthak prathante; na caivam avayavinirbhāsaḥ | tan nāvayavī tanvādir bhinno 'sti |
 atha mandamandaprakāśe avayavapratibhāsam antareṇāpy avayavini pratibhāsa upalabhyate tat kathaṃ pratibhāsābhāvāt tasyābhāvah ? asad etat; na hi tathābhūto 'spaṣṭapratibhāso 'vayavisvarūpavyavasthāpako yuktaḥ, tatpratibhāsasyāspaṣṭarūpasya spaṣṭajñānāvabhāsitatsvarūpeṇa virodhāt | atha svasrūpadvayam etad avayavinaḥ – spaṣṭam aspaṣṭaṃ ca | tatrāspaṣṭaṃ mandālokajñānaviṣayaḥ; spaṣṭaṃ tu sālokajñānabhūmiḥ | nanv etat svarūpadvayaṃ kenāvayavino gṛhyate ? na tāvad mandālokajñānena, tatra sālokajñānaviṣayaspaṣṭarūpānavabhāsanāt; aspaṣṭatatsvarūpapratibhāsaṃ hi tad anubhūyate | nāpi sālokajñānena spaṣṭatatsvarūpāvabhāsinā, tatra mandālokajñānāvabhāsitatsvarūpānavabhāsanāt; na hi parisphuṭapratibhāsavelāyām aviśadarūpākāro 'vayavyarthaḥ pratibhāti tat katham asāv avayavinaḥ svarūpam ? atha mandālokadṛṣṭam avayavinaḥ svarūpaṃ parisphuṭam idānīṃ paśyāmi iti tayor ekatā; nanu kim aparisphuṭarūpatayā parisphuṭaṃ rūpam avagamyate, āhosvit parisphuṭatayāparisphuṭām ? tatra yady ādyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tadāparisphuṭarūpasambandhitvam evāvayavinaḥ prāpnoti, parisphuṭasya rūpasyāsphuṭarūpatānupraveśena pratibhāsanāt | atha dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tathāsati spaṣṭasvarūpasambandhitvam eva, aspaṣṭasya viśadasvarūpānupraviṣṭatvena pratibhāsanāt | tan na svarūpadvayāvagamo 'vayavinaḥ | ekatvapratibhāsanaṃ tu pratibhāsarahitam abhimānamātraṃ spaṣṭāspaṣṭarūpayoḥ; anyathā sālokajñānavad mandālokajñānam api parisphuṭapratibhāsaṃ syāṭ | athālokabhāvābhāvakṛtas tatra spaṣṭāspaṣṭapratibhāsabhedaḥ; nanv ālokenāpy avayavisvarūpam evodbhāsanīyam, tac ced avikalaṃ mandāloke pratibhāti kathaṃ na tatra tadavabhāsakṛtaḥ spaṣṭāvabhāsaḥ ? anyathā viṣayā, avabhāsavyatirekeṇāpi jñānapratibhāsabhede na jñānāvabhāsabhedo rūparasayor api bhedavyavasthāpakaḥ syāt | athāvayavisvarūpam ekam evobhayatra pratīyate, vyaktāvyaktākārau tu jñānasyātmanāv ity ucyeta tad apy asat; yato yadi jñānākārau tau katham avayavirūpatayā pratibhātaḥ ? tadrūpatayā ca pratibhāsanād avayavyākārau tāv abhyupagantavyau | na hi vyaktarūpatām avyaktarūpatāṃ ca muktvā avayavisvarūpam aparam ābhāti | tat tasyānavabhāsād abhāva eva | vyaktāvyaktaikātmanaś cāvayavino vyaktāvyaktākāravad bhedaḥ | na hi pratibhāsabhede 'py ekatā, atiprasaṅgāt | tan na aspaṣṭapratibhāsam andhakāre 'vayavino rūpam avayavāpratibhāse 'pi pratibhātīti vaktuṃ yuktam, uktadoṣaprasaṅgāt |
 kiñca, kiṃ katipayāvayavapratibhāsi sati avayavī pratibhātīty abhyupagamyate, āhosvit samastāvayavapratibhāse ? yady ādyaḥ pakṣaḥ, sa na yuktaḥ; jalamagnamahākāyastambhāder uparitanakatipayāvayavapratibhāse 'pi samastāvayavavyāpinaḥ stambhādyavayavino 'pratibhāsanāt | atha dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ, so 'pi na yuktaḥ; madhyaparabhāgavarttisamastāvayavapratibhāsāsambhavenāvayavino 'pratibhāsaprasaṅgāt | atha bhūyovayavagrahaṇe sati avayavī gṛhyata ity abhyupagamaḥ, so 'pi na yuktaḥ; yato 'rvāgbhāgabhāvyavayavagrāhiṇā pratyakṣeṇa parabhāgabhāvyavayavāgrahaṇād na tena tadvyāptir avayavino grahītuṃ śakyā; vyāpyāgrahaṇe tena tadvyāpakatvasyāpi grahītum aśakteḥ; grahaṇe vātiprasaṅgaḥ | tathā hi – yad yena rūpeṇa avabhāti tat tenaiva rūpeṇa sad iti vyavahāraviṣayaḥ -
 104
 yathā nīlaṃ nīlarūpatayā pratibhāsamānaṃ tenaiva rūpeṇa tadviṣayaḥ, arvāgbhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhitayā cāvayavī pratibhātīti svabhāvahetuḥ | na ca parabhāgabhāvivyavahitāvayavāpratibhāsane 'py avyavahito 'vayavī pratibhātīti vaktuṃ śakyam, tad pratibhāsane tadgatatvenāpratibhāsanāt | yasmiṃś ca pratibhāsamāne yad rūpaṃ na pratibhāti, tat tato bhinnam – yathā ghaṭe pratibhāsamāne 'pratibhāsamānaṃ paṭasvarūpam | na pratibhāti ca arvāgbhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhyavayavisvarūpe pratibhāsamāne parabhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhyavayavisvarūpam iti kathaṃ na tat tato bhinnam ? tathāpy abhede 'tiprasaṅgaḥ pratipāditaḥ | nāpi parabhāgabhāvyavayavāvayavigrāhiṇā pratyakṣeṇa arvāgbhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhitvaṃ tasya gṛhyate, tatra tadavayavānāṃ pratibhāsāt tatsambandhy evāvayavisvarūpaṃ pratibhāseta nārvāgbhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhi; teṣāṃ tatrāpratibhāsanāt | tadpratibhāsane ca tatsambandhirūpasyāpy apratibhāsanāt, vyāpyāpratipattau tadvyāpakatvasyāpy apratipatteḥ | nāpi smaraṇena arvākparabhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhyavayavisvarūpagrahaḥ, pratyakṣānusāreṇa samaraṇasya pravṛttyupapatteḥ; pratyakṣasya ca tadgrāhakatvaniṣedhāt | nāpy ātmā arvākparabhāgāvayavavyāpitvam avayavino gragītum samarthaḥ – sattāmātreṇa tasya tadgrāhakatvānupapatteḥ; anyathā svāpamadamūrcchādyavasthāsv api tatpratipattiprasaṅgāt – kintu darśanasahāyaḥ; tac ca darśanaṃ na avayavino 'vayavavyāptigrāhakaṃ pratyakṣādikaṃ sambhavatīti pratipāditam | athārvāgbhāgadarśane saty uttarakālaṃ parabhāgadarśane 'nantarasmaraṇasahakārīndriyajanitaṃ sa evāyam iti pratyabhijñājñānam adhyakṣam avayavinaḥ pūrvāparāvayavavyāptigrāhakam, tad ayuktam; pratyabhijñājñānasyaitadviṣayasya pratyakṣatvānupapatteḥ | akṣānusāri hi pratyakṣam, na cākṣānām arvākparabhāgabhāvyavayavagrahaṇe vyāpāraḥ sambhavati, vyavahite teṣāṃ vyāpārāsambhavāt; sambhave vātivyavahite 'pi merupṛṣṭhādau vyāpāraḥ syāt | tan na tadanusāriṇo 'dhyakṣarūpasya pratyabhijñājñānasya tatra vyāpāraḥ | na ca smaraṇasahāyasyāpīndriyasyāviṣaye vyāpāraḥ sambhavati, yad yasyāviṣayaḥ na tat tara smaraṇasahāyam api pravartate – yathā parimalasmaraṇasahāyam api locanaṃ gandhādau, aviṣayaś ca vyavahito 'kṣāṇāṃ parabhāgabhāvyavayavasambandhitvalakṣaṇo 'vayavinaḥ svabhāva iti nākṣajasya pratyabhijñājñānasyāvayavisvarūpagrāhakatvam | na ca sa evāyam iti pratītir ekā, saḥ iti smṛteḥ rūpam, ayam iti tu dṛśaḥ svarūpam | tat parokṣāparokṣākāratvād naikasvabhāvāv etau pratyayau | atha sa evāyam ity ekādhikāraṇatayā etau pratibhāta iti ekaṃ pratyabhijñājñānam, na; ākārabhede sati darśanasmaraṇayor iva sāmānādhikaraṇyādhyavasāye 'py ekatvānupapatteḥ | anyatrāpy ākārabhede eva bhedaḥ, sa cātrāpi vidyata iti katham ekatvam ? kiñca, saḥ ity eva prāpto na ayam ity api iti katham ekā pratyabhijñā ? atha sa eva ayam ityākārau parasparānanupraviṣṭau pratibhātas tathāpi bhinnākārau bhinnaviṣayau ca dvau pratyayāv iti katham ekārthā ekā pratyabhijñā pratibhāsabhedasya viṣayabhedavyavasthāpakatvāt ? na ca pratibhāsabhede 'pi viṣayābhedaḥ, pratibhāsābhedavyatirekeṇa viṣayābhedavyavasthāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ vinā prameyābhyupagamaḥ syāt; tathā ca sarvaṃ sarvasya sidhyeta | tan na pratyabhijñāto 'py avayavyekatvagrahaḥ | anumānasya ca avayavisvarūpagrāhakasya pratyakṣaniṣedhe tatpūrvakasya niṣedhaḥ kṛta eva | sāmānyatodṛṣṭasya cāvayavipratiṣedhaprastāve niṣedho vidhāsyata ity āstāṃ tāvat |
 atha eko ghaṭaḥ iti dravyapratītir asti tadavayavavyatirekiṇī tat katham abhāvo 'vayavinaḥ ? na; ghaṭāvasāye 'pi tadavayavādhyavasāyaḥ nāmollekhaś cādhyavasīyate nāvayavi dravayam, varṇākṛtyakṣarākāraśūnyasya tadrūpasya kenacid apy ananubhavāt | varṇākṛtyakṣarākāraśūnyaṃ cāvayavisvarūpam abhyupagamyate | na ca tena rūpeṇa kalpanājñāne 'pi tat pratibhāti, na cānyākāraḥ pratibhāso 'nyākārasya vastusvarūpasya vyavasthāpaka; anyathā nīlapratibhāsaḥ pītasya vyavasthāpakaḥ syāt iti na vastuvyavashtā syāt |
 105
 tasmād na kalpanollikhyamānavapur apy anayavī bahir asti, kevalam anādir ayam ekavyavahāro mithyārthaḥ | na ca vyavahāramātrād bahir ekaṃ vastu sidhyati, nīlādīnāṃ svabhāvaḥ ity atrāpi vyavahārābhedād ekatāprāpteḥ svabhāvasya | atha tatra pratinīlādisvabhāvaṃ darśanabhedād ekatvaṃ bādhyate, ihāpi tarhi bahī rūpasyordhvādho – madhyādinirbhāsasya bhedād ekatā tanvādīnām pratidalatu | tan nāvayavirūpo bāhyo 'rtho 'sti |
 athāvayino 'bhāve tadavayavānām api pāṇyādīnāṃ digbhedādiviruddhadharmādhyāsād bhedaḥ, tadavayavānām apy aṅgulyādīnāṃ tata eva bhedāt tāvad bhedo yāvat paramāṇavaḥ, teṣāṃ ca sthūlapratibhāsaviṣayatvānupapattiḥ | stūlatanuś ca bahir nīlādirūpaḥ pratibhāsaḥ sphuṭam udbhāti | na ca sthūlarūpaṃ pratyekaṃ paramāṇuṣu sambhavati, tathātve paramāṇutvāyogāt | nāpi samuditeṣu sthūlarūpasambhavah, samuditāvasthāyām apy aṇūnāṃ svarūpeṇa sūkṣmāt | na ca tadvyatiriktaḥ samudāyo 'sti, tathātve dravyavādaprasaṅgāt; tatra cokto doṣaḥ | tan na sthūlatā paramāṇuṣu kathañcid api sambhavati | na cānyādṛg nirbhāso 'nyādṛkṣasyārthasya prakāśakaḥ, nīladarśanasyāpi pītavyavasthāpakatvāpatteḥ; tathā ca niyataviṣāyavyavasthocchedaḥ | kiñca, paramāṇor api nānādiksambandhād ekatā nopapannaiva | tathā cāha -
 ṣaṭkena yugapadyogāt paramāṇoḥ ṣaḍaṃśatā [   ] iti |
 punas  tadaṃśānām api nānādiksambandhāt sāṃśatāpattiḥ, tathā cānavasthā | tasmān na paramāṇūnām api sattvam ity avayavyagrahaṇe sarvāgrahaṇaprasaṅga iti pratibhāsābhāvāpatter na prasaṅgasādhanasyāvakāśaḥ | asad etad |
 avayavyabhāve 'pi nirantarotpannānāṃ ghaṭādyākāreṇa pramāṇūnaṃ sadbhāvāt tadgrāhakāṇām api jñānaparamāṇūnāṃ tathotpannānāṃ tadgrāhakatvād na bahirarthābhāvaḥ, nāpi tatpratibhāsābhāva iti kathaṃ prasaṅgasādbhavasya nāvakāśaḥ sthūlaikarūpāvayavyabhāve 'pi ? yadi cāvayavino 'bhāve paramāṇūnām apy abhāvaprasakteḥ pratibhāsābhāvena prasaṅgasādhanānavakāśaḥ pratipādyeta tadā sutarāṃ paramāṇurūpasya jñānarūpasya cārthasyābhāvi kāryatvādilakṣanasya hetor āśrayāsiddhatādoṣaḥ |
 bāhyārthanyena cāsmābhir āśrayāsiddhatādoṣāt kāryatvalakṣaṇād dhetor neśvarasiddhir iti pratipādayitum abhipretam, yadi punar vijñānaśūnyavādānukūlaṃ bhavatāpy anuṣṭhīyate tadā sādhyadṛṣṭāntadharmisādhyasādhanadharmādīnām anumityaṅgabhūtānāṃ sarveṣām apy asiddheḥ kuta upavyastaprayogād īśvarasiddhiḥ ? tad evaṃ tanvādilakṣaṇasya kāryatvādihetvāśrayasyāvayavino 'siddher āśrayāsiddho hetuḥ |}
 tathā, buddhimatkāraṇam iti sādhyanirdeśe buddhimat iti matubarthasya sādhyadharmaviśeṣaṇasyānupapattiḥ, tajjñānasya tato vyatireke akāryatve ca tasya iti sambandhānupapatteḥ | tadguṇatvāt tat tasyeti cet, na; akāryatve vyatireke ca tasyaiva tadguṇo nākāśādeḥ iti vyavasthāpayitum aśakteḥ | samavāyo vyavasthākārīti cet, na tasyāpi tābhyām arthāntaratve sa eva doṣaḥ – vyatireke samavāyasyāpi sarvatrānuśeṣān na tato 'pi  tadvyavasthā | atheśvarātmakāryatvād īśvarātmaguṇas tajjñānam, kuta etat ? tasmin sati bhāvād iti cet, ākāśādāv api sati tasya bhāvāt tatkāryatā kiṃ na syāt | atha tadbhāve 'bhāvāt tatkāyatvam, tan na; nityavyāpitvābhyāṃ tasya tadayogāt | tadātmany utkalitasya tasya darśanāt tatkāryateti, kim idaṃ tasya tatrotkalitatvam ? tatra samavetatvaṃ tasyeti cet, nanv idam eva pṛṣṭaṃ kim idam samavetatvaṃ nāma ? tatra samavāyena varttanam iti cet, nanu kiṃ vyāptyā samavāyena tatra varttanam, āhosvid avyāptyā ? yadi vyāptyā tadāsmadādijñānavailakṣaṇyaṃ yathā tajjñānasyādṛṣṭasyāpi kalpyate tathākṛṣṭotpattiṣu vane vanaspatyādiṣu ghaṭādau karmakartṛkaraṇanirvartyaṃ kāryatvam upalabdham api cetanakartṛrahitam api bhaviṣyatīti kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetur buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatve sādhye sthāvarair vyabhicārīti lābham icchato mūlakṣatir āyāteti | atha avyāptyā tat tatra vartate tadā deśāntarotpattimatsu tanvādiṣu tasyāsannidhāne 'pi yathā vyāpāras tathādṛṣṭasyāpy agnyādideśeṣv asannihitasyāpi ūrdhvajvalanādiviṣayo vyāpāro bhaviṣyatīti |
 agner ūrdhvajvalanam, vāyos tiryakpavanam, aṇumanasoś cādyaṃ karmādṛṣṭakāritam [VS 5.2.13]
 ity anena sūtreṇa sarvagatātmasādhakahetusūcanaṃ yat kṛtaṃ tad asaṅgataṃ syāt, jñānādiviśiṣṭaguṇavad adṛṣṭaguṇasya tatrāsannihitasyāpy agnyādyūrdhvajvalanādikāryeṣu vyāpārasambhavāt | na ca sāmānyaguṇaviśeṣaguṇatvalakṣaṇo 'pi viśeṣo guṇaguṇinor bhede sambhavati | kiṃ ca, samavāyasya sarvatrāviśeṣe tatraiva tena varttanaṃ nānyatra iti kuto 'yaṃ vibhāgaḥ ? atha tatrādheyatvaṃ samavetatvaṃ tadā ātmavad gaganāder api sarvagatatve tadātmany eva tadādheyatvaṃ nānyatra iti durlabho 'yam vibhāgaḥ | tatas tajjñānasya tadātmano vyatireke tasyaiva tajjñānam iti sambandhānupapattiḥ |
 atha tatas tajjñānasya bhede 'pi sambandhasya samavāyarūpasya bhāvān nāyaṃ doṣaḥ asad etat; samavāyasyānupapatteḥ |
 
 [prasaṅgavaśāt samavāyinirasanam]
 
 tathā hi – kiṃ satāṃ samavāya, āhosvid asatām iti ? tatra yadi asatām iti pakṣaḥ, sa na yuktaḥ; śaśaviṣāṇavyomotpalādīnām api tatprasaṅgāt | athātyantāsattvāt teṣāṃ na tatprasaṅgaḥ, nanu tadātmatajjñānayor atyantāsattvābhāvaḥ kutaḥ ? tatsamavāyād iti cet, itaretarāśrayatvam – siddhe tatsamavāye tayor atyantāsattvābhāvaḥ, tadabhāvāc ca tatsamavāya iti vaktam itaretarāśrayatvam | atha sattāṃ samavāyaḥ, nanu teṣāṃ samavāyāt prāk kutaḥ sattvam ? yadi aparasamavāyāt, tad asat; tasyaikatvābhyupagamāt | anekatve 'pi yadi aparasamavāyāt prak teṣāṃ sattvam, tatsamavāyād api prāgaparasamavāyāt teṣāṃ sattvam abhyupagantavyam ity anavasthā | atha samavāyāt prāk teṣāṃ svata eva sattvam iti nānavasthā tarhi samavāyavyatirekeṇāpi sattvābhyupagame vyarthaṃ samavāyaparikalpanam iti sattāsambandhdāt padārthānāṃ sattā ity ucyamānaṃ na śobhām āvahati |
 atha samavāyāt prāk padārthānāṃ sattvam nāpy asattvam sattāsamavāyaḥ sattvam, asad etat; yato yadi tatsamavāyāt prāk padārthā yogijñānam api na janyanti tadā kathaṃ teṣāṃ nāsattvam ? atha tad janayanti tadā kathaṃ teṣāṃ na sattvam ? kiṃ ca, anyonyavyavacchedarūpām ekaniṣedhasyāparasadbhāvanāntarīyakatvāt katham asattvaniṣedhe na sattvavidhānam ? tadvidhāne vā kathaṃ nāsattvaniṣedhaḥ ? ity ayuktam uktam udyotakareṇa – gotvasambandhāt prāg na gauḥ, nāpy agauḥ, gotvayogād gauḥ [NV 318, 21] iti | api ca, samavāyād yadi padārthānāṃ sattvaṃ samavāyasya kutaḥ sattvam iti vaktavyam ? yadi aparasamavāyāt, anavasthā | atha svata eva samavāyasya sattvam, padārthānām api tat svata evāstu, punar api vyarthaṃ sattāsamavāyakalpanam | atha yadi nāma samavāyasya svataḥ sattvam iti rūpam katham anyapadārthānām api tad eva rūpam iti sacetasā vaktuṃ yuktam ? na hi lavaṇasya svato lavaṇatve sūpāder api tadvyatirekeṇa tad bhavati, asad etat; yato 'dhyakṣataḥ siddhe padārthasvabhāve yujyeta tad vaktum; na ca samavāyādeḥ svarūpataḥ sattvam anyapadārthānāṃ tu tatsadbhāvāt sattvam ity adhyakṣāt siddham |
 api ca, ayaṃ samavāyaḥ kiṃ samavāyinoḥ parikalpyate, utāsamavāyinor iti vikalpadvayam | tatra yady asamavāyinor iti pakṣaḥ, sa na yuktaḥ; ghaṭapaṭayor atyantabhinnayos tatprasaṅgāt | na cāsamavāyinor bhinnasamavāyena samavāyitvaṃ tad abhinnaṃ vidhātuṃ śakyam, viruddhadharmādhyāsena tābhyāṃ tasya bhedaprasaṅgāt | nāpi bhinnam, tadbhinnasamavāyitvakaraṇe punar api tayor asamavāyitvam; anyonyopakārakaraṇe tv anavasthā | svata eva tu samavāyinoḥ kiṃ samavāyena taddhetunā parikalpitena ? atha samavāyena tayos tadvyavahāraḥ kriyate, nanu yadi samavāyinoḥ svarūpaṃ pratyakṣādipramāṇagocaras tadā tata eva tadvyavahārasyāpi siddher vyartham eva tadarthaṃ tatparikalpitam |
 atha pratyakṣapramāṇasiddhatvāt samavāyasya evaṃ vikalpanam ayuktam; yadi hi tatsiddhatvaṃ tasya syāt tadā yuktam etat, na ca pratyakṣapramāṇe tatsvarūpāvabhāsaḥ -
 107
 na hi tadātmā, jñānam tatsamavāyaś ceti tritayam indriyajādhyakṣagocaraḥ, nāpi svasaṃvedanādhyakṣaviṣayaḥ, tasya bhavatānabhyupagamāt | nāpy ekārthasamavetānantaramano 'dhyakṣaviṣayah, tasya prāg eva niṣiddhatvāt | na ca bāhyeṣv api ghaṭarūpādiṣv artheṣu ayaṃ ghaṭah, ete ca tatsamavetā rūpādayaḥ, ayaṃ ca tadantarālavarttī bhinnaḥ samavāyaḥ iti tritayam adhyakṣapratītau vibhinnasvarūpaṃ pratibhāti | tatpratibhāne vā dravyaguṇasamavāyānām adhyadkṣasiddhatvād vibhinnasvarūpatayā na guṇaguṇibhāve samavāye vā kasyacid vivādaḥ syāt, nāpy ekatvavibhramo ghaṭarūpādīnām, tataś ca  tannirākaraṇārthaṃ śāstrapraṇayanam apārthakaṃ syāt | nanu yathā pratyakṣeṇa pratipanne 'py anekānte jainena, svalakṣaṇe vā bauddhena svadurāgamāhitavāsanābalāl lokasyas tena tadapratipannatāvibhramaḥ tannirākaraṇārthaṃ ca śāstrapraṇayanam tathātrāpi syād iti tarhi tathāvidhaśāstrarahitānām abalābālādīnāṃ na samavāyapratyakṣatāvibhrama iti teṣāṃ śuklaḥ paṭaḥ iti pratītir na syāt, api tu ayaṃ paṭaḥ, ete śuklādayo guṇāḥ, ayaṃ ca tadantarālavarttī aparaḥ samavāyaḥ iti prtītiḥ syāt | atra samavāyasya sūkṣmatvāt pratyakṣatve 'py anupalakṣaṇāt tatrasthatvena rūpādīnām upacārāt śuklaḥ paṭaḥ iti pratipattiḥ syāt, naitat; evaṃ daṇḍe 'pi puruṣaḥ iti pratipattiḥ syāt | upacārāc ceyaṃ pratipattur upajāyamānā skhaladrūpā syād vāhīke gobuddhivat | na ca samavetam idaṃ vastu atra iti pratipattau viśeṣaṇabhūtaḥ samavāyaḥ  pratibhātīti vaktuṃ yuktam, bahiṣpratibhāsamānarūpādivyatirekeṇa antaś cābhijalpam antareṇāparasya varṇākṛtyakṣarākāraśūnyasya grāhyākāratāṃ vibhrāṇasya bahiḥ samavāyasvarūpasyāpratibhāsanāt | varṇādyākārarahitaṃ ca paraiḥ samavāyasvarūpam abhyupagamyate | na ca tatkalpanābuddhāv api pratibhāti | na cānyadṛśaḥ pratibhāso 'nyādṛkṣasyārthasya vyavasthāpakaḥ, atiprasaṅgāt | tan na samavāyo 'dhyakṣapramāṇagocaraḥ |
 yas tv āha – nityānumeyatvāt samavāyasyānumānagocaratā, tenāyam adoṣa iti | tac cānumānam – iha tantuṣu paṭaḥ iti buddhis tantupaṭavyatiriktasambandhapūrvikā, iha iti buddhitvāt, iha kaṃsapātryāṃ jalabuddhivat [   ] ity etat, so 'py ayuktavādī; samavāyasyānyasya vā padārthasya nityaikarūpasya kāraṇatvāsambhavāt kvacid api iti pratipādayiṣyamānatvāt | na ca tantuṣu paṭaḥ, śṛṅge gauḥ, śādhāyāṃ vṛkṣaḥ iti laukikī pratītir asti, paṭe tantavaḥ, gaviśṛṅgam, vṛkṣe śākhā ity ākāreṇa pratītyutpatteḥ saṃvedanāt, tasyāś ca samavāyanibandhanatve tantvādīnāṃ paṭādyārabdhatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
 kiṃ ca, samavāyasya samavāyibhir anabhisambandhe tasya tatra sambaddhabuddhijananaṃ teṣāṃ sambandha eva ca [   ] iti ca na yuktam, na hi daṇḍapuruṣayoḥ saṃyogaḥ sahyavindhyābhyām anabhisambandhyamānas tatra sambandhabuddhihetuḥ tatsambandho vā | tais tadabhisambandhe vā svataḥ dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ svādhāraiḥ svataḥ sambandhaḥ kiṃ na syāt yataḥ samavāyaparikalpanānarthakyam aśnuvīta ? iha samavāyiṣu samavāyaḥ iti ca buddhir aparinimittikā prakṛtasya hetor anaikāntikatvaṃ kathaṃ na sādhayet svatas tatsambandhābhyupaganme | samavāyāntareṇa tasya tadabhisambandhe 'navasthālatā gaganatalāvalambinī prasajyeta | viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvalakṣaṇasambandhabalāt tasya tadabhisambandhe tasyāpi taiḥ sambandho 'paraviśeṣanaviśeṣyabhāvalakṣaṇasambandhabalād yadi saivānavasthā | samavāyabalāt tasya tatsambandhe vyaktam itaretarāśrayatvam | svatas tais tasyābhisambandhe buddhyādīnām api svata eva svādhāraiḥ sambandho bhaviṣyatīti āstāṃ tāvat |
 tad evaṃ buddhes tadātmano vyatireke sambandhāsiddher matubarthānupapattiḥ |
 atha avyatiriktā tadātmanas tadbuddhis tathāpi tadanupapattiḥ, na hi tad eva tenaiva tadvad bhavati kiṃ ca, tadātmanas tadbuddher avyatireke yadi tadātmani tadbuddher anupraveśas tadā buddher abhāvād buddhivikalo gaganādivad jaḍasvasrūpas tadātmā kathaṃ jagatsṛṣṭā syāt ? buddhyādiviśeṣaguṇagaṇavaikalye ca tadātmano 'smadādyātmano 'py ātmatvena tadvaikalyād muktātmana iva saṃsāritvaṃ na syāt, navānāṃ viśeṣaguṇānām ātyantikakṣayopetasyātmano muktatvābhyupagamāt tasya cāsmadādyātmasv api samānatvāt bhavadabhyupagamena |
 108
 atha ātmatvāviśeṣe 'pi tadātmā asmadādyātmabhyo viśiṣṭo 'bhyupagamyate tarhi kāryatvāviśeṣe 'pi ghaṭādikāryebhyaḥ sthāvarādikāryam akartṛkatvena viśiṣṭaṃ kiṃ nābhyupaganyate ? tathā ca na kāryatvādilakṣaṇo hetur anupalabhyamānakartṛkaiḥ sthāvarādibhir avyabicārī syāt | atha tadbuddhau tadātmano 'nupraveśas tadā buddhimātram ādhāraśūnyam abhyupagantavyaṃ abhāti | tathā cāsmadādibuddher api tadvadādhāravikalatvena matubarthāsambhavād ghaṭādāv api buddhimatkāraṇatvasyāsiddhatvāt sādhyavikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ | atha asmadādibuddhibhyo buddhitve samāne 'pi tadbuddher evānāśritatvalakṣaṇo viśeṣo 'bhyupagamyate tarhi ghaṭādikāryebhyaḥ pṛthivyādikāryasya kāryatve samāne 'pi akartṛpūrvakatvalakṣaṇo viśeṣo 'bhyupagantavya iti punar api kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetus tair eva vyabhicārī |
 kiṃ ca, asau tatbuddhiḥ kṣaṇikā, akṣaṇikā vā iti vaktavyam | yadi kṣaṇiketi pakṣah, tadātmānaṃ samavāyikāraṇam, ātmamanaḥsaṃyogaṃ cāsamavāyikāraṇam, taccharīrādikaṃ ca nimittakāraṇam antareṇa kathaṃ dvitīyakṣaṇe tasyā utpattiḥ ? tadanutpattau ca acetanasyāṇvādeś cetanānadhiṣṭhitasya kathaṃ bhūdharādikāryakaraṇe pravṛttiḥ vāsyāder ivācetanasya cetanānadhiṣṭhitasya pravṛttyanabhyupagamāt ? tataś cedānīṃ bhūruhādīnām anutpattiprasaṅgāt kāryaśūnyaṃ jagat syāt | atha samavāyyādikāraṇam antareṇāpi tadbuddher asmadādibuddhivailakṣaṇyād utpattir abhyupagamyate; nanv evam ghaṭādikāryavailakṣaṇyaṃ bhūdharādikāryasya kiṃ nābhyupagamyate iti tad eva codyam | kiṃ ca, yadīśabhuddhiḥ samavāyyādikāraṇanirapekṣaivotpattim āsādayati tarhi muktānām apy ānandādikaṃ śarīrādinimittakāraṇādivyatirekeṇāpy utpatsyate iti na buddhisukhādivikalaṃ jaḍātmasvarūpaṃ muktiḥ syāt |
 atha akṣaṇikā tadbuddhiḥ; nanv evam asmadādibuddhir apy akṣaṇikā kiṃ nābhyupagamyate ? atha pratyakṣādipramāṇavirodhān nāsmadādibuddhir akṣanikā tarhi tadvirodhād eva adṛṣṭotpattiṣu sthāvareṣu kāryatvaṃ buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakaṃ nābhyupagantavyam | athāsmadādibuddheḥ kṣaṇikatvasādhakam anumānam akṣaṇikatvābhyupagamabādhakaṃ pravartate na punar adṛṣṭotpattiṣu sthāvareṣu, kiṃ punas tadanumānam ? atha kṣaṇikā buddhiḥ, asmadādipratyakṣatve sati vibhudravyaviśeṣaguṇatvāt, śabdavad ity etat | nanu yathā asyānumānasyāsmadādibuddhyakṣanikatvābhyupagamabādhakasya sambhavas tathādṛṣṭotpattiṣu sthāvareṣu kartṛpūrvakatvābhyupagamabādhakasya tasya sambhavaḥ pratipādayiṣyata iti nātra vastuni bhavatautsukyam āstheyam | yathā ca buddhikṣaṇikatvānumānasyānekadoṣaduṣṭatvaṃ tathā śabdasya paudgalikatvavicāraṇāyām pratipādayiṣyata ity etad apy āstāṃ tāvat | yathā vā buddhitvāviśeṣe 'pi īśāsmadādibuddhyor ayam aksaṇikatvakṣaṇikatvalakṣaṇo viśeṣas tathā bhūruhaghaṭādikāryayor apy akartṛkartṛpūrvakatvalakṣano viśeṣah kiṃ nābhyupagamyate ? iti punar api tad eva dūṣaṇaṃ kāryatvāder hetor anaikānitkatvalakṣaṇaṃ prakṛtasādhye | } tad evaṃ buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvalakṣaṇe sādhye matubarthāsambhavāt tanvādīnām anekadhā pramāṇavādhāsambhavāc ca śāstravyākhyānādiliṅgānumīyamānapāṇḍityaguṇasya devadattasyeva mūrkhatvalakṣaṇi sādhye 'numānabādhitakarmanirdeśānantaraprayuktasya kāryatvāder hetoḥ kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvena tatputratvāder ivāgamakatvam anumānabādhitatvaṃ vā pakṣasyeti sthitam |
 tathā, kāryatvāt iti hetur apy  asiddhaḥ | {tathā hi – kim idaṃ tanvādīnāṃ kāryatvam – prāg asataḥ svakāraṇasamavāyaḥ sattāsamavāyo veti cet, kutaḥ prāg iti ? kāraṇasamavāyād iti cet; nanu tatsamavāyasamaye prāg iva svarūpasattvavaidhurye prāk iti viśeṣaṇam anarthakam, sati sambhave vyabhicāre ca viśeṣaṇam upādīyamānam arthavad bhavati; atra tu vyabhicāra eva, na sambhavaḥ | tathā hi – yadi kāraṇasamavāyasamaye svarūpiṇa sad bhavati tanvādi tadā tatkāla iva tasya prāg api sattve kāryatvaṃ na syād iti viśeṣaṇam upādīyate prāg asataḥ iti | yadā punaḥ prāg iva kāraṇasamavāyavelāyām api svarūpasattavikalatā tadā prāk iti viśeṣaṇaṃ na kañcid arthaṃ puṣṇāti, asataḥ ity evāstu |
 na cāsataḥ kāraṇasamavāyo 'pi yuktaḥ, śaśaviṣāṇāder api tatprasaṅgāt | tasya kāraṇavirahān na tatprasaṅga iti cet, kuta etat ? asattvāt, tanukaraṇāder api tadvad asattve kiṃ kṛto 'yam vibhāgaḥ – asya kāraṇam asti na śaśaśṛṅgāder iti ? tanvādeḥ kāraṇam upalabhyate netarasyety api nottaram, yataḥ kāryakāraṇayor upalambhe sati etat syāt idam asya kāraṇam kāryaṃ cedam asya iti |
 109
 na ca parasya tadupalambhaḥ pratyakṣataḥ, upalambhakāraṇam upalambhaviṣaya iti naiyāyikānāṃ matam – arthavat pramāṇam [NBh 1.2] ity atra bhāṣye pramātṛprameyābhyām arthāntaram avyapadeśyāvyabhicārivyavasāyātmakajñāne kartavye 'rthaḥ sahakārī vidyate yasya tas arthavat pramāṇam [  ] iti vyākhyānāt | na cājanakaṃ sahakāri saha karotīti sahahakāri iti vyutpatteḥ | na cāsat śaśaviṣāṇasamaṃ jñānasyānyasya vā kāraṇam, virodhāt | api ca, indriyārthasannikarṣāt pratyakṣaṃ jñānam utpattimat, kāryakāraṇādinā ca indriyasannikarṣaḥ saṃyogaḥ, so 'pi kathaṃ tenāsatā janyate iti cintyam | samyogābhāve ca rūpādinendriyasya saṃyuktasamavāyaḥ, rūpatvādinā tu saṃyuktasamavetasamavāya iti sarvaṃ durghaṭām | etena dravyatvādisāmānyasambandho 'pi tasya nirūpitaḥ | tan na tanvādiviṣayam adhyakṣam | ata eva nānumānam api | tad evaṃ kharaviṣānādivat kāryakāraṇāder  anupalambhān na yuktam etatśarīrādeḥ kāraṇam asti, na śaśaśṛṅgāder iti |
 yadi punas tanukaraṇādiḥ san vandhyāsutādiparihāreṇeti matiḥ, tatra kutaḥ sa eva san ? kāraṇasamavāyāt, so 'pi kutaḥ ? sattvāt, anyonyasaṃśrayaḥ – tatsamavāyāt sattvam ataś ca tatsamavāya iti | prāgasataḥ sattāsamavāyāt sa eva sann iti cet, kutaḥ prāk ? sattāsamavāyāt; nanu tatsamavāyakāle prāg iva svarūpasattvavirahe prāg iti viśeṣaṇam anarthakam ityādi sarvaṃ vaktavyam | asataś ca sattāsamavāye kharaśṛṅgāder api sambhavet aviśeṣāt | prāg iti ca viśeṣaṇaṃ śaśaśṛṛṅgādivyavacchedārthaṃ pareṇoktam, sattāsambandhasamaye ca tanvādeḥ svarūpasattvābhāve kas tato viśeṣaḥ ? ayam asti viśeṣaḥ – kūrmaromādikam atyantāsat, itarat punaḥ svayaṃ na sat nāpy asat; ata eva sattāsambhandāt tad eva sat ity ucyata iti, tad etajjaḍātmano bhavataḥ ko 'nyo bhāṣate | tathā hi – na sat iti vacanāt tasya sattāsambandhāt prāgabhāva uktaḥ, satpratiṣedhalakṣaṇatvād asya | nāpy asat ity abhidhānād bhāvaḥ, asattvaniṣedharūpatvād bhāvasya rūpāntarābhāvāt | tathaiva vaiyākaraṇānaṃ nyāyaḥ – dvau pratiṣedhau prakṛtam arthaṃ gamyataḥ [   ] iti, katham anyathā nedaṃ nirātmakaṃ jīvaccharīram, prāṇādimattvāt ity atra nairātmyaniṣedhaḥ sātmakaḥ sidhyet ?
 atra kecit bruvate – naivaṃ prayogaḥ kriyate 'pi tu sātmakaṃ jīvaccharīram, prāṇādimattvāt iti | tair apy evaṃ prayogaṃ kurvadbhiḥ sātmakatvābhāvo niyamena prāṇādimattvābhāvena vyāpto 'bhyupagantavyaḥ – anyathā vyabhicārāśaṅkā na nirvarteta – tadabhyupagame cedam avaśyaṃ vaktavyam – jīvaccharīre prāṇādimattvaṃ pratīyamānaṃ svāhāvaṃ nivartayati, sa ca nivartamānaḥ svavyāpyaṃ sātmakatvābhāvam ādāya nivartate, itarathā tenāsau vyāpto na syāt | yasmin nivartamāne yan na nivartate na tena tad vyāptam, yathā nivartamāne 'pi pradīpe anivartamānaḥ paṭādir na tena vyāptaḥ, na nivartate ca prānādimattvābhāve nivartamāne 'pi sātmakatvābhāva iti | nivartate iti cet, tannivṛttāv api yadi sātmakatvaṃ na sidhyati na tarhi tadabhāvo nivartate, sātmakatvābhāvābhāve 'pi tadabhāvasya tadavasthatvāt | sidhyatīti cet, āyātam idam – dhvau pratiṣidhau prakṛtam arthaṃ gamayataḥ [    ] iti | tathā cedam api yuktam – nedam nirātmakaṃ jīvaccharīram, prāṇādimattvāt iti |
 anye tu manyante – anyatra dṛṣṭo dharmaḥ kvacid dharmiṇi vidhīyate, niṣidhyate ca [   ] iti vacanāt kevalaṃ ghaṭādau nairātmyam aprāṇādimattvena vyāptaṃ dṛṣṭam, tad eva niṣidhyate jīvaccharīre 'pi prāṇādimattvābhāvena, na punaḥ sātmakatvaṃ vidhīyate; tasyānyatrādarśanāt iti | teṣāṃ ghaṭāsau nairātmyaṃ pratipannaṃ pratiṣidhyate iti bhavatu sūktam, tathāpi tanniṣedhasāmarthyād yadi jīvaccharīre sātmakatvaṃ na syāt na tarhi tatra tanniṣedhaḥ – yadā hi nairātmyam ity aparas tanniṣedho mṛgyaḥ, tathā ca sati anavasthānān na nairātmyaniṣedhaḥ | kiṃ ca, yadi nāma ghaṭādau nairātmyam upalabdhaṃ kim ity anyatra niṣidhyate | itarathā devadatte pāṇḍityam upalabdhaṃ yajñadattādau niṣidhyeta | tatra prāṇādimattvadarśanād iti cet, yuktam etad yadi prāṇdimattvaṃ nairātmyaviruddhaṃ syād agnir iva śītaviruddhaḥ, na caivam; anyathā sarvam aśoeaviruddhaṃ bhavet | prāṇādimattvena svābhāvo nairātmyavyāpako viruddhaḥ,
 110
 tataḥ prāṇādimattvabhāvāt tadabhāvo nivartate, vahnibhāvād iva śītam, sa ca nivartamānaḥ svavyāpyaṃ nairātmyam ādāya nivartate, yathā dhūmābhāvaḥ pāvakābhāvam iti | dattam atrottaram – yadi nairātmyābhāvaḥ sātmako na bhavet tadavasthaṃ nairātmyam iti | bhavatu tarhi nairātmyaniṣedhaḥ sātmakaḥ, tathā sati sattāsambandhāt prāk tanvādir nā sat iti vacanāt tadā tasya sattvam uktam, na sat ity abhidhānād asattvam iti virodhaḥ | tato 'sad eva tadabhyupagantavyam iti na vandhyāsutādes tanukaraṇāder viśeṣaḥ | bhavatv evaṃ tathāpi tanvāder eva sattāsambandhāt sattvam na kharaśṛṅgādeḥ tathādarśanād iti cet, uktam atra tathādarśanopāyābhāvād iti |
 
 [prasaṅgikaṃ sattāpadārthanirasanam]
 
 api ca, sattāpi yadi asatī kathaṃ tato vandhyāsutāder ivāparasya sattvam ? satī ced yadi anyasattātaḥ, anavasthā | svataś cet, padārthānām api svata eva sattvaṃ syād iti vyarthaṃ tatparikalpanam | kiñca, yadi svata eva sattā satī upeyate tadā pramānaṃ vaktavyam | atha svataḥ sattā satī – tatsambandhāt tanvādīnāṃ sattvānyathānupapatteḥ – tarhy anyonyasaṃśrayaḥ – tatsambandhāt tanvādisattve siddhe sattāsattvasiddhiḥ, tatas tatsambandhāt tanvādisattvasiddhir iti vyaktam itaretarāśrayatvam | atha sattā svataḥ satī, sadabhidhānapratyayaviṣayatvāt, avāntarasāmānyādivat; na; dravyādina vyabhicāraḥ – dravyādir api sad dravyam, san guṇaḥ, sat karma ity evaṃ sadabhidhānapratyayaviṣayo bhavati, na cāsau pareṇa svataḥ sann abhyupagataḥ sattāprakalpanavaiphalyaprasaṅgāt | na ca dravyādau tadviṣayatvaṃ parāpekṣaṃ na sattāyām iti vaktuṃ yuktam, tasyām api tadapekṣatvasambhavāt | atha tatra tasya tadapekṣatve kiṃ tadaparam iti vaktavyam; nanv etad dravyādāv api samānam | tatra satteti cet, atrāpi dravyādikam iti tulyam – yathaiva hi sattāsambandhāt dravyādikaṃ sat na svataḥ tathā dravyādisvarūpasattvasambandhāt sattā satī na svataḥ | dravyādeḥ svarūpasattvaṃ nāsti tenāyam adoṣaḥ, tadastitve koḥ doṣa iti vācyam | nanu tasya svataḥ sattve 'vāntarasāmānyābhāvaprasaṅgo doṣaḥ, nanu svato 'sattve kharaviṣāṇāder iva sutarāṃ tadabhāvadoṣaḥ | api ca, yo hi tatra sattāsambandhaṃ necchati sa katham avāntarasāmānyasambandham iccet ? na cātra pramāṇaṃ svato 'santo dravyādayo nāvāntarasāmānyam iti | athaitat – dravyādayo na svataḥ santaḥ, avāntarasāmānyavattvāt, yat punaḥ svataḥ sat na tad avāntarasāmānyavat, yathā sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyā iti vyatirekī hetuḥ, naitat; yadi hi dravyādayo dharmiṇaḥ kutaścit pratītigocaracāriṇet santo bhavanti [sa katham avāntarasāmānyasambandham icchet ? na cātra pramāṇaṃ svato 'santo dravyādayo nāvāntarasāmānyam iti | athaitat – dravyādayo na svataḥ santo 'vāntarasāmānyavattvāt, yat punaḥ svataḥ sat na tad avāntarasāmānyavat, yathā] sāmānyapratītiḥ sattvaṃ sādhayantī svata iti pratijñām tad asattvaviṣayābādhane ced (cāriṇas santo bhavanti tadā kathaṃ na tatpratītiḥ svatas sattvaṃ sādhayantī na svatas santas te iti pratijñāṃ tadasattvaviṣayāṃ bādhate | na ced) matrottaram – na svataḥ santas te pratītiviṣayāḥ kintu sattāsambandhād iti, yato na svayam asantas tatsambandhāt tadviṣayā bhavanti ity uktam | kiṃ ca, dravyāder ekāntena yasya bhinnānyavāntarasāmānyāni kathaṃ tasya tāni syuḥ yato 'vāntarasāmānyavattvād iti hetuḥ siddhaḥ syāt ? atha tathāpi tasyeti, na; parasparam api syur iti sāmānyasamavāyāt pariśeṣavat (sāmānyasamavāyaviśeṣavat) iti vaidharmyanidarśanam ayuktam | yadi matam – dravyādau tāni samavetāni tatas tasya tāni na sāmānyāder viparyayād iti, tan na samyak; tatra samavetāni iti samavāyena sambaddhānīti yady arthaḥ, sa na yuktaḥ; samavāyasya niṣiddhatvān niṣetsyamānatvāc ca | bhavatu vā samavāyaḥ tathāpi yatra dravye, guṇe, karmaṇi ca dravyatvam, guṇatvam, karmatvaṃ cāvāntarasāmānyaṃ tatraiva pṛthivītvādīni, rūpatvādīni, gamanatvādīni ca tathāvidhāni sāmānyāni, samavāyo 'pi tatraiva sāmānyavat tasya sarvagatatvāc ca dravyādivad anyonyasattānīti na dravyādeḥ svataḥ sattvabādhanam ity āśaṅkā na nivartate – kiṃ dravyādisambandhāt sattā satī kiṃ vā tayā dravyādikaṃ sat iti | tan na sattātaḥ tanvādeḥ sattvam, tasyā evāsiddhatvāt |
 111
