 {sattāpratyakṣādipramāṇasiddhatvāt sattāyāḥ pratyakṣabādhitaviṣayatvena evam upanyasyamānasya prasaṅgasādhanasyānavakāśaḥ | na ca dravayapratibhāsavelāyām pratyakṣabuddhau parisphuṭarūpeṇa vyaktivivekena sattā na pratibhātīti śakyaṃ vaktum, anugatākārasya vyāvṛttākārasya ca pratyakṣānubhavasya saṃvedanāt | na cānugatavyāvṛttavastuvyatirekeṇa dvyākārā buddhir ghaṭati | na hi viṣayavyatirekeṇa pratītir utpadyate, nīlādisvalakṣaṇapratīter api tathābhāvaprasaṅgāt | atha taimirikasya bāhyārthasannidhivyatirekeṇāpi keśoṇḍukādipratītir udeti tathaivānugatarūpam antareṇāpi bhinnavastuṣv anugatākārā buddhir udeṣyatīti na tataḥ ssttāvyavasthā, tad ayuktam, taimirikapratītau hi pratibhāsamānasya keśoṇḍikāder bādhakakāraṇadoṣaparijñānād atattvam; sattādarśane tu na bādhakapratyayodayaḥ nāpi kāraṇadoṣaparijñānam iti na tadgrāhiṇo vijñānasya mithyātvam | tathā hi – vibhinneṣv api ghaṭapaṭādiṣv artheṣu sat sat iti abhedam ullikhantī pradtītir udayam āsādayati; na cāsau kālāntarādau viparyayam upagacchantī lakṣyate, sarvadā sarveṣāṃ ghaṭapaṭādiṣu sat sat iti vyāhṛteḥ | vyavahāram upacāryantī ca pratītiḥ parair api pramāṇam abhyupagamyate | yathoktaṃ taiḥ – prāmāṇyaṃ vyavahāreṇa XE "[PV II 5a]"[PV II 5a] iti | tad evam avastthitam – anugatākārā hi buddhir vyāvṛttarūpapratītyanadhigataṃ sādhāraṇarūpam ullikhantī supariniścitarūpā bādhāyogāt pramānam | sā ca akṣānvayavyatirekānusāritayā pratyakṣam | tathā hi – visphāritalocanasya ghaṭapaṭādiṣu rūpam ārūḍhāṃ sattām ullikhantī sat sat iti pratītiḥ, tadabhāve ca na bhavatīti tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyitayā kathaṃ na pratyakṣam ? tasmād bahuṣu vyāvṛtteṣu tulyākārā buddhir ekatām avasyti | yac cātra vibhinneṣu ghaṭādiṣu pratiniyatam ekam anugatasvarūpaṃ saiva jātiḥ |
 atha vyaktiriktā jātir upeyate na ca vyaktidarśanavelāyāṃ tadrūpasaṃsparśaviṣayavyatiriktavapur aparam anugatirūpaṃ pratibhāti tat kathaṃ tat sāmānyam ? naitad asti; yasmād agṛhītasaṅketasyāpi tanubhṛtaḥ prathamam udbhāti vastu; dvitīye tulyarūpatām anusarati buddhiḥ kvacid eva na sarvatra | pratipattyanyatā ca sarvatra bhedavyavahāranibandhanaṃ tulyadeśakālo 'pi rūparasādau ca, pratipattyanyatā jātāv api vidyata iti kathaṃ na sā bhinnāsti ? tathā hi – vyaktyākāravivekena viśadam anugatirūpatā bhāti tadvivekena ca vyāvṛttarūpateti kathaṃ vyaktisvarūpād bhinnāvabhāsinī jātir bhinnā nābhyupagamaviṣayaḥ ?
 athaikendriyāvaseyatvād jātivyaktyor ekatā rūparasādau tu bhinnendriyagrāhyatvād bhedaḥ, tad apy asaṅgatam; yata ekendriyagrāhyam api vātātapādikaṃ samānadeśaṃ ca bhinnaṃ pratibhātīti bhinnavapur abhyupeyate tathā pratiniyatendriyaviṣayam api jātivyaktidvayaṃ bhinnam; bhinnapratibhāsād eva | tathā hi – ghaṭam antareṇāpi paṭagrahaṇe sat sat iti pūrvapratipannā sattāvagatir dṛṣṭā; yadi tu vyaktir eva satī na jātiḥ tatsattve 'pi tadavyatiriktā ca, tathā sati vyaktirūpavat tadananugatir api  sarvadā | tenaikarūpaiva jātiḥ, pratyakṣe tathābhūtāyā eva tasyāḥ pratibhāsanāt; śabdaliṅgayor api tasyām eva sambandhagrahaṇam iti tābhyām api sā pratīyate | tad evaṃ pratyakṣādipramāṇāvaseyatvāt sattāyāḥ na tannirākaraṇāya prasaṅgasādhanānumānapravṛttir iti |}
 asat etat |
 yato na vyaktidarśanavelāyāṃ svarūpeṇa bahirgrāhyākāratayā pratītim avatarantī jātir udbhāti, na hi ghaṭapaṭavastudvayapratibhāsasamaye tadaiva tadvyavasthitamūrttibhinnābhinnā vā jātir ābhāti, tadākārasyāparasya grāhyatayā bahis tatrāpratibhāsanāt | bahirgrāhyāvabhāsaś ca barirarthavyavasthākārī, nāntarāvabhāsaḥ | yadi tu so 'pi tadvyavasthākārī syāt tathā sati hṛdi parivartamānavapuṣaḥ sukhāder api pratibhāsād bahis tadvyavasthā syāt; tathā ca sukhāsyātmakāḥ śabdādayaḥ iti sāṅkyadarśanam eva syāt | atha sukhādir ākāro bāhyarūpatayā na pratibhātīti na bahir asau, jātir api tarhi na bahīrūpatayā pratibhātīti na bahīrūpābhyupagantavyā, yataḥ kalpanāmatir api darśanadṛṣṭam eva ghaṭādirūpaṃ bahirullikhantī tadgiraṃ ca antaḥ pratibhāti,
 112
 na tu tadvyatiriktavapuṣaṃ jātim uddyotayati | tan na tadavaseyāpi bahirjātir asti taimirikajāne bahiṣprakāśamānavapuṣo 'pi hi keṣoṇḍukādayo na tathābhyupeyante, bādhyamānajñānaviṣayatvāt | jātis tu na bahīrūpatayā kvacid api jñāne pratibhātīti kathaṃ sā sattvābhyupagamaviṣayaḥ ? buddhir eva kevalaṃ ghaṭapaṭādiṣu pratibhāsamāneṣu sat sat iti tulyatanur āabhāti | yadi tarhi na bāhyā jātir asti, buddir api katham ekarūpā pratibhāti na hi bahirnimittam antareṇa tadākārotpattimatī sā yuktā ? nanu kenocyate bahirnimittanirāpekṣā jātimatir iti, kin tu bahirjātir na nimittam iti, bāhyās tu vyaktayaḥ kāścid eva jātibuddher nimittam | nanu yadi vyaktinibandhanānugatākārā bahiḥ, tathā sati yathā khaṇḍamuṇḍavyaktidarśane gaur gauḥ iti pratipattir udeti tathā giriśikharādidarśane 'pi gaur gauḥ ity etadākārā pratipattir bhavet vyaktibhedāviśeṣāt, tad ayuktam; bhedāviśeṣe 'pi khaṇḍamuṇḍādivyaktiṣu gaur gauḥ ityākārā matir udayam āsādayantī samupalabhyata iti tā eva tām upajanayituṃ samarthā ity avasīyate, na punar guruśikharādiṣu gaur garuḥ iti matir dṛṣṭeti na te tannibandhanam | yathā ca āmalakīphalādiṣu yathāvidhānam upayukteṣu vyādhiviratilakṣaṇaṃ phalam upalabhyata iti tāny eva tadvidhau samarthānīty avasīyate, bhedāviśeṣe 'pi na punas trapuṣadadhyādīni | atha bhinneṣv api bhāveṣu sat sat iti matir asti, vibhinneṣu ca bhāveṣu yad ekatvaṃ tad eva jātiḥ, tatrocyate – tad ekatvaṃ ghaṭapaṭādiṣu kim anyat, utānanyat ? na tāvad anyat, tasyāpratibhāsanād ity ukte | nāpy ananyat, ekarūpāpratibhāsanāt; na hi ghaṭasysa paṭasya buddhir ea tulyākārapratibhāsā sat sat iti śabdaś ca dṛśyata iti tadanvaya eva yuktaḥ na jātyanvayaḥ, tasyādarśanāt | na ca buddhidvarūpam apy aparabuddhisvarūpam anugacchati iti na tad api sāmānyam ity evānugatajātivādo mithyāvādaḥ |
 api ca, anekavyaktivyāpi sāmānyaṃ tadvādibhir abhyupagamyate | na ca tadvyapitvaṃ tasya kenacit jñānena vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyam | tathā hi – sannihitavyaktipratibhāsakāle jātis tadvyaktisaṃsparśinī sphuṭam avabhāti na vyaktyantarasambandhitayā, tasyātathāsannidhānena pratibhāsāyogāt | tadapratibhāse ca tanmiśratāpi nāvagateti katham asannhitavyaktyantarasambaddhaśarīrā jātir avabhāti | yad eva hi parisphuṭadarśane pratibhāti rūpaṃ tad eva tasyā yuktam, darśanāsaṃsparśinaḥ svarūpasyāsambhavāt; sambhave vā tasya dṛśyasvabhāvād bhedaprasaṅgāt, tadekatve sarvatra bhedapratihatoḥ anānaikaṃ jagat syāt | darśanagocarātītaṃ ca vyaktyantarasambaddhaṃ gātisvarūpam apratibhāsanād asat; pratibhāsane vā tasya tatsambaddhānāṃ vyavahitavyakty antarāṇām api pratibhāsaprasaṅga iti sakalajagatpratibhāsaḥ syāt | atha matam – sannihitavyaktidarśanakāle vyaktyantarasambandhinī jātir na bhāti; yadā tu vyaktyantaraṃ dṛśyate tadā taddarśanavelāyāṃ tadgatatvena jātir ābhātīti sādhāraṇasvarūpaparicchedaḥ paścāt sambhavatīti, tataś ca paścād arthānvayadarśane kathaṃ na tasyās tadvyāpitāgrahaḥ ? asad etat; yato vyaktyantaradarśanakāle 'pi tatpariharam eva jāteḥ svarūpaṃ pratibhāti na pūrvavyaktisaṃsparśitayā; tasyāḥ pratyakṣagocarātikrāntatayā tatsambaddhasyāpi rūpasya tadatikrāntatvāt | tat kathaṃ sannihitāsannihitavyaktisambaddhajātirupāvagamaḥ ?
 atha pratyabhijñānād anekavyaktisambandhitvena jātiḥ pratīyate, nanu keyaṃ pratyabhijñā ? yadi pratyakṣam, kutas tadavaseyā jātir ekānekavyktivyāpinī pratyakṣā ? atha nayanavyāparāntantaraṃ samupajāyamānā pratyabhijñā kathaṃ na pratyakṣam ? nirvikalpakasyāpy akṣānvayavyatirekānuvidhānāt pratyakṣatvaṃ tad atrāpi tulyam, asad etat; yadi akṣajā pratyabhijñā tathā sati prathamavyaktidarśanakāla eva samastavyaktisambaddhajātirūpaparicchedo 'stu | atha tadā smṛtisahakārivirahān na tattvāvagatiḥ, yadā tu dvitīyavyaktidarśanaṃ tadā pūrvadarśanāhitasaṃskāraprabodhasamupajātasmṛtisahitam indriyaṃ tattvadarśanaṃ janayati, tad apy asat; yataḥ smaraṇasacivam api locanaṃ puraḥsannihitāyām eva vyaktau tatsthajātau ca pratipattiṃ janayitum īśam; na pūrvavyaktau, asannidhānāt | tan na tatsthitāṃ jātiṃ darśanaṃ paridṛśyamāne vyaktyantare sandhatte |
 athendriyavṛttir na smaraṇasamavāyinī karaṇatvād iti nāsau sandhānakāriṇī, puruṣas tu kartṛtayā smṛtisamavāyīti cakṣuṣā parigate 'rthe tadupadarśitapūrvavyaktigatāṃ jātiṃ sandhāsyatīti, tad asat; yataḥ so 'pi na svatantratayārthagrāhakaḥ kintu darśanasahāyaḥ |
 113
 yadi punaḥ savatantra evārthagrāhakaḥ syāt tathā sati svāpamadamūrchādiṣv api sarvavyaktyanugatajātipratipattiḥ syāt | tasmād ātmāpi darśanasahāya evārthavedī | darśanam ca puraḥsannihitaṃ vyaktisvarūpam anusarati, na hi smṛtigocaram api pūrvadṛṣṭavyaktigataṃ jātyādikam iti na darśanasahāyo 'pi tadanusandhānasamartha ātmā | atha smaraṇopanītaṃ jātirūpam ātmā tatra sandhāsyati; nanv atrāpi smṛtiḥ parihṛtapurovarttivyaktidarśanaviṣayā pūrvadṛṣṭam artham anusarantī saṃlakṣyate | tat katham purovarttini apravartamānā svaviṣayān sāmānyādīṃs tatra saṅghatayituṃ kṣamā ? tad asmṛtaṃ ca saṅghatanaṃ katham ātmāpi kartuṃ kṣamaḥ ? tathā hi – darśane 'sti draṣṭari tasya svarūpe jāte tannimagnaṃ na smṛtikṛtaṃ smartṛrūpaṃ bhāti | yadi tu bhāti tathā sati draṣṭṛrūpa evāsau, na smartā | atha smartṛrūpe draṣṭṛsvarūpam anupraviṣṭaṃ pratibhāti, tathāpi smartaivāsau na dṛṣṭā | atha draṣṭṛsmrtṛsavarūpe vivikte bhātaḥ, tathā sati tayor bheda iti naikatvam | tathā hi – draṣṭasvarūpaṃ dṛgviṣayāvabhāso pratibhāti, smartṛsvarūpam api puṃsaḥ smṛtiviṣayam avatīrṇam avabhāti; tat kutaḥ pūrvāparayor jātirūpayoḥ saṅghānam ?
 yad punar uktam smarataḥ pūrvadṛṣṭārthānusaṅghānād utpadyamānā matiś cakṣuḥsambaddhatve pratyakṣam iti, etad apy asat; nendriyamatiḥ smṛtigocarapūrvarūpagrāhiṇī, tat kathaṃ sā tatsaṅghānam ātmasātkaroti ? pūrvadṛṣṭasaṅghānaṃ hi tatpratibhāsanam, tatpratibhāsasambandhe cendriyamateḥ parokṣārthagrāhitvāt parisphuṭapratibhāsanam asannihitaviṣayagrahaṇaṃ ca, tat kutas tayor aikyam ? atha parokṣagrahaṇaṃ svātmanā nendriyamatiḥ saṃspṛśati, evaṃ tarhi tadviviktendriyamatir iti kathaṃ tatsaṅghāyikā sāmagrī abhyupiyate ? yadi ca smṛtiviṣayasvabhāvatayā dṛśyamāno 'rthaḥ pratyakṣabuddhibhir avagamyate, tathā sati smṛtigocaraḥ pūrvasvabhāvo vartamānatayā bhātīti viparītakhyātiḥ sarvaṃ darśanaṃ bhavet | atha yat tadā tatrāvidyamānam artham avaiti jñānaṃ tatra viparītakhyātiḥ, pratyakṣapratītis tu pūrvasaṅghānād apy upajāyamānā puraḥ sad eva vastu gṛhṇatī kathaṃ viparītakhyātir bhavet ? nanu purvarūpatāhitayā tasyāḥ sadarthagrahaṇam eva na sambhavati; smaraṇopaneyaṃ hi rūpaṃ pratiyatī vartamānatayā pratyakṣabuddir na pratibhāsamānavapuṣaḥ sattāṃ sādhayitum akṣam, pratyastamite 'pi rūpe smṛter avatārāt | tadanusāriṇī cākṣamatir api tad evānusarantī na sattāspadam | tasmād indriyamatiḥ sakalā pūrvarūpagrahaṇaṃ pariharantī vartamāne parisphuṭe vartata iti tadaiva tadgatāṃ jātim udbhāsayituṃ prabhur iti na pūrvāparavyaktigatā jātiḥ samasti | yad eva hi dvitīyavyaktigataṃ rūpaṃ bhāti tad eva sat, pūrvavyaktigataṃ tu rūpaṃ na abhātīti nan tat sat | tataś cānekavyaktivyāpikāyā jāter siddhir iti na tatra liṅgaśabdayor api pravṛttir iti na tābhyām api tatpratipattiḥ | yathā ca vyaktibhinnānusyūtā jātir na sambhavati tathā yathāsthānaṃ pratipādayiṣyata ity āstāṃ tāvat |
 tad evaṃ sattāsamavāyayoḥ paraparikalpitayor asiddheḥ prāgasataḥ kāraṇasamavāyaḥ sattāsamavāyo vā kāryatvam iti kāryatvasyāsiddhatvāt svarūpāsiddho 'pi kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetuḥ } |
 atha syād eṣa doṣo yadi yathoktalakṣaṇaṃ karyatvaṃ hetutvenopanyastaṃ syāt, yāvatābhūtvā bhavanalakṣaṇaṃ kāryatvaṃ hetutvenopanyastaṃ tenāyam adoṣaḥ | nanv evam api bhūbhūdharādeḥ katham evaṃbhūtaṃ kāryatvaṃ siddham ? atha yadi atra vipratipattiviṣayatā tadānumānatas teṣu kāryatvaṃ sādhyate, tac cānumānam – bhūbhūdharāyaḥ kāryam, racanāvattvāt, ghaṭādivat iti kathaṃ na teṣu kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetuḥ siddhaḥ ? asad etat; yato 'trāpi prayoge bhūbhūdharāder avayavino 'siddher āśrayāsiddhaḥ racanāvattvāt iti hetuḥ, tadasiddhatvaṃ ca prāk pratipāditam |
 kiñca, {yadi avayavasanniveśo racanā tadvattvaṃ ca pṛthivyādes tadutpādyatvam tadāvayavasanniveśasya saṃyogāparanāmno 'siddhatvād asiddhaviśeṣaṇo racanāvattvād iti hetuḥ | tathā, pṛthivyādiṣu samyogajanyatvasya viśeṣyasyāsiddhatvād asiddhaviśeṣaś ca prakṛto hetuḥ |
 kathaṃ saṃyogāsiddhatvam yenoktadoṣāduṣṭaḥ prakṛto hetuḥ syāt ? ucyate, tadgrāhakapramāṇābhāvāt bādhakapramāṇopapatteś ca | tathā hi -

 [prāsaṅgikaṃ saṃyogasya nirasanam]
 
 saṅkhyāparimāṇāni pṛthaktvam saṃyogavibhāgau paratvāparatve karma ca rūpi(dravya)samavāyāc cākṣāṇi [VS 4.1.11]
 iti vacanāt dṛśyavastusamavetasya saṃyogasya pareṇa pratyakṣagrāhyatvam abhyupagatam | na ca niranarotpannavastudvayapratibhāsakālo 'dhyakṣapratipattau tadvyatirekeṇa paraḥ saṃyogo bahirgrāhyarūpatāṃ vibhrāmaḥ pratibhāti, nāpi kalpanabuddhau vastudvayaṃ yathoktaṃ vihāya śabdollekhaṃ cāntaram aparaṃ varṇākṛtyakṣarākārarahitaṃ saṃyogasvarūpam udbhāti | tad evam upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasya saṃyogasyānupalabdher bhāvah, śaśaviṣāṇavat |
 tena yad āha uddyotakaraḥ 
 yadi saṃyogo nārthāntaraṃ bhavet tadā kṣetrabījodakādayo nirviśiṣṭatvāt sarvadaivāṅkurādikāryaṃ kuryuḥ, na caivam, tasmāt sarvadā kāryānārambhāt kṣetrādīny aṅkurotpattau karaṇāntarasāpekṣāṇi, yathā mṛtpiṇḍādisāmagrī ghaṭādikaraṇe kulālādisāpekṣā; yo 'sau kṣetrādibhir apekṣyaḥ sa saṃyoga iti siddham | kiñca, asau saṃyogo dravyayor viśeṣanabhāvena pratīyamānatvāt tato 'rthāntaratvena pratyakṣasiddha eva | tathā hi – kaścit kenācit saṃyukte dravye āhara ity ukto yayor eva dravyayoḥ saṃyogam upalabhate te evāharati na dravyamātram | kiñca, dūrataravartinaḥ puṃsaḥ sāntare 'pi vane nirantararūpāvasāsinī buddhir udayām āsādayati, seyaṃ mithyābuddhiḥ mithyapadārthānubhavam antareṇa na kvacid upajāyate | na hy ananubhūtagodarśanasya gavaye gauḥ iti vibhramo bhavati | tasmād avaśyaṃ saṃyogo mukhyo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ | tathā, na caitraḥ kuṇḍalī ity anena pratiṣedhavākyena na kuṇḍalaṃ pratiṣidhyate, nāpi caitraḥ, tayor anyatra deśādau sattvāt | tasmāc caitrasya kuṇḍalasaṃyogaḥ pratiṣidhyate | tathā, caitraḥ kuṇḍalī ity anenāpi vidhivākyena na caitrakuṇḍalayor anyataravidhānam, tayoḥ siddhatvāt; pāriśeṣyāt samyogavidhānam | tasmād asty eva saṃyogaḥ [NV 219] iti, tannirastaṃ draṣṭavyam; saṃyuktadravyasvarūpāvabhāsavyatirekeṇāparasya saṃyogasya pratyakṣe nirvikalpake sanikalpake vāparitabhāsasya pratipāditatvāt |
 na ca saṃyuktapratiyayānyathānupapattyā samyogakalpanopapannā, nirantarāvasthayor eva bhāvayoḥ saṃyuktapratyayahetutvāt | yāvac ca tasyām avasthāyāṃ saṃayogajanakatvena saṃyuktapratyayaviṣayau tāv iṣyete tāvat samyogam antareṇa saṃyuktapratyayahetutvena tadviṣayau kiṃ neṣyete pāramparyeṇa ? na ca sāntare vane nirantarāvabhāsinī buddhir mukhyapadārthānubhavapūrvikā, askhalatpratyayatvenānupacaritatvāt | na caitraḥ kuṇḍalī ityādau caitrasambandhikuṇḍalaṃ niṣidhyate vidhīyate vā na saṃyogaḥ | na ca sambandhavyatirekeṇa caitrasya kuṇḍalasambandhānupapattir iti vaktuṃ śakyam, yataś caitrakuṇḍalayoḥ kiṃ sambandhinoḥ sa sambandhaḥ, utāsambandhinoḥ ? nāsambandhinoḥ, himavadvindhyayor ivāsambandhinoḥ sambandhānupapatteḥ; na cāsambandhinor bhinnasambandhena tadabhinnaṃ sambandhitvaṃ śakyaṃ vidhātum, viruddhadharmādhyāsena bhedāt | nāpi bhinnam, tatsadbhāve 'pi tayoḥ svarūpeṇāsambandhitvaprasaṅgāt; bhinnasya tatkṛtopakāram antareṇa  tatsambandhitvāyogāt; tato 'paropakārakalpane 'navasthāprasaṅgāt | sambandhinos tu sambandhaparikalpanaṃ vyartham, sambandham antareṇāpi tayoḥ svata eva sambandhisvarūpatvāt |
 yat tūktam viśiṣṭāvasthāvyatirekeṇa kṣitibījodakādīnāṃ nāṅkurajanakatvam, sā ca viśiṣṭāvasthā teṣāṃ saṃyogarūpā śaktiḥ, tad asāram; yato yathā viśiṣṭāvasthāyuktāḥ kṣityādayaḥ saṃyogam utpādayanti tathā tadavasthāyuktā aṅkurādikam api kāryam niṣpādayiṣyantīti vyarthaṃ saṃyogaśaktes tadantarālavartinyāḥ parikalpanam | atha saṃyogaśaktivyatirekena na kāryotpādane kāraṇakalāpaḥ pravartata iti nirbandhas tarhi saṃyogaśaktyutpādane 'py aparasamyogaśaktivyatirekeṇa vāsau pravartata ity aparā saṃyogaśaktiḥ parikalpanīyā, tatrāpy aparety anavasthā | atha tām antareṇāpi śaktim utpādayanti tarhi kāryam api tām antareṇaivāṅkurādikaṃ nirvartayiṣyantīti vyarthaṃ saṃyogaśaktes tadantarālavartinyāḥ kalpanam | na ca viśiṣṭāvasthāvyatirekeṇa pṛthivyādayaḥ saṃyogaśaktim api nirvartayituṃ kṣamāḥ; tathābhyupagame sarvadā tannirvartanaprasaṅgād aṅkurāder apy anavaratotpattiprasaṅgaḥ | na cānyatarakarmādisavyapekṣāḥ saṃyogam utpādayanti kṣityādaya iti nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, karmotpattāv api samyogapakṣoktadūṣaṇasya sarvasya tulyatvāt | tasmād ekasāmagryadhīnaviśiṣṭotpattimatpadārthavyatirekeṇa nāparaḥ saṃyogaḥ, tasya bādhakapramāṇaviṣayatvāt sādhakapramāṇābhāvāc ca |
 115
 yas tu saṃyukte dravye ete iti anayor vāyaṃ saṃyogaḥ iti vyapadeśaḥ sa bhedāntarapratikṣepāpratikṣepābhyāṃ tathāvasthotpannavastudvayanibandhana eva, nāto 'parasya saṃyogasya siddhiḥ | na cākṣaṇikatve tayoḥ sa sambandhī yuktaḥ | tatsambandhasya samavāyasya niṣiddhatvāt niṣetsyamānatvāc ca | na ca tajjanyatvād asau tatsambandhī, akṣaṇikatve janakatvavirodhasya pratipādayiṣyamāṇatvāt | kṣaṇikatve 'pi tayor ekasāmagryadhīnā nairantaryotpattir eva, nāparaḥ saṃyoga iti racanāvattvāt ity atra hetor viśeṣaṇasya saṃyogaviśeṣasya racanālakṣaṇasyāsiddhes tadvato viśeṣyasyāpy asiddhir iti dvarūpāsiddhatvam} |
 atha pṛtivyādeḥ kāryatvaṃ bauddhair abhyupagamyata eveti nāsiddhatvaṃ tair asya hetoḥ pareranīyam | nanv atrāpi yādṛgbhūtaṃ buddhimatpūrvakatvena devakulādiṣv anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ vyāptaṃ kāryatvam upalabdham – yad akriyādarśino 'pi jīrṇadevakulādāv upalabhyamānaṃ laukikaparīkṣakādes tatra kṛtabuddhim utpādayati – tādṛgbhūtasya kṣityādiṣu kāryatvasyānupalabdher asiddhaḥ kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetuḥ | upalambhe vā tatra tato jīrṇadevakulādiṣv ivākriyādarśino 'pi kṛtabuddiḥ syāt; na hy anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ suvivecitaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ vyabhicarati XE "[cf. PVin II 37,15 ~ PVSV 23,26]"[cf. PVin II 37,15 ~ PVSV 23,26], tasyāhetukatvaprasaṅgāt | ataḥ kṣityādiṣu kāryatvadarśanād akriyādarśinaḥ kṛtabuddhyanupapatter yad buddhimatkāraṇatvena vyāptaṃ kāryatvaṃ devakulādiṣu niścitaṃ tat tatra nāstīty asiddho hetuḥ; kevalaṃ kāryatvamātraṃ prasiddhaṃ tatra | na ca prakṛtyā parasparam arthāntaratvena vyavasthito 'pi dharmaḥ śabdamātreṇābhedī hetutvenopādīyamāno 'bhimatasādhyasiddhaye paryāpto bhavati, sādhyaviparyaye 'pi taya bhāvāvirodhāt; yatha valmīke dharmiṇi  kumbhakārakṛtatvasiddhaye mṛdvikāramātraṃ hetutvenopādīyamānam iti | yad buddhimatkāraṇatvena vyāptaṃ devakulādau kāryatvaṃ pramāṇataḥ pratisiddhaṃ yac ca kṣityādāv asiddham; yac ca kṣityādau nānaikāntikam; na tato 'bhimatasādhyasiddhiḥ |
 nanv etat kāryasamaṃ nāma jātyuttaram | tathā hi – kṛtakatvād anityaḥ śabdhaḥ ity ukte jātivādy atrāpi prerayati – kim idaṃ ghaṭādigataṃ kṛtakatvaṃ hetutvenopanyastam, kiṃ vā śabdagatam, athobhayagatam iti ? ādye pakṣe hetor asiddhiḥ, na hy anyadharmo 'nyatra vartate | dvitīya 'pi sādhanavikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ | tṛtīye 'py etāv eva doṣāv iti | etac ca kāryasamaṃ nāma jātyuttaram iti pratipāditam; yathoktam – kāryatvānyatvaleśena yat sādhyāsiddhidarśanaṃ tat kāryasamam XE "[PS VI 8]"[PS VI 8] iti, kāryatvasāmānyasyānityatvasādhakatvenopanyāse 'bhyupagate dharmibhedena vikalpanavad buddhimatkāraṇatve kṣityādeḥ kāryatvamātreṇa sādhye 'bhīṣṭe dharmibhedena kāryatvāder vikalpanāt, asad etat; yataḥ sāmānyena kāryatvānityatvayor viparyaye bādhakapramāṇabalād vyāptisiddhau kāryatvasāmānyaṃ śabdādau dharmiṇyupalabhyamānam anityatvaṃ sādhayatīti kāryatvamātrasyaiva tatra hetutvenopanyāse dharmavikalpanaṃ yat tatra kriyeta tat sarvānumānocchedakatvena kāryasamajātyuttaratām āsādayate; na tv evaṃ kṣityāder buddhimatkāraṇatve sādhye kāryatvasāmānyaṃ hetutvena sambhavati, tasya viparyaye bādhakapramāṇābhāvāt sandigdhavipakṣavyāvṛttikatvenāvaikāntikatvāt | yac ca buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvena vyāptaṃ devakulādau kāryatvaṃ pratipannam – yad akriyādarśino 'pi jīrṇaprāsādādau kṛtabuddhim utpādayati  – tat tatrāsiddham iti pratipāditam |
 api ca, yadi atra vyāpakanityaikabuddhimatkāraṇaṃ kṣityādeḥ kāraṇatvenābhipretaṃ kāryatvalakṣaṇād dhetoḥ tathā sati ghaṭādau dṛṣṭāntadharmiṇi tatpūrvakatvena kāryatvasyāniścayāt sādhyavikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ, viruddhaś ca hetuḥ syād, anityabuddhyādhārāvyāpakānekakartṛpūrvakatvena vyāptasya kāryatvasya ghaṭādau niścayāt | atha buddhimatkāraṇatvamātraṃ sādhyatvenābhipretaṃ kṣityādau tarhi nityabuddhyādhāravyāpakaikakartṛpūrvakatvalakṣaṇasya vśeṣasya kṣityādāv asiddher neśvarasiddhiḥ | atha buddhimatkāraṇatvasāmānyam eva kṣityādau sādhyate, tac ca pakṣadharmatābalād viśiṣṭaviśeṣādhāraṃ sidhyati nirviśeṣasya sāmānyasyāsambhavāt, anityajñānavataḥ śarīriṇaḥ kṣityādivinirmāṇasāmarthyarahitatvena ghaṭādāv upalabdhasya viśeṣasya buddhimatkāraṇatvasāmānyādhārasya tatrāsambhavāt | nanv evaṃ sāmānyāśrayatvena yad ghaṭādau vyaktisvarūpaṃ pratipannaṃ tasya kṣityādāv asambhavāt, anyasya ca vyaktisvarūpasya vivakṣitasāmānyāśrayatvenāprasiddhatvāt, nirādhārasya ca sāmānyasyāsambhavāt, buddhimatkāraṇatvasāmānyasyaiva kṣityādau na siddhiḥ syāt;
 116
 na hi kvacid gotvādhārasya khaṇḍādivyaktiviśeṣasyāsambhave 'nyarūpamahiṣyādivyaktisamāśritaṃ gotvaṃ kutaścid dhetoḥ siddhim āsādayati |
 atha kāryatvasya kṣityādau buddhimatkāraṇatvābhāve 'bhavaprasaṅgād vilakṣaṇavyaktyāśritasyāpi tatsāmānyasya tatra siddhir bhavaty eva; yathā mahānasavilakṣaṇagiriśikharādyādhārasyāgnisāmānyasya dhūmāt prasidhiḥ | syād etat, yadi adhūmavyāvṛttaṃ dhūmātram anagnivyāvṛttenāgninā vyāptaṃ yathā pratyakṣānupalambhalakṣanāt pramāṇāt prasiddhaṃ tathātrāpy abhuddhimatkāraṇavyāvṛttena buddhimatkāraṇatvamātreṇākāravyāvṛttasya kāryamātrasya kutaścit pramāṇād vyāptiḥ sarvopasaṃhāreṇa niścitā syāt, yāvatā saiva na siddhā | atha yathā karyadharmānuvṛtteḥ kāryaṃ hutabhujo dhūmaḥ, sa tadabhāve 'pi abhavan hetumattāṃ vilaṅghayet iti nāgnivyatirekeṇa dhūmasya sadbhāva iti sarvopasaṃhāreṇa vyāptisiddhis tathātrāpi bhūdharādi kāryadharmānuvṛttito buddhimatkāraṇakāryam, tadabhāve tad bhavad  nirhetukaṃ syād iti sarvopasaṃhāreṇa vyāptisiddhiḥ | nanu ghaṭādilakṣaṇaḥ kāryaviśeṣo buddhimadanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyī ya upalabhyamānas tatsamāneṣu padārtheṣv akriyādarśino 'pi kṛtabuddhim utpādayati, sa eva buddhimatkāraṇakāryatvāt tadabhāve bhavan nirhetukaḥ syād iti vaktuṃ śākyam, na punaḥ kāryatvamātraṃ kāraṇamātrahetukaṃ buddhimatkāraṇābhāve bhavan nirhetukam āsajyate, tad dhi kāraṇamātrābhāve bhavad nirhetukaṃ syāt | na ca kāryaviśeṣaḥ kartāram antareṇa nopalabdha iti kāryatvamātram api kartṛviśeṣānumāpakam iti nyāyavidā vaktuṃ yuktam; anyathā dhūmaviśeṣas tatkālavahnyavyabhicarito mahānasādāv upalabdha iti dhūpaghaṭikādau dhūmamātram api tatkālavahnyanumāpakaṃ syāt | atha tatra tatkālavahnyanumāne tataḥ pratyakṣāvirodhaḥ, sa tarhi bhūruhādāv apy akṛṣṭajāte kartranumāne kāryatvalakṣaṇād dhetoḥ samānaḥ | atha tatkartur atīndriyatvābhyupagamād na pratyakṣavirodhaḥ, dhūpaghaṭikādāv api vahner atīndriyatvābhyupagame ko doṣo yeva pratyakṣavirodha udbhāvyeta ? mahānasādau tathābhūtasyaiva tasya darśanād iti cet; nanv evaṃ bhūruhādāv api yadi kartā syāt tadā śarīravān dṛśya eva syāt, ghaṭādau kartus tathābhūtasyaiva tasyopalambhāt iti samānaṃ paśyāmaḥ |
 atha vṛkṣaśākhābhaṅgādikāryasyādṛśyaḥ piśācādiḥ kartā yathābhyupagataḥ, svaśarīrāvayavānāṃ vāparaśarīravyatirekeṇāpi yathā vā prerako devadattādiḥ, tathā bhūruhādikāryakartādṛśyaḥ śarīrādirahitaś ca yadi syāt ko doṣaḥ ? na kaścid dṛṣṭavyatikramavyatirekeṇa | tathā hi – devadattāder api svaśarīrāvayavaprerakatvaṃ viśiṣṭaśarīrasambandhavyatirekeṇa nopalabdham ity etāvanmātram eva tatra tasya kartṛtvanibandhanam, nāparaśarīrasambaddhas tatra tasyopayogī iti bhūruhādikartur api śarīrasambaddhasyaiva kāryakaraṇe vyāpāro yuktaḥ nānyathā | tatsambandhaś ca tasya yadi tatkṛto 'bhyupagamyate tadā śarīsasambandharahitasya tadakaraṇasāmarthyam ity aparaśarīrasambandho 'bhyupagantavyaḥ, anyathā śarīrasambandharahitasya kathaṃ prastutakāryakaraṇam ? tathā, tadabhyupagame vyāparāparaśarīranirvartane kṣīṇavyāpāratvādīśasya na bhūruhādikāryanirvartavam | atha tadanirvartitaṃ taccharīraṃ tadātrāpi vaktavyam – kiṃ tat kāryam uta nityam iti ? yady ādyaḥ pakṣaḥ, tadā tasya kāryatve saty api na kartṛpūrvakatvam, tatas tenaiva kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetur vyabhicārī | atha nityam, tadā yathā taccharīrasya śarīratve saty api nityatvalakṣaṇaḥ svabhāvātikramo 'bhyupagamyate tathā bhūruhādeḥ kāryatve saty apy akartṛpūrvakatvam anabhyupagantavyam iti punar api tair hetur vyabhicārī prakṛtaḥ |
 piśācādes tu śarīrasambandharahitasya kāryakartṛtvaṃ muktātmana ivānupapannam | athāsty eva tasya śarīrasambandhaḥ, kintv adṛśyaśarīrasambandhād asāv adṛśyaḥ kartābhyupagamyate; nanu kulālāder api śarīrasambandha eva dṛśyatvaṃ nāparam, svarūpeṇātmano 'dṛṣṭyatvaṃ nāparam, svarūpeṇātmano 'dṛśyatvāt | atha dṛśyaśarīrasambandhāt tasya dṛṣyatvam; nanu piśācādiśarīrasya śarīratve saty api katham adṛśyatvam ?
 117
 asmadādicakṣurvyāpāreṇa tasyānupalambhād iti cet; nanu yathā śarīratve saty apy asmadādiśarīravilakṣaṇaṃ piśācādilakṣaṇaṃ śarīram anupalabhyatvenābhyupagamyate tathā ghaṭādivilakṣaṇaṃ bhūruhādi kāryatve saty apy akartṛkatvena kiṃ nābhyupagamyate ? tathābhyupagame ca punar api prakṛto hetur vyabhicārī | tad evam asiddhatvānaikāntikatvaviruddhatvadoṣadiṣṭatvād nāsmād dhetoḥ prastutasādhyasiddhiḥ | tena yad uktam pṛthivyādigatasya kāryatvasyāpratipatter na tasmād īśvarāvagamaḥ iti, tad yuktam evoktam |
 yat tūktan pṛthivyādīnāṃ bauddhaiḥ kāryatvam abhyupagatam te katham evaṃ vadeyuḥ iti, tad asāram; prakṛtasādhyasiddhinibandhanasya kāryatvasya teṣv asiddhatvapratipādanāt | yac cābhāṣi ye 'pi cārvākas teṣāṃ kāryatvaṃ necchanti teṣām api viśiṣṭasaṃsthānayuktānāṃ katham kāryatā iti, tas apy ayuktam; saṃsthānayuktatvasyāsiddhatvādidoṣaduṣṭatvapratipādanāt | yac ca saṃsthānaśabdavācyatvaṃ kevalaṃ ghaṭādibhiḥ sāmānyaṃ pṛthivyādīnām, na tv arthaḥ kaścid dvayor anigataḥ samāno vidyate tad evam eva; yat tūktam dhūmādāv api pūrvāparavyaktigato naiva kaścid anugato 'rthaḥ samāno 'sti ityādi, tad asaṅgatam; ghaṭādisaṃsthānebhyaḥ pṛthivyādisaṃsthānasya vailakṣaṇyena hetor asiddhatvapratipādanāt |
 yad apy uktam vyutpannānām asty eva pṛthivyādisamsthānavattvakāryatvāder hetor dharmidharmatāvagamaḥ, avyutpannānāṃ tu prasiddhānumāne dhūmādāv api nāsti iti, tad apy acāru; yato yady anumānanimittahetupakṣadharmatvapratibandhalakṣaṇāṃ vyutpattim āśritya vyutpannā abhidhīyate tadā pṛthivyādigatasaṃsthānakāryatvādau ghaṭādisaṃsthānavailakṣaṇye prakṛtasādhyasādhake vyutpattur na keṣāñcid api bhavati, yathoktasādhyavyāptasya pṛthivyādau saṃsthānāder abhāvāt | bhāve vā śarīrādimato 'smadādīndriyagrāhyasyānityabuddhayādidharmakalāpopetasya ghaṭādau saṃsthānādihetuvyāpakatvena pratipannasya kartuḥ pṛthivyādau tataḥ pratipattiḥ syāt, na hi hetuvyāpakam apahāyāvyāpakasya viruddhadharmākrāntasyāparasya sādhyadharmasya pratipattiḥ sādhyadharmiṇi yathoktalakṣanalakṣitahetubalasamutthety anumānavidāṃ vyavahāraḥ | kāraṇamātrapratipattau tu tataṣ tatra na vipratipattir iti siddhasādhyatā | atha hetulakṣaṇavyutpattivyatiriktāṃ vyutpattim āśritya vyutpannānām ity ucyate tadā kenacit sraṣṭrā jagat sṛṣṭam iti nirmūladurāgamāhitavāsanānām asty eva pṛthivyādisaṃsthānavattvakāryatvāder hetor dharmidharmatāvagamādiḥ; na ca tathābhūtadharmidharmatādyavagamāt sādhyasiddhiḥ, vede mīmāṃsakasyāsmaryamāṇakartṛkatvādeḥ dharmidharmatāvagamāder yathāpauruṣeyatvasya | avyutpannānāṃ tu pratisiddhānumāne dhūmādāv api nāsti iti yuktam eva, asmābhir apy abhyupagamyāt |
 yat tu prāsādādisaṃsthānāder vailakṣaṇye 'pi pṛthivyādisaṃsthānādeḥ kāryatvādi pṛthivyādīnām iṣyate, kāryaṃ ca kartṛkaraṇakarmapūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam ityādi, tad apy asaṅgatam; yato yato nāma ghaṭāder viśiṣṭakāryasya kartṛpūrvakatvam upalabdhaṃ naitāvatāviśiṣṭasyāpi bhūruhādikāryasya kartṛpūrvakatvam abhyupagantuṃ yuktam; anyathā pṛthivīlakṣaṇasya kāryasya rūparasagandhasparśaguṇayogitvam upalabdhaṃ bhūtatve sati, vāyor api tadyogitvam abhyupagamanīyaṃ syāt, tattvād eva | athātra pratyakṣādibādhaḥ, sa bhūruhādikāryeṣv api samāna iti prāk pratipāditam | yat tūktam kartṛpūrvakasya kāryatvādes tadvailakṣaṇyād na tataḥ sādhyāvagamaḥ ityādi, tat satyam eva; tadvailakṣaṇyasya prasādhitatvāt | ata iva siddham yādṛgadhiṣṭhātṛbhāvābhāvānuvṛttimat sanniveśādi ityādigranthapratipāditasya dūṣaṇasya kāryatvādau sarvasminn īśvarasādhake hetau samānatvād na kasyacit tatsādhakatā | yady evam anumānocchedaprasaṅgaḥ, dhūmādi yathāvidham agnyādisāmagrībhāvābhāvānuvṛttimat tathāvidham etad yadi parvatopari bhavet syāt tato vahnyādyavagamaḥ ityādikas tu pūrvapakṣagranthaḥ pūrvam eva vihitottaraḥ |
 yathā yathābhūto 'dhūmavyāvṛtto dhūmo 'nagnivyāvṛttenāgninā vyāpto viparyaye bādhakapramānabalād avasito giriśikharādāv upalabhyamānas taddeśam agnisāmānyam aniyatatārṇapārṇādyagnivyaktisamāśritam anumāpayati;
 118
 naivaṃ karyatvamātram buddhimatkāraṇatvasāmānyena vyāptaṃ viparyaye bādhakapramāṇabalād niścitaṃ kintu kāraṇatvamātreṇa vyāptaṃ tat tadbalād niścitam, tac copalabhyamānaṃ kṣityādau kāraṇamātram anumāpayati yathā giriśikharādāv upalabhyamāno dhūmas tatsambaddham agnimātram aniyatavyaktiniṣṭham, tena pṛthivyādigatakāryatvadarśanāt kartradarśinas tadapratipattivat śikharyādigatavahnyādyadarśināṃ dhūmād api tatpratipattir astu iti ko 'nyo 'numānasvarūpavido bhavato vaktuṃ kṣamaḥ | yadi hi kāryaviśeṣād dhūmalakṣaṇād upalabhyamānād gṛhītāvinābhāvasya puṃso 'gnilakṣaṇakāraṇaviśeṣapratipattir giriśikharādau bhavati tadā kāryamātrāt pṛthivyādāv upalabhyamānād buddhimatkāraṇaviśeṣasya tatra pratipattau kim āyātam ? kāraṇamātrapratipattis tu tatas tatra bhavaty eva, suvivecitaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ na vyabhicarati XE "[PVin II 37,15 ~ PVSV 23,26]"[PV
 in II 37,15 ~ PVSV 23,26] iti nyāyāt | ata evānyasya sambaddhasyānyataḥ pratipattau kāryakāraṇāvagamādau prayatnaḥ kāryaḥ; anyathā tadutthapramāṇasya pramāṇābhāsatā syāt | yat tu na cātra śabdasāmānyaṃ vastvanugamo nāstīti yuktaṃ vaktum, dhūmādāv api śabdasāmānyasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāt iti, tad apy asaṅgatam; dhūmādivailakṣaṇyena pṛthivyādau kāryatvasya buddhimatkāraṇatvāvyāpteḥ śabdasāmānyasya sādhitatvāt |
 yac ca ghaṭādivat pṛthivyādi svāvayavasaṃyogair ārabdham avaśyaṃ tadviśleṣād vināśam anubhaviṣyati ityādi, tad apy asaṅgatam; avayavasaṃyogavat tadviśleṣasyāpi vibhāgalakṣaṇasya vināśaṃ prati hetutvenopanyastasyāsiddhitvāt; tadasiddhatvaṃ ca samyogavad vaktavyam | evaṃ vināśād vā sambhāvitāt kāryatvānumānaṃ racanāsvabhāvatvād vā ityādi sarvaṃ nirastaṃ draṣṭavyam | yat tu cārvākaṃ prati kāryatvasādhanāyoktam – yathā laukikavaidikyo racanayor aviśeṣāt kartṛpūrvakatvaṃ tathā prāsādādipṛthivyādisamsthānayor api tadrūpatāstu, aviśeṣāt tad apy acāru; tadviśeṣasya pratipāditatvāt; na hi kevalāt pakṣadharmād vyāptiśūnyāt sādhyāvagamaḥ tat satyam eva, vyāptirahitād dhetoḥ sādhyasiddher asambhavāt |
 yac ca ghaṭādau kartṛpūrvakatvena kāryatvāvagame 'pi keṣāñcit kāryāṇām akartṛpūrvakāṇāṃ kāryatvadarśanān na sarvaṃ kartṛpūrvakam yathā vaneṣu vanaspatyādīnāṃ iti, tad api satyam eva tasmād neśvarasiddhau kaścid dhetur avyabhicāry asti ity etadparyantam | yad apy uktam nākṛṣṭajātaiḥ sthāvarādibhir vyabhicāro vyāptyabhāvo vā, sādhyābhāve hetur vartamāno vyabhicāry ucyate; teṣu kartragrahaṇaṃ na kartrabhāvaniścayaḥ iti, tad apy asāram; sarvapramāṇaviṣayatve 'pi yadi sthāvarādiṣu kartrabhāvaniścayo na bhavati tathā sati ākāśādau rūpādyabhāvaniścayo mā bhūt | atha tatra rūpādisadbhāvabādhakapramāṇasadbhāvāt tadabhāvaniścayaḥ, so 'trāpi samānaḥ | tac ca pramāṇaṃ pradarśayiṣyāmo 'nanantaram eva |
 yat tūktam kṣityādyanvayavyatirekānuvidhānadarśanāt teṣām tadatiriktasya karyaṇatvakalpane 'tiprasaṅgakoṣa iti, etasyāṃ kalpanāyāṃ dharmādharmayor api na kāraṇatā bhavet ityādi, tad ayuktam; dharmādharmādeḥ kāranatvaṃ jagadvaicitryānyathānupapattyā vyavasthāpyate | tathā hi – sarvān utpattimataḥ prati bhūmyādeḥ sādhāraṇatvāt tadanyādṛṣṭākhyavicitrakāraṇakṛtaṃ kāryavaicitryam | na caivam īśvarasya kāraṇatvaparikalpanāyāṃ kiñcin nimittaṃ sambhavati, tadvyatirekeṇa kasyacid arthasyānupapadyamānasyādṛṣṭeḥ | na ca cetanaṃ kartāraṃ vinākāryasvarūpānupapattir iti śakyam vaktum; dṛṣṭasyaiva sugatasutaiś caitanyasya jagadvaicitryakartṛkatvenābhyupagamāt; tadā tadvyatiriktānyeśvarasya kalpanāyāṃ nimittābhāvāt |
 na cādṛṣṭasya (?) cetaneṣv api sakalajagadupādānopakaraṇasampradānādyabhijñātā na sambhavatīti tadvyatirikto 'paro maheśas tajjñaḥ kalpanīya iti vaktuṃ yuktam, tajjñānavattvena tasyāpy asiddheḥ | na ca  sakalajagatkartṛtvasiddhiḥ, tatsiddhau ca tasya tadabhijñatvasiddhir iti vyaktam itaretarāśrayatvam |
 119
 atha yad yat karyam tat tad upadānādyabhijñakartṛpūrvakam upalabdhaṃ ghaṭādivat; pṛthivyādy api kāryam, tena tad api tadabhijñakartṛpūrvakaṃ yuktam iti netaretarāśrayadoṣaḥ | nanu ghaṭādikāryakartur api kulālāder yad api sarvathā ghaṭādyupādānādyabhijñatvaṃ siddhaṃ syāt tadā yujyetāpy etad vaktum; na ca tasyāpi ghaṭādyupādānopakaraṇādeḥ parimāṇāvayavasaṅkhyeyatvādyanekadharmasākṣātkaraṇajñānam asti, tattvaṃ siddham (?) | (kiṃ) cinmātraparijñānaṃ tu cetanatve 'dṛṣṭasyāpi tadādhārasya vā sattvasya taddṛṣṭanirvartitaphalopabhoktuḥ pratiniyataśarīrādhiṣṭhāyakasya vidyate iti vyarthaṃ vyatiriktāparajñānavato maheśasya parikalpanam | na cāyam ekāntaḥ – sarvaṃ kāryaṃ tadupādānādyabhijñenaiva kartrā nirvartyata iti; svāpamadāvasthāyāṃ śarīrādyavayavapreraṇasya kāryasya tadupādānābhijñānābhāve 'pi tatkṛtatvenopalabdheḥ |
 yac coktam – na cākṛṣṭajāteṣu sthāvarādiṣu tasyāgrahaṇena pratikṣepaḥ, anupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatvād adṛṣṭavat iti, tad apy acāru: yato yadi tasya śarīrasambandharahitasya kartṛtvam abhyupeyate tan na yuktisaṅgatam, tatsambandharahitasya muktātmano 'kartṛtvam na punaḥ śarīrasambandhābhāvād iti viṣamo dṛṣṭāntaḥ, tad ayuktam; jñānādisamavāyasya kartṛtvenābhyupagatasya tatrāpi niṣiddhatvāt | tasmāc charīrasambandhād eva tasya jagatkartṛtvam abhyupagantavyam kulālasyeva ghaṭakartṛtvam | tadsambandhaś ced abhyupagamyate kathaṃ na tasyopalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatvam ? kulālāder api śarīrasambandhād eva upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptam na punaḥ tatsambandharahitasyātmano dṛśyatvam | tac ceśvare 'pi śarīrasambandhitvaṃ kartṛtvād abhyupagantavyam ity upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptasyānupalabdhes tatkartuḥ sthāvarādiṣv abhāvaḥ siddha iti kathaṃ na taiḥ kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetur vyabhicārī ?
 athādṛśyaṃ taccharīram atas tatra sad api nopalabhyata ity ayam adoṣah; nanv evam api asmin sati idaṃ sthāvarādikaṃ jātam iti pratipattir ma bhūt, tathānyakāraṇabhave 'pi yathātīndriyasyendriyasyābhāve rūpādijñānam nopajāyate tathā pṛthivyādikaraṇasākalye 'pi kadācit taccharīravirahe tatsthāvarādikāryaṃ nopajāyata iti vyatirekāt pratītiḥ kiṃ na syāt ? yadā yatra taccharīraṃ niyamena sannihitam iti cāyaṃ doṣas tarhi yugapadbhāviṣu trilokādhikaraṇeṣu bhāveṣu kā vārtā ? na hy ekasya mūrtasya sāvayavasya maheśvaravapuṣo 'pi yugapatsakalavyāptiḥ sambhavati | amūrvatve niraṃśaprasaṅgād ākāśam eva taccharīram, tasya taccharīratvenādyāpy asiddhatvāt | atha yāvanti kramabhāvīny aṅkurādikāryāṇi tavanti tathāvidhāni taccharīrāṇi kalpyante tarhi taccharīraiḥ sakalaṃ jagad āpūritam iti nāṅkurādikāryair utpattavyam tadutpattideśābhāvād, nāpi māheśvaraiḥ kvacit pravartitavyam, kutaścid vā nivartitavyam; taccharīrāṇāṃ pādādyabhighātabhayāt | api ca, tāny api kāryāṇi, sāvayavatvāt, kumbhavat; tatas tatkaraṇe tāvānty evāparāṇi tasya śarīrāṇi kalpanīyāni, punas tatkaraṇe 'pi (iti) nānavasthāto muktiḥ | tan na śarīravyāpārasahāyo 'py asau sthāvarādikāryam karotīti kalpayituṃ yuktam, anekadoṣaprasaṅgāt |
 nāpi sattāmātreṇāsau svakāryamṃ karotīti kalpayituṃ yuktam, śarīrakalpanavaiyarthyaprasaṅgāt | atha sarvotpattimatām īśvaro nimittakāraṇam, tasya tatkāraṇatvaṃ sakalakāryakāraṇaparijñāne nānyathā, tatparijñānaṃ cānityasyendriyaśarīram antareṇānupapatteḥ atas tadarthaṃ tatparikalpanam iti cet, na; sakalahetuphalaviṣayaṃ tasyānityaṃ sendriyaśarīrajaṃ jñānaṃ na sambhavati, indriyāṇāṃ yugapatsarvārthasannikarṣābhāvāt; indriyārthasannikarṣajaṃ ca naiyāyikaiḥ pratyakṣam abhyupagamyate, tad uktam-
 indriyārthasannikarṣotpannaṃ jñānam avyapadeśyam avyabhicāri vyavasāyātmakaṃ pratyakṣam [NS 1.1.4] |
 sāmagrīphalasvarūpaviśeṣaṇapakṣatraye 'pīndriyārthasannikarśajasya tasya prāmāṇyābhyupagamāt; tathā, pramāṇato 'rthapratipattau pravṛttisāmarthyād arthavat pramāṇam [NBh 1] ity atra bhāṣye pramātṛprameyābhyām arthāntaraṃ pramitilakṣaṇe phale sādhakatamavad iti arthaḥ sahakāri pramāṇaṃ pratipāditam, sahakāritvaṃ cārthasya pramāṇasya phalajanane vyāpriyamānasya phalajanakatvena tasyāpi sahāyabhāvaḥ, saha karotīti sahakāri iti vyutpatteḥ | na cāsannihitasyārthasyātītasyānāgatasya vā pramitilakṣaṇaphalajananaṃ prati vyāpāraḥ sambhavati | na ca pramityajanako 'rthaḥ, tad abhyupagame na pramāṇaviṣayatety ataḥ sendriyaśarīrajanitapratyakṣajñānavattvābhyupagame maheśasya na sakalakāryakāraṇaviṣayajñānasambhavaḥ; iti śarīrasambandhāt tasya jagatkartṛtvābhyupagame tadakartṛtvam eva prasaktam, iti na tasyādṛśyaśarīrasambandho 'bhyupagantuṃ yuktaḥ |
 api ca, ghaṭādi kāryaṃ dṛśyaśarīrasambaddhapuruṣapūrvakam upalabdham ity aṅkurādi kāryam api tathā kalpanīyam | atha tatparikalpane pratyakṣabādhānavasthādidoṣād aṅkurādikāryasya kartṛpūrvakataiva viśīryata iti na tathākalpanam | nanu tadviśareṇe ko doṣaḥ ? athāṅkurādeḥ kāryatā anekakaraṇamātrābhāve samupajāyamānasya tasya akāryatāprasaktiḥ, na punaḥ kartṛbhāve; anyathā gopālaghaṭikādau tatkālāgnyabhāve dhūmasyāpy abhāvaprasaktiḥ, na punaḥ kartṛbhāveṇānalapūrvakatvaṃ vyāptigrahaṇakāle dhūmasya pratipannam, tena tatas tatra tatkāraṇam analānumānam (?) | nanv evaṃ kāryamātraṃ kāraṇamātrapūrvakatvena vyāptaṃ vyāptigrahaṇakāle pratipannam aṅkurādāv upalabhyamānaṃ kāraṇamātram idam anumāpayatu na punar buddhimatkāraṇaviśeṣam, tena kāryamātrasya vyāpter niścayāt | na ca dṛśyaśarīrasambaddhabuddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvaṃ kāryaviśeṣasyopalabdham aṅkurādau tu kāryatvam upalabhyamānaṃ tathābhūtakartṛpūrvakatvānumāne tatra pratyakṣavirodha ity adṛśyasambaddhaśarīrakartṛpūrvakatvam anumāpayatīti vaktuṃ śakyam; tathābhyupagame gopālaghaṭikādāv api tatkālādṛśyānalānumāpako dhūmaḥ kiṃ na syāt ? na ca vahnir adṛśyo na sambhavatīti vaktuṃ śakyam, nāyanaraśmiṣv adṛśyasya tasya sadbhāvābhyupagamāt | athāvyavahitarūpopalabdhyanyathānupapattyā tasya tathābhūtasya parikalpanam; nanv evaṃ dhūmasadbhāvānyathānupapattyā tatra tasya tathābhūtasya kiṃ na parikalpanam ? api ca, yathānalasya bhāsvarūpasambandhitve saty api tasyodbhūtatvānudbhūtatvābhyāṃ dṛśyatvādṛśyatve parikalpyete tathā prāsādāṅkurādīnāṃ kāryatve kiṃ na parikalpyete nyāyasya samānatvāt ? tan nādṛśyaśarīrasambandhāt tasyāṅkurādikāryotpādakatvam yuktam | dṛśyaśarīrasambandhāt tatkartṛtve upalabhyānupalambhāt kathaṃ tasya nābhāvaḥ ? yat tūktam na ca sarvā karaṇasāmagryupalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptā ityādi, tat satyam eva; idaṃ tv asatyam – īśvarasya kāraṇatve 'pi na tatsvarūpagrahaṇam pratyakseṇa, adṛṣṭavat kāryadvāreṇa tatpratipatteḥ iti; adṛṣṭapratipattāv iveśvarapratipattau kāryatvāder hetor nirdoṣasyāsambhavād iti pratipāditatvāt |
 yat tūktam sthāvareṣu kartragrahaṇam kartrabhāvāt, āhosvid vidyamānatve 'pi tasyāgrahaṇam anupalabhyasvabhāvatvena, evaṃ sandigdhavyatiriktatve na kaścid dhetur gamakaḥ, dhūmāder api sakalavyaktyākṣepeṇa vyāptyupalambhakāle na sakalā vahnivyaktayo dṛśyāḥ ityādi yāvat sarvam utpattimat kartṛkaraṇapūrvakaṃ dṛṣṭam, tasya sakṛd api tathādarśanāt tajjanyatāsvabhāvaḥ, tasyaivaṃ svabhāvaniścitāv anyatamābhāve 'pi kathaṃ svabhāvaḥ iti, tad apy asaṅgatam; yato yādṛgbhūtam iva ghaṭādikāryaṃ tatpūrvakam upalabdhaṃ tasya sakṛd api tathādarśanāt tajjanyaḥ svabhāvo vyavasthita iti tadanyatamābhāve 'pi tasya bvhāve sakṛd api tatsadbhāvo na syād iti yuktaṃ ca vaktum na punas tadvilakṣaṇaṃ bhūruhādikaṃ kartṛkaraṇapūrvakaṃ kadācanāpy upalabdham kintu kāraṇamātrapūrvakam, atas tadabhāve tasya bhavato 'hetukatvaprāptes tad eve tad gamayatīty asakṛd āveditam |
 121
 yac ca anupalabhyamānakartṛkeṣu sthāvareṣu kartur anupalambhaḥ śarīrādyabhāvāt, na tv asattvāt ityādi, tad api pratikṣiptam; uktottaratvāt | yad apy uktam caitanyamātreṇopādānādyadhiṣṭhānāt kathaṃ pratyakṣavyāpṛtiḥ ? tad asaṅgatam; tathopādānādyadhiṣṭhāyakatvasya kvacid apy adarśanāt, adṛṣṭasyāpi tasya kalpane buddhyanadhiṣṭhitasyāpi bhūruhādyupādānasya tatkartṛtvaṃ kiṃ na kalpyate adarśanāviśeṣāt ?
 yac cābhyadhāyi kāryasya śarīreṇa saha vyabhicāro dṛśyate, svaśarīrāvayavānāṃ hi śarīrāntaram antareṇāpi pravṛttinivṛttī kevalo vidadhāti iti, tad apy ayuktam; yataḥ śarīrasambandhavyatirekeṇa cetanasya svaśarīrāvayaveṣv anyatra vā kāryanirvartakatvaṃ na dṛṣṭam ity na yatrāpi tat tasya na kalpanīyam ity etāvanmātram eva pratipādyate, na tv aparaśarīrasambandhaparikalpanam atropayogi | yadi ca śarīrarahitasyāpi tasya bhūruhādikārye vyāpāraḥ parikalpyate tahri muktasyāpi tadantareṇa jñānasamavāyikāraṇatvaparikalpanaṃ kiṃ na kriyate ? tathābhyupagame na jñānasukhādiguṇarahitātmasvarūpāvasthitir muktiḥ sambhavati iti tadartham īśvarārādhanam asaṅgatam āsajyeta | yad api kāryaṃ śarīreṇa vinā karotīti naḥ sādhyam, tat svaśarīragatam, anyagataṃ neti nānena kiñcit iti, tad apy asāram; śarīravyatirekeṇa kāryakaraṇādarśanāt; svaśarīrapravṛttisvarūpe 'pi kārye taccharīrasambaddhasyaiva vyāpārāt, ataḥ acetanaḥ kathaṃ bhāvas tadicchām anuvartate [   ] iti dūṣaṇaṃ vyavasthitam eva, acetanasya śarīrādeḥ śarīrāsambaddhecchāmātrānuvartanādarśanāt | tadasambaddhasyecchāyā apy abhāvāt muktasyeva kutas tadanuvartanam acetanakāryeṇa ? atha adṛṣṭāpi icchā aśarīrasya sthāṇoḥ parikalpyate, kim iti bhūruhādikaṃ kāryaṃ kartṛvikalaṃ sṛṣṭam pai na kalpyate 'etena īśvarasyāpi prayatnasadbhāve na kācit kṣatiḥ iti nirastam, śarīrābhāve muktātmana iva prayatnāsambhavāt | aparaśarīrarahitasvaśarīrāvayavapreraṇaprayatnasadbhāvo 'pi na śarīrābhāve prayatnasadbhāvāvedakaḥ, sarvathā śarīrarahitasya tasya kvacid apy adarśanāt; dṛṣṭānusāriṇyaś ca kalpanā bhavanti | tataḥ sthāvareṣu śarīrābhāvād na tatkartur anupalabdhiḥ kintu kartur abhāvād iti kathaṃ na taiḥ kāryatvāder hetor vyabhicāraḥ ?
 na ca yathādṛṣṭasyendriyasya cānvayavyatirekayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvavyavasthāpakayor abhāve 'pi kāraṇatvasiddhir vyatirekamātrāt tathā maheśvarasyāpi tatas tatsiddhiḥ, tasya nityavyāpakatvābhyupagamena vyatirekāsambhavāt | ato na vyāptisiddhiḥ kāryatvādes tatsādhakatvenopanyastasya hetoḥ | ata eva na satpratipakṣatāpi, naikasmin sādhyānvite hetau sthite dvitīyasya tathāvidhasya tatrāvakāśaḥ iti satyam, kintu sthāṇusādhakasya karyatvādeḥ sādhyānvitatvam eva na sambhavatīti pratipāditam | yac coktam nāpi bādhā, abuddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvasya pramāṇenāgrahaṇāt iti, tad asāmpratam; buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvābhāvapratipādakasya pramāṇasyāṅkurādāv akṛṣṭotpattau sadbhāvāt | athāṅkurādikartur atīndriyatvād na pratyakṣāt tadabhāvasiddhiḥ, na; pratyakṣāt tadabhāvāsiddhāv apy anumānasya tatra tadabhāvagrāhakasya bhāvāt | tathā hi – yad yasyānvayavyatirekau nānuvidhatte na tat tatkāraṇam, yathā na paṭādayaḥ kulālakāraṇāḥ, nānuvidadhati cāṅkurādayo buddhimatkāraṇānvayavyatirekāv iti vyāpakānupalabdhiḥ; yac ca yatkāraṇaṃ tat tasyānvayavyatirekāv anuvidhatte, yathā ghaṭādayaḥ kulālasya | na copalabdhimatkāraṇasannidhāne prāganupalabdhasyāṅkurāder upalambhas tadabhāve cāparakāraṇasākalye 'pi tasyānupalambha ity anvayavyatirekānuvidhānam aṅkurādikāryāṇām |
 athāṅkurādikartur upalabdhilakṣaṇaprāptatvasyābhāvād na pratyakṣeṇa sabhāvābhāvapratītir iti nāṅkurades tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhānasyopalabdhir yuktā | nanu mā bhūt tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhānopalabdhiḥ, vyatirekānuvidhānopalabdhis tu yuktā. yathā rūpālokamanaskārasākalye 'pi kadācid vijñānakāryānutpattyā kāraṇāntarasyāpi tatra sāmarthyam avasīyate, yac ca tatkāraṇāntaraṃ sā indriyaśaktiḥ, tadabhāvād rūpajñānaṃ na sañjātam ity anupalabhyasvabhāvasyāpi kāraṇasya vyatirekaḥ kāryeṇānuvidhīyamāna upalabhyate;
 122
 na dehopalabdhimatkāraṇasya vyatireko 'ṅkurādikāryeṇānuvidhīyamāna upalabhyate, buddhimatkāraṇavyatiriktapṛthivyādisāmagrīsākalye 'ṅkurāder avāśyāṃbhāvadarśanāt | indriyaśakter anityatvāvyāpakatvena vyatirekasambhavāt tadvyatirekānuvidhānasyopalabdhir yuktā, na buddhimatkāraṇavyatirekānuvidhānasya; tasya nityatvavyāpakatvena vyatirekānuvidhānābhāvād iti cet, astu nāmaivam, tathāpīśvarasya jñānaprayatnacikīrṣāsamavāyo 'ṅkurādikāryakaraṇe vyāparaḥ, tasya sarvadā sarvatrābhāvāt tadanuvidhānaṃ syāt |
 atha tatsamavāyasyāpi sarvatra sarvadā bhāvād nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, na; tasya nityatvavyāpakatve saty api tadviśeṣaṇānāṃ īśvarajñānaprayatnacikīrṣādīnām anityatvāt avyāpakatvāc ca vyatirekānuvidhānam upalabhyeta | atha tajjñānāder api nityatvād nāyaṃ doṣaḥ sarvadā tarhy aṅkurādikāryotpattiḥ syāt | sarvadā sahakāriṇām asannidhānād neti cet, nanu te 'pi tajjñānādyāyattajanmānaḥ kiṃ na sarvadā sannidhīyante ? atha naiva te tadāyattotpattayas tarhi tair eva kāryatvādihetur anaikāntikaḥ | tatsahakāriṇām api sarvadā svotpattihetūnāṃ sakāryāṇām asannidhānād na sarvadotpadyanta iti cet, anavasthā; tathā ca aparāparasahakāripratīkṣāyām evopakṣīṇaśaktitvāt tajjñānādeḥ prakṛtakāryakartṛtvaṃ na kadācid api syāt | ataḥ sudūram api gatvā kvacid avasthām icchatā nityatvaṃ sahakāriṇām atadāyattotpattikatvaṃ vābhyupagamanīyam, tadāyattotpattikāryasyāpi tajjñānādivyatirekeṇāpy utpattir abhyupagantavyā iti vṛthā tatparikalpanā | nityatve vā punar api sahakāriṇāṃ tajjñānādīnāṃ ca nityatvāt sarvadā kāryotpattiprasaṅgaḥ | tad evaṃ tajjñānādīnāṃ ca nityatvāt sarvadā kāryasyotpattir anutpattir vā syāt ity anityās tajjñānādayo 'bhyupagantavyāḥ, tathā ca sati tadanyasāmagrīsākalye 'py aṅkurādyanutpattiḥ kadācit syāt | sakalatadanyasāmagrīsannidhānānantaram eva tajjñānādyutpattiḥ syāt | te tu sahakāriṇas tajjñānādyapreritā eva tajjñānādi janayanto 'ṅkurādi janayanti kim antargaḍutajñānādikalpanayā ? tajjñānādisahakṛtā eva tajjñānādikaṃ janayantīty abhyupagame tajjñānādyantaraṃ sahakārikāraṇajanyam ajanyatvā (?) tadanantaram anutpadyamānaṃ kāryam api tajjñānādikaṃ tadanantaraṃ notpādayati, ity āyātaḥ sa eva kāraṇāntarasākalye 'py aṅkurādikāryādyanutpattiprasaṅgaḥ, sahakāribhyas tajjñānādyantarotpattau sa eva prasaṅgaḥ anavasthā ca | tasyāṃ cāparāparajñānotpādana iva sahakāriṇāṃ sarvadopayogān na kārye kadācid apy upayogo bhavet | sahakāribhiḥ saha tajjñānādikaṃ niyamenotpattimad iti cet tarhi sahakāriṇāṃ tajjñānādeś caikasāmagryadhīnatvam abhyupagantavyam; anyathāsahabhāvāt tathaikasāmagrīlakṣaṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ tajjñānādibhir anyair janitam ajanitaṃ vā taj janayati ? [na cājanitam, tathaiva kāryatvāder hetor vyabhicāritvaprasaṅgāt janitaṃ tajjñānādikam abhyupagantavyam, tac ca tena janyena saha niyamenotpadyamānaṃ tadekasāmagryadhīnatvam abhyupanantaraṃ sāmagryadhīnaṃ syāt | sā ca sāmagrī tajjñānāntareṇotpāditā ca iti vikalyadvaye pūrvoktadoṣadvayaprasaṅgaḥ | prāganantarotpattiniyamābhyupagame sahakārihetubhir ekasāmagryadhīnatayā syāt tatrāpi saikasāmagrī tajjñānādyantareṇa preritā janayatīty abhyupeyam; anyathācetanasyācetanānadhiṣṭitasya vāsyāder iva janakatvāsambhavāt, jñānādyantaraṃ ca preryāt sāmagrīviśeṣāt prāgantaram niyamenotpadyamānaṃ taddhetubhir ekasāmagryadhīnaṃ syāt; anyathā prāganantaraṃ niyamenotpattir na syāt | sāmagryantaraṃ ca preritam apreritaṃ vā janayatīti vikalpadvaye doṣadvasyaprasaṅgaḥ, tenemaṃ doṣaṃ parijihīrṣatā na tajjñānādyutpattiḥ tadanantaraṃ saha prāg vānantaram abhyupagantavyā, tadanantaraṃ saha prāg vānantaram utpattiniyamābhāve 'pi] cāṅkurādikāryasya tadvyatirekānuvidhānam upalabhyeta; na copalabhyate, kṣityudakabījādikāraṇasāmagrīsannidhāne pratibandhe cāsati aṅkurādikāryasyāvaśyaṃbhāvadarśanāt |
 123
 atas tajjñānādyanuvidhānasya tatkāraṇatvavyāpakasyānupalambhāt tatkāraṇatvābhāvo 'ṅkurādikāryasyānumīyate | ato bādhā vyāpakānupalabdhyā buddhimatkāraṇānumānasya |
 buddhimatkāraṇānumānenaiva vyāpakānupalabdhiḥ kasmān na bādhyate ? lohalekyaṃ vajram, pārthivatvāt, kāṣṭhavad ity anumānena pratyakṣaṃ tasya tadalekhyatvagrāhakaṃ kiṃ na bādhyate iti samānam | pratyakṣeṇa tadviṣayasya bādhitatvān na tena tad bādhyata iti ced, buddhimatkāraṇatvānumānasyāpi tarhi vyāpakānupalabdhyā viṣayasya bādhitatvāt kathaṃ tadbhādhakatvam ? buddhimatkāraṇatvānumānenaiva vyāpakānupalabdher viṣayasya bādhitatvān na tadbādhakatvam iti cet, na; pārthivatvānumānena tadlekhyatvagrāhakasya pratyakṣasya bādhitaviṣayatvān na tadbādhakatvam ity api vaktuṃ śakyatvāt ? atha tadanumānasya tadābhāsatvād na prakṛtapratyakṣaviṣayabādhakatvam, naitat; itaretarāśrayadoṣaprasaṅgāt | tathā hi – pratyakṣabādhitaviṣayatvāt tadanumānasya tadābhāsatvam, tasya tadābhāsatvāt pratyakṣasya tadabādhitaviṣayatvenātadābhāsatvāt tadviṣayabādhakatvam iti vyaktam itaretarāśrayatvam | athānumānābādhitaviṣayatvanibandhanaṃ na tatpratyakṣasyātadābhāsatvam kiṃ tarhi svaparicchedyāvyabhicāranibandhanam ? nanv evam anumānasyāpi svasādhyāvyabhicāranibandhanaṃ kiṃ nātadābhāsatvam apy abhyupagamaviṣayaḥ ? athābādhitaviṣayatve sati tasya tad eva svasādhyāvyabhicāritvaṃ parisamāpyate | nanv evam abādhitaviṣayatvasya pratipattum aśakter na kvacid api svasādhyāvyabhicāritvasyānumāne 'tadābhāsatvanibandhanasya prasiddhiḥ; na hi bādhānupalambhād bādhābhāvaḥ, tasya vidyamānabādhakeṣv apy anutpannabādhakapratipattiṣu bhāvāt| atha yatra bādhakasadbhāvas tatra prāg bādhakānupalambhe 'py uttarakālam avaśyaṃbhāvinī bādhakopalabdhiḥ, yatra tu na kadācid bādhakopalabdhis tatra na tadbhāvaḥ, asad etat; na hy arvāgdṛśā bādhakānupalambhamātreṇa na kadācanāpy atra bādhakopalabdhir bhaviṣyati iti jñātuṃ śakyam, svasambandhino 'nupalambhasyānaikāntikatvāt, sarvasambandhino 'siddhatvāt; na hy asarvavit sarveṇāpy atra bādhakaṃ nopalabhyate upalapsyate vā ity avasātuṃ kṣamaḥ | nāpi bādhakābhāvo 'bhāvagrāhipramāṇāvaseyaḥ, tasya niṣiddhatvāt niṣetsyamānatvāc ca | na cājñāto bādhakābhāvo 'numānāṅgaṃ pakṣadharmatvādivat | na ca svasādhyāvyabhicāritvaniścayād eva bādhakābhāvaniścayaḥ, tanniścayam antareṇa tvadabhiprāyeṇa svasādhyāvyabhicāritvasyāparisamāptatvena niścayāyogāt | tasmāt pakṣadharmatvānvayavyatirekaniścayalakṣaṇasvasādhyāvinābhāvitvasya prakṛtānumāne 'pi sadbhāvāt pratyakṣavad na tasyāpi tadābhāsatvam | atha viparyaye bādhakapramāṇābhāvāt pārthivatvānumānasya nāntarvyāptir iti tadābhāsatvam, evaṃ tarhi kāryatvānumāne 'pi viparyaye bādhakapramāṇābhāvād vyāptyabhāvatas tadābhāsatvam iti na vyāpakānupalabdhiviṣayabādhakatā | atha pratyakṣaṃ nānumānena bādhyata iti lohalekhyatvānumānasya na tadlekhyatvagrāhakapratyakṣabādhakatā, kathaṃ tarhi diśāntaraprāptiliṅgajanitānumānena sthiracandrārkagrāhipratyakṣasya bādhā ? atha tasya pratyakṣābhāsatvād anumānena bādhā, nanu kutas tasya tadābhāsastvam ? anumānena bādhitaviṣayatvād iti cet, nanu tad evetaretarāśrayatvam – anumānena bādhitaviṣayatvāt tasya tadābhāsatvam, tasya tadābhāsatve ca tena abhādhitaviṣayatvād anumānasyātadābhāsatvena tadviṣayabādhakatvam iti vyaktam itaretarāśrayatvam | tasmāt svagrāhyāvyabhicāra eva sarvatra prāmāṇyanibandhanam | sa ca vyāpakānupalabdhau pakṣadharmānvayavyatirekasvarūpaḥ pramāṇapariniścito vidyate iti tasyā eva svasādhyapratipādakatvena prāmāṇyam na punar buddhimatkāraṇānumānasya, tatra svasādhyāvyabhicārābhāvasya pradarśitatvāt |
 na ca vyāpakānupalabdhāv api pakṣadharmatvānvayavyatirekaniścayasya svasādhyāvyabhicāritvaniścayalakṣaṇasyābhāva iti vaktuṃ yuktam, yato vyāpakānupalabdhes tāvat pakṣavyāpakatvaniścayaḥ prāg evoktaḥ | vipakṣe bādhakapramāṇasadbhāvād anvayavyatirekāv api tatrāvagamyete, tatkāraṇeṣu hi kumbhādiṣu tatanvayavyatirekānuvidhānasyopalabdhis tad anupalabdher bādhakaṃ pramāṇam | atha vā tatkāraṇatvaṃ tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhānena vyāptam, tadabhāve tatkāraṇatvāsambhavāt; tadabhāve 'pi bhavatas tatkāraṇatve sarvaṃ sarvasya kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ ca syāt, na kvacid kāryakāraṇabhāvavyavasthā;
 124
 anvayavyatirekānuvidhānaṃ hi kāryakāraṇabhāvavyavasthānibandhanam, tadabhāve 'pi kāryakāraṇabhāvaṃ kalpayataḥ kim anyat tadvyavasthānibandhanaṃ syād iti ? ato 'tivyāptiparihāreṇa kvacid eva kāryakāryaṇabhāvavyavasthām icchatā tadabhāve kāryakāryaṇabhāvo nābhyupagantavya ity anvayavyatirekānuvidhānena kāryakāraṇabhāvo vyāptaḥ, sa yatropalabhyate tatrānvayavyatirekānuvidhānasannidhāpanena tadabhāvaṃ bādhata ity anumanasiddho vyatirekaḥ; tatsiddheś cānvayo 'pi siddhaḥ | tathā hi – ya eva sarvatra sādhyābhāve sādhanābhāvalakṣaṇo vyatirekaḥ sa eva sādhanasadbhāve 'vaśyatayā sādhyasadbhāvasvarūpo 'nvaya iti vyāpakānupalabdheḥ pakṣadharmatvānvayavyatirekalakṣaṇaḥ sādhyāvyabhicāraḥ pramāṇataḥ siddhaḥ | na caivaṃ kāryatvāder ayam avinābhavaḥ sambhavati, pakṣavyāpakatve saty anvayavyatirekayor abhāvasya viparyaye bādhakapramāṇābhāvataḥ pratipāditatvāt |
 api ca, buddhimatkāraṇatve tanvādīnāṃ sādhye tadviparyayo 'buddhimatkāraṇāḥ paramāṇvādayaḥ, na ca tebhyo buddhimatkāraṇasādhyavyāvṛttinimittakāryatvādivivṛttipratipādakapramāṇapravṛttiḥ siddhā, ghaṭāder avayavitvanirākaraṇena viśiṣṭāvasthāprāptaparamāṇurūpatvāt | na ca tebhyo buddhimatkāraṇavyāvṛttikṛtā kāryatvavyāvṛttiḥ pratyakṣataḥ siddhā, buddhimatkāraṇanimittakāryatvagrāhakatvena pratyakṣasya pratyakṣānupalambhaśabdavācyasya tatra pravṛtteḥ, paramāṇvantarāsaṃsṛṣṭaparamāṇūnāṃ ca pratyakṣabuddhāv apratibhāsanān na tataḥ sādhyavyāvṛttiprayuktā sādhanavyāvṛttipratipattiḥ | nāpy abuddhimatkāraṇeṣu kāryatvāder adarśanāt sākalpena tato vyatirekasiddhiḥ, dvasambandhino 'darśanasya paracetovṛttiviśeṣair anaikāntikatvāt sarvasambandhino 'siddhatvāt; na tato vipakṣād dhetor vyāptyā vyatirekasiddhiḥ | nāpi paramāṇvādīnām anumānān nityatvasiddher kāryatvasya karyatvaviruddhasya teṣu sadbhavāt tato vyāvartamānaḥ kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetu buddhimatkāraṇatvenānvitaḥ sidhyati, kāryatvasyābuddhimatkāraṇatvena virodhāsiddher aṅkurādiṣv abuddhimatkāraṇaniṣpādyeṣu api tasya sambhavāt | atha nityebhyo 'kṛtatvād eva kāryatvaṃ vyāvṛttam, utpattimatāṃ sāṅkurādīnāṃ buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvea pakṣīkṛtatvān na tair hetor vyabhicāra āśaṅkanīya iti teṣu vartamānaḥ kāryatvalakṣaṇo hetur buddhimatkāraṇatvenānvitaḥ sidhhaḥ | syād etat yadi pakṣīkaraṇamātreṇainvābuddhimatkāraṇatvābhāvas teṣu siddhaḥ syāṭ, tathābhyupagame vā pakṣīkaraṇād eva sādhyasiddher hetūpādānaṃ vyartham | atha tatsahitāt sādhyanirdeśāt tadabhāvasiddhiḥ, kathaṃ sākalyenāniścitavyatirekāt kāryatvān nityavyatiriktānāṃ sarveṣām aṅkurādīnām abuddhimatkāraṇatvābhāvasiddhiḥ ? tadabhāvāsiddhau ca na sākalyena vyatirekaniścaya iti itaretarāśrayadoṣaḥ kathaṃ na syāt ? tad evaṃ vyāptyā vyatirekāsiddhau na sākalyenānvayasiddhiḥ, tadasiddhau ca na vyatirekasidhdir iti na kāryatvāder hetoḥ prakṛtasādhyasādhakatvam | na ca sarvānumāṇeṣv eṣa doṣaḥ samānaḥ, anyatra viparyaye bādhakapramāṇabalād anvayavyatirekasiddheḥ | na ca prakṛte hetau tad astīty asadkṛd āveditam | tena sādhyābhāve hetor abhāvaḥ svasādhyavyāptatvād eva siddhaḥ iti nirastam, yathoktaprakāreṇa svasādhyavyāptatvasya prakṛtahetor asambhavāt | nāpi dharmyasiddhatā iti, etad api nirastam; dharmyasiddhatvasya prāk pratipāditatvāt | kāryakāraṇasaṅghāt ityādikas tu grantho 'yuktatvena pratipāditaḥ | ata eveśvarāvagame pramāṇābhāvaḥ, tatpratipādakapramāṇasya samānadoṣatvenānyasyāpy acetanopādānatvāder nirākṛtatvāt |
 yac coktam nāpi hetor viśeṣaviruddhatā ityādi, tad apy asaṅgatam; yato yadi kāryamātrāt kāraṇamātraṃ tanvādeḥ sādhyate tadā vyāptisidhher na viruddhāvakāśaḥ | atha buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvaṃ sādhyate tadā tatra vyāpter asiddhatvaṃ pratipāditam eva | yadi punar ghaṭādau kāryatvaṃ buddhimatkāraṇasahacaritam upalabdham iti pṛthivyādāv api tat sādhyate; tathā sati dṛṣṭānte 'nīṣvarāsarvajñakṛtrimajñānaśarīrasambandhikartṛpūrvakatvaṃ kāryatvasyopalabdham iti tatas tādṛgbhūtam eva kṣityādau sidhim āsādayati na tu tatsahacaritatvenādṛṣṭam īśvaratvādiviruddhadharmakalāpopetabuddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvam; na hi mahānasapradeśe 'gnisahacaritam upalabdhaṃ dhūmamātraṃ giriśikharādāv upalabhyamānam agniviruddhadharmādhyāsitodakagamakaṃ yuktam, atiprasaṅgāt |
 125
 yac coktam pūrvoktaviśeṣaṇānāṃ dharmiviśeṣarūpāṇāṃ vyabhicārāt ityādi, tat apy asaṅgatam; yādṛgbhūtaṃ hi kāryatvaṃ ghaṭādāv anīśvarādidharmopetabuddhimatkāraṇatvena vyāptam upalabdhaṃ tādṛgbhūtaṃ tadanyatrāpi jīrṇaprāsādādāv upalabhyamānam akriyādarśino 'pi tathābhūtakartṛpūrvakatvapratipattiṃ janyati | na ca tatra kenacid anīśvaratvādidharmeṇa vyabhicāraḥ kadācit kenāpy upalabhyate | tathābhūtasādhyavyāptahetūpalambha eva tatra tadavyabhicāraḥ, sa ced asti katham anīśvaratvādidharmāṇāṃ vyabhicārād asādhyatvaṃ  sacetasā vaktuṃ yuktam; anyathā dhūmād agnipratipattāv api bhāsvararūpasambandhādidharmāṇāṃ vyabhicārāt tathābhūtasyāgner asādhyatvam syāt | etena pūrvasmād dhetoḥ svasādhyasiddhāv uttareṇa pūrvasiddhasyaiva sādhyasya kiṃ viśeṣaḥ sādhyate, āhosvit pūrvahetoḥ svasādhyasidddhipratibandhaḥ kriyate ? ityādi sarvam nirastam, yato yadi kāryatvamātram hetutvena kṣityāsau sādhyasādhakatvenopādīyate tadā tasya sandhigdhavipakṣavyāvṛttikatvena buddhimatkāraṇapūrvakatvāsādhakatvam | atha ghaṭādau buddhimatkāraṇatvena vyāptaṃ yat kāryatvaṃ tat tatra hetutvenopādīyate, tat tatrāsiddham iti kathaṃ tat tatra buddhimatkāraṇatvasyāpi gamakam ? ity aviśiṣṭasya kāryatvasya vyāptyabhāvād evāparaviśiṣṭasādhakahetuvyāpāram antareṇāpi kāryatvasya hetoḥ svasādhyasādhakatvavyāghātaḥ sambhavaty eva, kāryatvaviśeṣasya tu tatrāsiddhatvād eva sādhyāsiddhilakṣaṇas tadvighātaḥ |
 yat tu śabdasya kṛtakatvād anityatvasiddhau hetvantareṇa guṇatvasiddhāv api na pūrvasya kṣatiḥ iti, tad apy acāru; guṇatvaṃ hi dravyāśritatvādidharmayuktatvam ucyate, tac cec chabde siddhim āsādayati tadā pūrvahetusādhitam anityatvaṃ tatra vyāhanyata eva; na hy anutpannasya tasyāsattvād eva dravyāśritatvam guṇatvasamavāyo vā sambhavati; utpannasyāpy utpattyanantaradhvaṃsitvena tan na sambhavati | na ca tadāśritasya utpattyādikam eva tat, kharaviśāṇāder api tattvaprasaṅgād ityādi āśrayādāv asiddhatvaṃ hetoḥ pratipādayadbhir nirṇītam iti na punar ucyate | sarvathotpādakakāraṇavyaktivyatiriktasya kṣaṇamātrasthiter guṇatvaṃ na sambhavati tatsambhave ca kṣaṇikatvaṃ vyāhanyata iti pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ | dvitīye tu vikalpe na ca dharmiviśeṣaviparyayodbhāvanena kasyacid api rūpasyābhāvaḥ kathyate iti yad uktam, tad apy asaṅgatam; yato yady anyādṛśasya dharmiṇaḥ kutaścid dhetoḥ kṣityāsau siddhiḥ syāt tadā yujyetāpi vaktum – dharmiviśeṣaviparyayodbhāvanena na kasyacid dheturūpasyābhava iti, tathābhūtasya tu dharmiṇo na prakṛtasādhanād avagama iti pratipāditam | ata evāgamād hetvantarād vā na tatra viśeṣasiddhiḥ, buddhimatkāraṇasyaiva dharmiṇaḥ kṣityādikartṛtvenāsiddhatvāt | tataḥ tac cānvayavyatirekipūrvaṃ kevalavyatirekisaṃjñakaṃ tad eva cānvayavyatirekilakṣaṇaṃ pakṣadharmatāprasādād viśeṣasādhanam iti pratipādanaṃ dūrāpāstam, dharmyasiddhau tadviśeṣasiddher dūrotsāritatvāt | ata eva ya itthambhūtasya pṛthivyādeḥ kartā niyamenāsāv akṛtrimajñānasambandhī śarīrarahitaḥ sarvajña eka ity evaṃ yadā viśeṣasiddhis tadā na viśeṣaviruddhānām avakāśaḥ iti niḥsāratayā vyavasthitam |
 yat tūktam śarīrasambandhasya tāvad vyāptyabhāvena tatra nirākṛtiḥ, śarīrāntararahitasyāpy ātmanaḥ śarīradhāraṇapreraṇakriyāsu yathā vyāpāras tatheśvarasyāpi kṣityādikārye iti, tad apy ayuktam; aparaśarīrarahitatve 'py ātmanaḥ śarīrasambaddhasyaiva taddhāraṇādikartṛtvopalabdher īśvarasyāpi śarīrasambaddhasyaiva kāryakartṛtvam abhyupagantavyam iti pratipāditatvāt | yadi ca śarīrasambandhābhāve 'pi tasya kṣityādikāryakartṛtvaṃ tadā vaktavyam  kiṃ punas tat tatra ? jñānacikīrṣāprayatnānāṃ samavāyaḥ tat tatroktam eveti cet, na; samavāyasya niṣiddhatvāt | na ca kumbhakārādau śarīrasambandhavyatirekeṇānyat kartṛtvam upalabdham itīśvare 'pi tad eva parikalpanīyam, dṛṣṭānusāritvāt kalpanāyāḥ | na ca śarīravyatirekeṇa jñānacikīrṣāprayatnānām api sadbhāvaḥ kvacid apy upalabdha iti neśvare 'pi tadabhāve 'sāv abhyupagantavyaḥ |
 126
 tathā hi – jñānādīnām utpattāv ātmā samavāyikāraṇam, ātmamanaḥsaṃyogo 'samavāyikāraṇam, śarīrādi nimittakāraṇam; na ca kāraṇatrayābhāve pareṇa kāryotpattir abhyupagamyate | na cāsamavāyikāraṇātmamanaḥsaṃyogādisadbhāva īśvare 'bhyupagata iti na jñānāder api tatra bhāvaḥ |
 athāsamavāyikāraṇāder abhāve 'pi tatras jñānavyutpattis tarhi nimittakāraṇeśvaravyatirekeṇāṅkurādeḥ kim iti notpattir yuktā ? atha jñānādyabhāve tadanadhiṣṭhitānāṃ katham acetanānāṃ tadupādānādīnāṃ pravṛṭtiḥ vāsyādivad acetanasya cetanānadhiṣṭhitasya pravṛttyadarśanāt ? pravṛttāv api nirabhiprāyāṇāṃ deśakālākāraniyamo na syāt, tad adhiṣṭhitasyaiva tasya tanniyatatvadarśanāt | nanv evaṃ cetanasyāpi cetanāntarānadhiṣṭhitasya karmakarāder iva svāmyanadhiṣṭhitasya kathaṃ pravṛttiḥ ? atha svāmina evānyānadhiṣṭhitasya cetanasya pravṛttir upalabhyate, kim aṅkurāder kṛṣṭotpatter upādānasya tadanadhiṣṭhitasya sā nopalabhyate ? atha ghaṭāder upādānasya tadkaraṇe pravṛttir nopalabhyata ity aṅkurādyupādānasyāpi tadadhiṣṭhitasyaiva sā prasādhyate tarhi karmakarāder api svāmyanadhiṣṭhitasya sā nopalahyata iti svāmino 'py aparacetanāntarādhiṣṭhitasya sā sādhyatām | yadi punaḥ svāmino 'py adhiṣṭātā cetano maheśaḥ parikalpyate tarhi tasyāpy apara ity anavasthā |
 na ca cetanasyāpy aparacetanādhiṣṭhitasya pravṛttyabhyupagame acetanaṃ cetanādhiṣṭitam iti prayoge acetanam iti dharmiviśeṣaṇasya acetanatvādeḥ iti hetoś cāvyartham upādānam, acetanavac cetanasyāpi cetanādhiṣṭhitasya pravṛttyabhyupagame vyvacchedyābhāvāt | na cācetanānām api svahetusannidhisamāsāditotpattīnāṃ cetanādhiṣṭātṛvyatirekeṇāpi deśakālākāraniyamo 'nupapannah, tanniyamasya sahetubalāyātatvāt; anyathādhiṣṭhātṛjñānakṛto 'pi sa na syāt | tajjñānasya sarvācetanādhiṣṭhāyakatve kṣaṇikatve ca – tajjñeyatvam eva tadadhiṣṭhitatvam – teṣāṃ sarvakālabhāvikārye tadaiva pravṛttir iti ekakṣaṇa evottarakālabhāvikāryotpattiprasaṅgaḥ, aparakṣaṇe 'pi tathābhūtatajjñānasadbhāve punar apy anantarakālakāryotpattiḥ sadaiva, iti yo 'yaṃ krameṇāṅkurādikāryasadbhāvaḥ sa viśīryeta | katipayācetanaviṣayatve ca tajjñānādeḥ tadaviṣayāṇāṃ svakārye pravṛttir na syāt iti tatkāryaśūnyaḥ sakalaḥ saṃsāraḥ prasaktaḥ, na hi tajjñānādiviṣayatvavyatirekeṇāparaṃ teṣāṃ tadadhiṣṭhitatvaṃ pareṇābhyupagamyate | atha nityaṃ tajjñānādi; nanv evaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ jñānam, asmadādipratyakṣatve sati vibhudravyaviśeṣaguṇatvāt, śabdavat ity atra prayoge maheśajñānena hetor vyabhicāraḥ |
 atha tajjñānādivyatireke sati iti viśeṣaṇān nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, na; vipakṣaviruddhaviśeṣaṇaṃ hetos tato vyāvartakaṃ bhavati; anyathā tadvyāvartakatvāyogāt | na cākṣaṇikatvena tadvyatiriktatvaṃ viruddham, dvividhyasyāpi virodhasyānayor asiddheḥ | na ca nipakṣāviruddhaviśeṣaṇopādānamātreṇa hetor vyabhicāraparihāraḥ; anyathā na kaścid hetur vyabhicārī syāt, sarvatra vyahicāraviṣaye etadvyatiriktatve sati iti viśeṣaṇasyopādātuṃ śakyatvāt | na ca naiyāyikamatenākṣaṇikaṃ jñānaṃ sambhavati arthavat pramāṇam [NBh 1] iti vacanāt arthakāryaṃ jñānaṃ tadviṣayam abhyupagatam, arthaś ca kramabhāvī atīto 'nāgataś ca  | yac ca kramavajjñeyaviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ tat kramabhāvi, yathā devadattādijñānaṃ jvālādigocaram, kramavadvijñeyaviṣayaṃ ceśvarajñanam iti svabhāvahetuḥ |
 prasasṅgasādhanaṃ cedam, tenāśrayāsiddhatā hetor nāśaṅkanīyā | na ca viparyae bādhakapramāṇābhāvād vyāpyavyāpakabhāvāsiddher na prasaṅgasādhanāvakāśo 'treti vaktavyam, tasya bhāvāt | tathā hi – yadi kramavatā viṣayeṇa tad īśvarajñānaṃ svanirbhāsaṃ janyate tadā siddham eva kramitvam | atha na janyate tadā pratyāsattinibandhanābhāvād na tad jānīyāt, viṣayam antareṇāpi ca bhavataḥ prāmāṇyam abhyupagataṃ hīyeta, atītānāgate viṣaye nirviṣayatvaprasaṅgād iti viparyayabādhakasadbhāvaḥ siddhaḥ |
 tajjñānādeś ca nityatve tadviṣayatvam antareṇāparasya cetanādhiṣṭhitatvasyābhāvād avikalakāraṇasya jagato yugapadutpattiprasaṅgaḥ | tathā hi – yad avikalakāraṇaṃ tad bhavaty eva, yathāntyāvasthāprāptāyāḥ sāmagryā avikalakāraṇo bhavann aṅkuraḥ, avikalakāraṇaṃ ca sarvadā sarvam īśvarajñānādihetukaṃ jagat iti yugapad bhavet |
 127
 {syāt etat, neśvarabuddhyādikam eva kevalaṃ kāraṇam api tu dharmādharmādisahakarikāraṇam apekṣya tat tat karoti, nimittakāraṇatvādīśvarabuddhyādeḥ; ato dharmādiḥ sahakārikāraṇasya vaikalyād avikalakāraṇatvam asiddham, asad etat; yadi hi tasya sahakāribhiḥ kaścid upakāraḥ kriyeta tadā syāt sahakārisavyapekṣata, yāvatā nityatvāt parair anādheyātiśayasya na kaścit tasya sahakāribhyaḥ prāptavyam asti kim iti tat tathābhūtān anupakāriṇo 'pekṣeta  kiñca te 'pi sahakāriṇaḥ kim iti satataṃ na sannihitā bhavanti yadi tajjanyāḥ ? aparasvasannidhihetusahakārivaikalyād iti nottaram, teṣāṃ api tatsannidhisahakāriṇāṃ tadāyāttotpattīnāṃ tadaiva sannidhiprasakteḥ katham asiddhatā hetoḥ ? atha nityatve tadbuddhyādikaṃ sahakārikaraṇam utpādya paścād aṅkurādikāryam upajanayatīty abhyupagamas tarhy aparāparasahakārijanane evopakṣīṇaśaktitvāt tasya nāṅkurādikāryajananam | athātajjanyā eva dharmādharmādisahakāriṇaḥ ato 'yam adoṣaḥ; nanv evaṃ tair eva acetanopādānatvāt iti hetur anaikāntikaḥ syāt, atas tadāyattasannidhayo dharmādisahakāriṇa iti nāvikalakāraṇatvākhyo hetur asiddhaḥ |
 na cānaikānntiko 'pi, avikalakāraṇatvahāniprasaṅgāt, avikalakāraṇasyāpy anutpattau sarvadaivānutpattiprasaṅgāc ca viśeṣābhāvāt | etena yad api udyotakareṇoktam – yady api nityam īśvarākhyaṃ kāraṇam avikalaṃ bhāvānāṃ sannihitaṃ tathāpi na yugapadutpattiḥ, īśvarasya buddhipūrvakāritvāt | yadi hīśvaraḥ sattāmātreṇaivābuddhipūrvaṃ bhāvānām utpādakaḥ syāṭ tadā syād etac codyam; yadā tu buddhipūrvam karoti tadā na doṣaḥ tasya svecchayā kāryeṣu pravṛtteḥ, ato 'naikāntikatāiva hetoḥ [   ] iti, tad api nirastam; na hi kāryāṇāṃ kāraṇasyecchābhāvābhāvāpekṣayā pravṛttinivṛttī bhavataḥ yenāpratibaddhasāmarthye 'pīśvarākhye kāraṇe sadā sannihite tadīyecchābhāvād na pravarteran, kiṃ tarhi kāraṇagatasāmarthyabhāvābhāvānuvidhāyino bhāvāḥ | tathā hi – icchāvato 'pi karacaraṇādivyāpārākṣamāt kulālāder asamarthād notpadyante ghaṭādayo bhāvāḥ, samarthāc ca bījāder anicchāvato 'pi samutpadyamānā upalabhyante 'ṅkurādayaḥ | tatra yad īśvarākhyaṃ kāraṇaṃ kāryotpādakālavad apratihataśakti sadaivāvasthitaṃ bhāvānāṃ tat kim iti tadīyām anupakāriṇīṃ tām icchām apekṣante yenotpādakālavad yugapan naivotpadyeran ? evaṃ hi tair avikalakāraṇatvam ātmano darśitaṃ bhavet yadi yugapad bhaveyuḥ | na cāpīśvarasya parair anupakāryasya kācid apekṣāsti yenocchām apekṣeta | na ca buddhiviśeṣavyatirekeṇāparecchā tasya sambhavati |
 buddhiś ceśvarasya yadi nityā vyāpikaikā cābhyupagamyeta tadā saivācetanapadārthādhiṣṭhātrībhaviśyatīti kim aparatadādhāreśvarātmaparikalpanayā | athānāśritaṃ tajñānaṃ na sambhavatīti tadātmaparikalpanā; nanu tadātmāpy anāśrito na sambhavatīti aparāparāśrayaparikalpanayānanteśvaraprasaṅgaḥ | atha dravyatvāt tasyānāśritasyāpi sadbhāvaḥ na buddheḥ guṇatvāt iti nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | nanu kutas tasyā guṇatvaṃ ? tatra samavetatvād iti nottaram, taysaivāniścayāt | tadādheyatvāt tasyāḥ tatsamavetatvam iti cet, nanu kenaitat pratīyate ? na tāvad īśvareṇa, tenātmanaḥ jñānasya cāgrahaṇād idam atra samavetam iti tasya pratīter ayogāt | tajjñānasya tatra samavetatvam eva tadgrahaṇam ity api nottaram, anyonyasaṃśrayāt – siddhe hi idam atra iti grahaṇe tatra samavetatvasiddhiḥ, asyāś ca tadgrahaṇasiddhir ity anyonyāśrayaḥ | tan neśvaras tajjñānam ātmani samavetam avaiti, yaś cātmīyam api jñānam ātmani vyavasthitaṃ na vetti sa sarvajagadupādānasahakārikāraṇādikam avagamayiṣyatīti kaḥ śraddhātum arhati ? nāpi tajjñānam avaiti sthāṇāv ahaṃ samavetam iti, tenātmano 'vedanāt ādhārasya ca | na ca tadgrahaṇe idaṃ mama rūpam atra sthitam iti pratītiḥ sambhavati |na ca tat ātmānam apy avaiti, asvasaṃviditatvābhyupagamāt | na cāparaṃ tadgrāhakaṃ nityam jñānaṃ tasyeśvarasyāpi sambhavati – yenaikena sakalaṃ padārthajātam avagamayati, apariṇa tu tajjñānam – samānakālaṃ yāvaddravyabhāvisajātīyaguṇadvayasyānyatrānupalabdhes tatrāpi tatkalpanāsambhavāt | tatkalpane vā kartṛkam aṅkurādikāryaṃ kiṃ na kalpyeta ? astu vā tatra yathoktaṃ jñānadvayam tathāpi tayor jñānayor anyatareṇa svagrahaṇavidhureṇa na svādhārasya, na sahacarasya jñānasya, nāpy anyasya gocarasya grahaṇam | tathā hi – yat svagrahaṇavidhuraṃ tan nānyagrahaṇam, yathā ghaṭādi, svagrahaṇavidhuraṃ ca prakṛtaṃ jñānam, tato 'nena sahacarasyāgrahaṇāt kathaṃ tenāsya grahaṇam ? tenāpi grahaṇavirahitenāsya vedane tad eva vaktavyam iti na kasyacid grahaṇam iti na tatsamavetatvena tadbuddher guṇatvam, nāpi tadādhārasya dravyatvaṃ siddhim upagacchati |
 128
 tasmān nityabuddhipūrvakatve 'ṅkurādīnāṃ kim iti yugapadutpādo na bavet īśvaravat tadbuddher api sadā sannihitatvāt ? anityabuddhisavyapekṣasyāpīśvarasyācetanādhiṣṭhāyakatvena jagadvidhātṛtve tasya nityatvena tadbuddher api sadā sannihitatvam, avikalakāraṇayoḥ sarvadā sannihitatvāt yugapadaṅkurādikāryotpattiprasaṅgaḥ | tasmāt buddhimattvāt iti viśeṣaṇam akiñcitkaram eva; iti nānaikāntikatā hetoḥ |
 na cāpi viruddhatā, sapakṣe bhāvāt |} na caivaṃ bhavati tasmād viparyayaprayogaḥ – yad yadā na bhavati na tat tadānīm avikalakāraṇam, yathā kuśūlāvasthitabījāvasthāyām anupajāyamāno 'ṅkuraḥ, na bhavati caikapadārthotpāttikāle sarvaṃ viśvam iti vyāpakanupalabdhiḥ | na ca siddhasādhyatā, īśvarasya tajjñānāder vā kāraṇatve vikalakāraṇatvānupapatteḥ prasādhitatvāt | tan na nityajñānaprayatnacikīrṣāṇāṃ tatsamavāyasya vā nityasya kartṛtvaṃ yuktam |
 tasmāc charīrasambandhasyaiva kumbhakārādau kartṛtvavyāpakatvena pratītes tadabhāve kartṛtvasyāpi vyāpyasyābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | tac ca kvacit karādivyāpāreṇa karakaprayoktṛtvalakṣaṇam – yathā kumbhakārasya daṇḍādikāraṇaprayoktṛtvam, aparaṃ vāgvyāpāreṇa – yathāsvāminaḥ karmakarādiprayoktṛtvasvarūpam, anyac ca prayatnavyāpāreṇa – yathā jāgrataḥ svaśarīrāvayavaprerakatvasvabhāvam, kiñcic ca nidrāmadapramādaviśeṣeṇa tālvādikarādiprerakatvam, sarvathā śārīrasambandha eva kartṛtvasya vyāpakaḥ; sa ced īśvarān nivartate svavyāpyam api kartṛtvam ādāya nivartate iti na tasya kartṛtvam abhyupagantavyam iti prasaṅgaḥ | atha tasya jagatkartṛtvam abhyupagamyate tadā śarīrasambandhaḥ kartṛtvavyāpako 'bhyupagantavya iti prasaṅgaviparyayaḥ | na ca kārakaśaktiparijñānalakṣaṇaṃ tasya kartṛtvam – yena prasaṅgaviparyayayor vyāptyasiddher abhāvaḥ syāt – kumbhakārādau mṛddaṇḍādikārakaśaktiparijñāne 'pi śarīravyāpārābhāve ghaṭādikāryakartṛtvādarśanāt suptapramattādau ca tālvādikāraṇaparijñānābhāve 'pi tadvyāpāre prayatnalakṣaṇe sati tatpreraṇakāryakarśanāt | yad apy abhidhānamātreṇa viṣāpahārādikāryakartṛtvam tad api na jñānamātranibandhanam kintu śarīrasambandhāvinābhūtaviśiṣṭātmaprayatnahetukam eva |
 api ca , viśiṣṭadharmādharmādyupadeśavidhāy īśvaraḥ sarvajñatvena mumukṣubhir upāsyaḥ; anyatha ajñopadeśānuṣṭhāne teṣāṃ vipralambhaśaṅkyā tatra pravṛttir na syāt | tad uktam -
 jñānavān mṛgyate kaścit taduktapratipattaye |
 ajñopadeśakaraṇe vipralambhanaśaṅkibhiḥ || [   ]
 tasya ca sarvajñatve saty apy aśarīriṇo vaktrābhāvād upadeṣṭṛtvāsambhava iti tatkṛtatvena tadupadeśasya prāmāṇyāsidder na mumikṣūṇāṃ tatra pravṛttiḥ syād iti upadeśakartṛtve tasya śarīrasambandho 'py avaśyam abhyupagntavyaḥ, vyāpyābhyupagamasya vyāpakābhyupagamanāntarīyakatvāt | śarīrasambandhābhāve tu vyāpyasyāpy upadeśanidhātṛtvasyābhāva iti prasaṅgaviparyayau | vyāpyavyāpakabhāvaprasādhakaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ tālvādivyāpārābhāve 'py upadeśasya sadbhāve tasya taddhetukatvaṃ na syād iti kāryakāaṇabhāvaprasādhakaṃ prāg eva pradarśitam iti na punar ucyate | tat sthitam etat – na śarīrābhāve maheśasya kartṛtvam iti | tena śarīramanaḥsambandhābhāve prayatnabuddhyāder abhāvād īśvarasattaivāsiddhā | ataḥ tadabhāve kasya viśeṣaḥ śarīrādiyogalakṣaṇaḥ sādhyate ? ityādi pūrvapakṣavacanaṃ niḥsāratayā vyavasthitam, prasaṅgaviparyayor nimittabhūtavyāptipradarśanasya vaihitatvāt | yad apy uktam jñānacikīrśāprayatnānāṃ samavāyo 'stīśvare, te tu jñānād asya tatra nityāḥ iti, tad apy ayuktatvena pratipāditam | yac coktam tatra śarīrasambandhasya vyāptyabhāvād asiddhiḥ, tad apy asat; śarīrasambandhasya kartṛtvavyāpakatvapratipādanāt |
 yad apy uktam nāpy asarvajñatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ  kulālādiṣu dṛṣṭas tatra sādhyate ityādi, tad apy ayuktam; kulālāder ghaṭādikāryasyopāsānādyabhijñatve karmādinimittakāraṇābhijñātvaprāpteḥ sarvajñataprasaktiḥ iti vyartham apareśvarasarvajñaparikalpanam, tannirvartakātīndriyādṛṣṭaparijñānavat tasyāpi sakalapadārthaparijñānaprasakteḥ | athādṛṣṭāparijñāne 'pi kulālo mṛtpiṇḍadaṇḍādikatipayakārakaśaktiparijñānād eva ghaṭāḍilakṣāṇaṃ svakāryaṃ nirvartayatīti na sakalakāryakartṛtvānyathānupapattyā tasyātīndriyādyaśeṣapadārthajñātvalakṣaṇasarvajñatvasiddhiḥ |
 129
 yac coktam kṣetrajñānāṃ niyatārthaviṣayagrahaṇaṃ sarvavidadhiṣṭhitānāṃ, yathā pratiniyataśabdādiviṣayagrāhakāṇām aniyataviṣayasarvavidadhiṣṭhitānāṃ jīvaccharīre ityādi, tad apy asaṅgatam; yataḥ śabdhādiviṣayagrāhakāṇām iti dṛṣṭāntatvenopanyāso yadīndriyāṇāṃ tadā teṣāṃ karaṇatvād vedanalakṣaṇakriyānāśrayatvcāt katham niyataśabdādiviṣayagrahaṇam ? atha grahaṇādhāratvena na teṣāṃ niyataśabdādiviṣayagrahaṇaṃ kintu karaṇatvena; nanv evaṃ kṣetrajñānām api viṣayagrahaṇe karaṇatvam na kartṛtvam iti ghaṭādi kulālākartṛkaṃ tatkāraṇaśaktiparijñānena na siddham iti kutas taddṛṣṭāntāt kṣityāder jñānādhārakartṛkatvaṃ  siddhim upagacchati ? yadi punar jñānasamavāyeva cakṣurāḍīnāṃ niyataviṣayāṇāṃ kartṛtve 'py aniyataviṣayāparakṣetrajñakartradhiṣṭhitatvam aṅgīkriyate tarhi cetanānām api kṣ_trajñānāṃ niyataviṣāyāṇāṃ yathā paro 'niyataviṣayaś cetano 'dhiṣṭhātā tathā tasyāpi pratiniyataviṣayatvakalpanāyām aparo 'niyataviṣayas tadadhiṣṭhātābhyupagantavyaḥ, tasyāpy apara ity anavasthāprasaktiḥ | tathā, cetanānām api kṣetrajñānāṃ yadā cetano 'dhiṣṭhātābhyupagamyate tadā acetanaṃ cetanādhiṣṭhitaṃ pravartate, avetanatvāt, vāsyādivat iti prayoge 'cetanagrahaṇaṃ dharmihetuviśeṣaṇaṃ nopādeyaṃ syāt, vyavacchedyābhāvāt |
 yat tūktam bhavatv aniṣṭhā yadi tatprasādhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ kiñcid asti, tāvata evānumānasiddhatvāt iti, tad apy asaṅgatam; yataḥ pramāṇam antareṇa hetvābhāsād yady ekasya siddhir abhyupagamyate aparasyāpi tata eva sā kiṃ nābhyupagamyate ? pramāṇasiddhatvaṃ tu tāvato 'pi nāsti, aniṣṭhayā tatprasādhakasya pramāṇasyāprāmāṇyāsaṃjanād | yad apy uktam āgamo 'py asmin vastuni vidyate ityādi, tad apy ayuktam; āgamasya tatpraṇītatvena prāmāṇyam, tatprāmāṇyāc ca tatas tatsiddhir itītaretarāśrayaprasakteḥ | nityasya tv āgamasya prāmāṇyam vaiśeṣair nābhyupagatam, īśvarakalpanāvaiyarthyaprasaṅgāt | nāpy anyeśvarakṛtatadāgamāt tatsiddhiḥ, tatrāpi tatkṛtatvena prāṃāṇye itaretar_śrayadoṣāt | apareśvarapraṇītāparāgamakalpane 'pi tad eva vaktavyam ity aniṣṭhāprasaktiḥ | tad evaṃ svarūpe 'rthe āgamasya prāmāṇye 'pi na tata īśvarasiddhiḥ | yat tūktam tasya ca sattāmātreṇa svaviṣayagrahaṇapravṛttānāṃ kṣetrajñānām adhiṣṭhāyakatā yathā sphaṭikādīnām upadhānākāragrahaṇapravṛttānāṃ savitṛprakāśaḥ tad ayuktam; sattāmātreṇa savitṛprakāśasyāpi sphaṭikādyadhiṣṭhāyakatvāsambhavāt – tad asambhavaś cākāśāder api sattāmātrasya sadbhāvāt tadadhiṣṭhāyakatā syāt – kintu savitṛprakāśasya tadviśiṣṭāvasthājanakatvena tadadhiṣṭhāyakatvam, tac cet kṣetrajñeṣv īśvarasya parikalpyate tadā teṣāṃ tatkāryatāprasaktiḥ; tathā ca yathā kṣetrajñānām ātmatve 'viśiṣṭe 'pi kāryatā tatheśvarasyātmatvāviśeṣāt kāryateti tadadhiṣṭhāyako 'paras tatkartrābhyupagantavyaḥ, tatrāpy apara ity anavasthā | atha tasya kāryatve saty apy anadhiṣṭhitasyaiva svakārye pravṛttis tarhi jagadupādānāder api tadanadhiṣṭhitasya pravṛttir iti vyabhicārī adhiṣṭhātṛsādhakatvenopanyasyamānas tenaiva hetuḥ |
 api ca, sattāmātreṇa tasya tadadhiṣṭhāyakatve gaganasyeva na sarvajñatvam iti sarvajñatvasādhakahetos tadviparyayasādhanād viruddhatvam | na ca sarvaviṣayajñānasamavāyāt tatra tasyaiva sarvajñatvaṃ nākāśāder iti vaktuṃ yuktam, samavāyasya niṣiddhatvāt | sattve 'pi nityavyāpakatvenākāṣādāv api bhāvaprasaṅgāt | na ca samavāyāviśeṣe 'pi samavāyinor viśeṣa iti vaktuṃ śakyam, tadviśeṣasyaivāsiddhatvāt | siddhatve 'pi samavāyaparikalpanāvaiyarthyaprasaṅgād iti pratipādayiṣyamāṇatvāt | na ca sattāmātreṇa tasya tadadhiṣṭhāyakatve jñānamātram apy upayogi, āstāṃ sakalapadārthasārthakārakaparijñānam | yad apy uktam jñānasya svaviṣayasadarthaprakāśatvaṃ nāma svabhāvaḥ, tasyānyathābhāvaḥ kutaś cid doṣasadbhāvāt tat satyam eva | yac coktam yat punaś cakṣurādyanāśritaṃ jñānaṃ parasya siddham, tatsiddhau cakṣurādyanāśritasya jñānasyeva sukhasyāpi siddher ānandarūpatā katham muktānāṃ na saṅgacchate – yena sukhādiguṇarahitam ātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ muktiḥ ity abhyupagamaṃ śobheta | na ca rāgāder āvaraṇasyābhāvo maheśe siddhaḥ yena tajjñānam anāvṛtam aśeṣapadārthaviṣayaṃ tatra siddhim upagacchet, tatsvarūpasyaivāsiddhatvāt tatra rāgādyabhāvapratipādakasyāvyabhicaritasya hetos tvadabhyupagamavicāraṇayā dūrāpāstatvāc ca |
 130
 yat tūktam viparyāsakāraṇā rāgādyayaḥ, viparyāsaś cādharmanimittaḥ, na ca bhagavaty adharmaḥ iti tad apy asāram; adharmavad dharmasyāpi taddhetoś ca samyagjñānādes tatrāsambhavasya pratipāditatvāt | yac coktam rāgādaya iṣṭāniṣṭasādhdhaneṣu viṣayeṣūpajāyamānā dṛṣṭāḥ, na ca bhagavataḥ kaścid iṣṭāniṣṭāsādhano viṣayaḥ, avāptakāmatvāt iti, tad apy asāram; yato yadi iṣṭāniṣṭasādhano na tasya kaścid viṣayaḥ kathaṃ tarhi asāv iṣṭāniṣṭopādānaparivarjanārthaṃ pravartate buddhipūrvikāyāḥ pravṛtter heyopādeyajihāsopāditsāpūrvakatvena vyāptatvāt ? tadabhāve 'pi pravṛttāv unmattakapravṛttivad na buddhipūrvikeśvarapravṛttiḥ syāt heyopādeyajihāsopāditse apy anāptakāmatvena vyāpte, avāptakāmasya heyopādeyajihāsopāditsānupapatteh | anāptakāmatvam apy anīśvaratvena vyāptam, īśvarasyānāptakāmatvāyogād iti yatra buddhipūrvikā pravṛttir iṣyate tatra heyopādeyajihāsopāditse avaśyam aṅgikartavye, yatra ca te tatrānāptakāmatvam, yatra ca tat tatrānīśvaratvam iti prasaṅgasādhanam – īśvaratve cāvāptakāmatvam, avāptakāmatvāc ca na heyopādeyaviṣaye taddhānopādānecchā, tadabhāve na buddhipūrvikā pravṛttir iti prasaṅgaviparyayaḥ | ata eva svatantrasādhanapakṣe yad āśrayāsiddhatvādihetudoṣodbhāvanam tad asaṅgatam, vyāptiprasiddhimātrasyaivātropayogāt; sā ca pratipāditā | yā tu pravṛttiḥ śarīrādisarge sā krīḍārthā, avāptakāmānām eva ca krīḍā bhavati iti yad uktam tad asaṅgatam, ratim avindatām eva krīḍā bhavati, na ca ratyarthī bhagavān, duḥkhābhāvāt iti vārttikakṛtaiva pratipāditatvāt | yac coktam na hi duḥkhitāḥ krīḍāsu pravartante iti, tat prakramānapekṣaṃ vacanam, duḥkhābhāve 'pi krīḍāvatāṃ rāgādyāsaktinimitteṣṭasādhanaviṣayavyatirekeṇa tasyāsambhavāt |
 yac ca kāruṇyāt tasya tatra pravṛttiḥ ityādi, tad apy anālocitābhidhanam; na hi karuṇāvatāṃ yātanāśarīrotpādakatvena prāṇīgaṇaduḥkhotpādakatvaṃ yuktam | na ca tathābhūtakarmasavyapekṣas tathā teṣāṃ duḥkhotpādako 'sau nimittakāraṇatvāt tasyeti vaktuṃ yuktam, tatkarmaṇām īśvarānāyattatve kāryatve ca tenaiva kāryatvalakṣaṇasya hetor vyahicāritvaprasaṅgāt | tatkṛtatve vā karmaṇo 'bhyupagamyamāne prathamaṃ karma prāṇināṃ vidhāya punas tadupabhogadvāreṇa tasyaiva ksayaṃ vidadhato maheśasyāprekṣākāritāprasaktiḥ, na hi prekṣāpūrvakāriṇo gopālādayo 'pi prayojanaśūnyaṃ vidhāya vastu dhvaṃsayanti | tan na karuṇāpravṛttasya karmasavyapekṣasyāpi prāṇiduḥkhotpādakatvaṃ yuktam | kiñca, prāṇikarmasavyapekṣo yady asau prāṇināṃ duḥkhotpādaka iti na kṛpālutvavyāghātas tarhi karmaparatantrasya prāṇiśarīrotpādakatve tasyābhyupagamyamāne varaṃ tatphalopabhoktṛsattvasya tatsavyapekṣasya tadutpādakatvam ahyupagantavyam, evam adṛṣṭeśvaraparikalpanā parihṛtā bhavati | yathā prabhuḥ sevābhedānurodhāt phalaprado nāprabhus tathā maheśvaro 'pi karmāpekṣaphalaprado nāprabhuḥ ity apy ayuktam; yato yathā rājñaḥ sevāyattaphalapradasya rāgādiyogaḥ nairghṛṇyam sevāyattatā ca pratītā tatheśasyāpy etat sarvam abhyupagamanīyam; anyathābhūtasyānyaparihāreṇa kvacid eva sevake sukhādipradatvānupapatteḥ | tad evaṃ karmaparatantratve tasyānīśatvam, karuṇāpreritasya kartṛtve sṛjec ca śubham eva saḥ iti vārttikakārīyadūṣaṇasya vyavasthitatvam | yac ca nārakatiryagādisargo 'py akṛtaprāyacśittānāṃ tatratyaduḥkhānubhave punar viśiṣṭasthānāvāptau abhyudayahetur iti siddhaṃ duḥkhiprāṇisṛṣṭāv api karuṇayā pravartanam īśasya iti, tad api prativihitam eva; yataḥ karma prāṇināṃ duḥkhapradaṃ vidhāya tatphalopabhogavidhānadvāreṇa kṣayanimittaṃ prāṇinām abhyudayaṃ vidadhatas tasyāśucisthānapatitagṛhītaprakṣālitam odakatyāgavidhāyino nasamānabuddhitvaprasaktiḥ | api ca, yadi prānikarmaparavaśas teṣāṃ duḥkhādikaṃ tatkṣayanimittaprāyaścittakalpam upajanayatīty abhyupagamas tadā tatkarmakāryatvam tasya prasaktam – tatkṛtopakārābhāve tad apekṣyā ayogāt; upakārasya ca tatkṛtasya tadbhede tena sambandhāyogāt, abhinnasya tatkarane tasyaiva karaṇam iti kathaṃ na tatkāryatvam ?
 atha yad yadā yatra karmādikaṃ sahakārikāraṇam āsādayati tena saha sambhūya tat tadā tatra sukhādikaṃ kāryaṃ janayati, ekakāryakāritvam eva sahakāritvam iti na kāryatvalakṣaṇas tasya doṣaḥ; nanu karmādisahakārisavyapekṣāḥ kāryajananasvabhāvas tasya karmādisahakārisannidhānād yadi prāg apy asti tadā – sahakārisannidhāne'pi svarūpeṇaivāsau kāryaṃ nirvartayati,
 131
