 pararūpeṇa janakatve sarvasya svarūpeṇājanakatvāt kāryānutpādaprasaṅgaḥ, tasya cāvikalasya tajjananasvabhāvasya bhāvād uttarakālabhāvisamastakāryotpattis tadaiva syāt | tathā hi – yad yadā yajjananasamarthaṃ tat tadā tad janayaty eva, yathāntyāvasthāprāptaṃ bījam aṅkuram, ajanane vā tadā tasya tadjananasvabhāvam eva na syāt, tajjananasvabhāvaś ca karmādisāmagryasannidhāne 'py ekasvabhāvatayābhyupagamyamāno maheśa iti svabhāvahetuḥ | atha karmādisāmagryabhāve tatsvabhāvo 'py asau vivakṣitakāryaṃ na janayati, na tarhi tajjanakasvabhāvaḥ – yo hi yadā yan na janayati sa tajjanakasvabhāvo na bhavati; yathā śālibījaṃ yavāṅkurasya, atajjanakasyāpi tatsvabhāvatve 'tiprasaṅgaḥ, na janayati ca karmādisāmagryabhāve vivakṣitaṃ kāryam īśa iti vyāpakānupalabhdiḥ | atha karmādisāmagryabhāve sa svabhāvas tadapekṣakāryajanakatvalakṣaṇo nāsti tarhi svabhāvabhedāt kathaṃ na tasya bhedaḥ aparasya tannibandhanasyābhāvāt ? tathā ca kramavartyanekam aṅkurādikāryaṃ nākramaikeśvaravihitam iti naikatvaṃ tasya sidddhim āsādayati | tan na sarvajñatvāśarīritvaikatvādidharmayogas tasya siddhim upaḍhaukate | nāpi kṛtrimajñānasambandhitvaṃ tajjñānasya pratyarthaniyamābhāvāt ityādi yad uktam, tad api nirastam; nityasarvapadārthaviṣayajñānasambandhitvasya tatra pratiṣiddhatvāt |
 yac ca yathā sthapatyādīnāṃ mahāprāsādādikaraṇe ekābhiprāyaniyamitānām aikamatyaṃ tadvad atrāpi yadi kṣityādyanekakāryakaraṇe bahūnāṃ niyāmakaḥ kaścid eko 'sti, sa eveśvaraḥ ity uktam, tad apy asaṅgatam; yato na hy ayaṃ niyamaḥ – ekenaiva sarvaṃ kāryam nirvartanīyam, ekaniyamitair vā bahubhir iti; anekadhā kāryakartṛtvadarśanāt | tathā hi – kvacid eka evaikakāryasya vidhātā upalabhyate yathā kuvindaḥ kaścid ekasya paṭasya, kvacid eka eva bahūnāṃ kāryāṃ yathā ghaṭaśarāvodañcanānām ekaḥ kulādaḥ, kvacid aneko 'py anekasya yathā ghaṭapaṭaśakaṭādīnāṃ kulālādiḥ, kvacid aneko 'py ekasya yathā śibikodvahanāder anekaḥ puruṣasaṅghātaḥ | na ca prāsādādilakṣaṇe 'py anekasthapatyādinirvartye 'vaśyaṃtayaikasūtradhāraniyamitānāṃ teṣāṃ tatra vyāpāra upalabdhaḥ, pratiniyatābhiprāyānāṃ apy ekasūtradhārāniyamitānāṃ tatkaraṇāvirodhāt iti naikaḥ kartā kṣityādīnāṃ siddhim āsādayati | ata eva na tannibandhanā sarvajñatvasiddhir api tasya yuktā | tad evaṃ nityatvādiviśeṣasākalyasādhakānumānāsambhavāt tadviparyayasādhakasya ca prasaṅgasādhanasya tatra bhāvāt kathaṃ na viśeṣaviruddhāvakāśaḥ ? atha śarīrādimad buddhimat kāraṇatvavyāptam yadi kṣityādau kāryatvam upalabhyeta tadā tatas tatra tat siddhim āsādayat tathābhūtam eva sidhyed iti bhavet kāryatvāder viruddhatvam, sādhyaviparyayasādhanāt; na ca tathābhūta tat tatra vidyate iti kathaṃ viruddhatā ? na; paraprasiddhapakṣadharmatvam viparyayavyāptiṃ nāśritya viruddhatābhidhānāt | paramārthatas tu kāryatvaviśeṣasya kṣityādāv asiddhatvam tatsāmānyasya tv anaikāntikatvam iti pratipāditam | sarveṣu ceśvarasādhanāyopanyasteṣv anumāneṣv asiddhatvādidoṣaḥ samānaḥ iti karyatvadūṣaṇenaiva tāny api dūṣitāni iti na pratyuccārya dūṣyante | maheśvarasya ca nityatvaṃ tadvādibhir abhyupagamyate; na cākṣaṇikasya sattvaṃ sambhavati iti pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ |
 yac ca pṛthivyādimahābhūtāni svāsu kriyāsu buddhimatkāraṇādhiṣṭhitāni pravartante, anityatvāt, vāsyādivat iti, tatra kulālādibuddhāv apy anityatvalakṣaṇasya hetoḥ sadbhāvāt tatrāpy aparabuddhimatkāraṇādhiṣṭhitatvaprasaktiḥ, tathābhyupagame maheśabuddher apy anityatvasya prasādhanāt tasyāpy aparabuddhimadadhiṣṭhitatvam, tadbuddhāv apy evam ity anavasthā | atha buddher anityatve saty api na buddhimadadhiṣṭhitatvaṃ tadā vyabhicārī hetuḥ; aparaṃ cātra prativihitatvān nāśaṅkyate | yac ca kāryatvahetor dūṣanam asiddhatvādi tad atrāpi samānam | tathā hi – yādṛśam anityatvam buddhimadadhiṣṭhitaṃ vāsyādau siddhaṃ tādṛśaṃ tanvādiṣv asiddham | anityatvamātrasya pratibandhāsiddher vyabhicāraḥ | pratibandhābhyupagame satīṣṭaviparītasādhanād viruddhatvam | sādharmyadṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatā, nityaikabuddhimadadhiṣṭhitatvena sādhyadharmeṇānvayāsiddheḥ | sāmānyena sādhye siddhasādhyatā, viśeṣeṇa vyabhicāraḥ, ghaṭādiṣv anyathādarśanād iti | evaṃ sarvasu prakṛtasādhyasādhanāyopanyasteṣu hetuṣu yojyam |
 132
 yac ca sthitvā pravṛtteḥ iti sādhanam uktam, tatrānyad api dūṣaṇaṃ vācyam – sarvabhāvānām udayasamanantarāpavargitayā kṣaṇamātram api na sthitir asti  iti kutaḥ sthitvā pravṛttiḥ ? tasmāt prativādyasiddho hetuḥ anaikāntikaś ceśvareṇaiva; yataḥ so 'pi kramavatsu kāryeṣu sthitvā pravartate atha ca nāsau cetanāvatādhiṣṭhitaḥ anavasthāprasaṅgāt | atha acetanatve sati iti saviśeṣaṇo hetur upādīyate yathā praśastamatinopanyastas tathāpi sandigdhavipakṣavyāvṛrttikatayānaikāntikatvam anivāryam – yad eva hi viśeṣaṇaṃ vipakṣād dhetuṃ nivartayati tad eva nyāyyam, yat punar vipakṣe sandehaṃ na vyāvartayati tadupādānam apy asatkalpam, pūrvoktaś cāsiddhatādoṣaḥ saviśeṣaṇatve 'pi tadavastha eva | yac coktam sargādau vyavahāraś ca ityādi, tatrāpi uttarakālaṃ prabuddhānām ity etad viśeṣaṇam asiddham | tathā hi – nāsmanmate pralayakāle praluptajñānasmṛtayo vitanukaraṇāḥ puruṣāḥ santiṣṭhante kintv ābhāsvarādiṣu spaṣṭajñānātiśayayogiṣu devanikāyeṣūpadyante; ye tu pratiniyatanirayādivipākasaṃvartanīyakarmāṇas te lokadhātvantareṣūtpadyante iti matam | vivartakāle 'pi tata eva ābhāsvarādeś cyutvā ihāluptajñānasmṛtaya eva sambhavanti tasmāt uttarakālaṃ prabuddhānām iti viśeṣaṇam asiddham | anaikāntikaś ca hetuḥ sandigdhavipakṣavyāvṛttikatvāt |
 kiñca, anyopadeśapūrvakatvamātre sādhye siddhasādhyatā, anāder vyavahārasya sarveṣām evānyopadeśapūrvakatvasyeṣṭatvāt | atheśvaralakṣaṇapuruṣopadeśapūrvakatvaṃ sādhyate tadānaikāntikatā, anyathāpi vyavahārasambhavāt, dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyavikalatā | etac cānyahetusāmānyaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ pūrvam uktam | viruddhaś ca hetuḥ abhyupetabādhā ca pratijñāyāḥ, nirmukhasyopadeṣṭṛtvāsambhavāt – yadi īśvaropadeśapūrvakatvaṃ vyavahāṛasya sambhavet tadā syād aviruddhatā hetoḥ yāvatāsau vigatamukhatvād upadeṣṭā na yuktaḥ, tac ca vimukhatvaṃ vitanutvena, tad api dharmādharmavirahāt, tathā ca uddyotakareṇoktam – yathā buddhimattāyām īśvarasya pramāṇasambhavaḥ, naivaṃ dharmādinityatve pramāṇam asti [NV 464,14] iti | tasmād īśvarasyopadeṣṭṛtvāsambhavāt tadupadeśapūrvakatvaṃ vyavahārasya na siddhyati kintv īśvaravyatiriktānyapuruṣopadeśapūrvakatvam; ata iṣṭavidhātakāritvād viruddho hetuḥ | atheśvarasyopadeṣṭṛtvam aṅgīkriyate tadā vimukhatvam abhyupetaṃ hīyata ity abhyupetabādhaḥ | evam anyeṣv api sarvajñatvāditadviśeṣasādhakeṣu hetuṣv asiddhatvānaikānitkatvaviruddhatvādidoṣajālaṃ svamatyābhyūhyaṃ diṅmātradarśanaparatvāt prayāsasya | atha eva sapta bhuvanāny ekabuddhinirmitāni, , ekavastvantargatatvāt, ekāvasathāntargatānekāpavarakavat; yathaikāvasathāntargatānām apavarakāṇāṃ sūtradhāraikabuddhinirmitatvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tathaikasmin eva bhuvane 'ntargatāni sapta bhuvanāni, tasmāt teṣām apy ekabuddhinimitatvaṃ niścīyate; yad buddhinirmitāni caitāni sa bhagavān maheśvaraḥ sakalabhuvanaikasūtradhāraḥ [   ] ityādikāḥ prayogāḥ praśastamatiprabhṛtibhir upanyastās teśv api hetur asiddhaḥ, na hy ekaṃ bhuvanam āvasathādir vāsti, vyavahāralāghavārthaṃ bahuṣv iyaṃ saṃjñā kṛtā, ata eva dṛṣṭānto 'pi sādhanavikalaḥ; ekasaudhādyantargatānām apavarakādīnām ekasūtradhāraghaṭitatvadarśanāc cānaikāntiko hetuḥ |
 yac ca ekādhiṣṭhānā brahmādayaḥ piśācāntāḥ, parasparātiśayavṛttitvāt; iha yeṣāṃ parasparātiśayavṛttitvaṃ teṣāṃ ekāyattatā dṛṣṭā, yatheha loke gṛhagrāmanagaradeśādhipatīnām ekasmin sārvabhaumanarapatau , tathā ca bhujagarakṣoyakṣaprabhṛtīnāṃ parasparātiśayavṛttitvaṃ; etena manyāmahe teṣām apy ekasminn īśvare pāratantryam iti, tad etad yadi īśvarādhyenādhiṣṭhāyakenaikādhiṣṭhānāḥ ity ayam arthaḥ sādhayitum iṣṭas tathānaikāntikatā hetoḥ, viparyaye bādhakapramāṇābhāvāt pratibandhāsiddheḥ | dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyavikalatā | athādhiṣṭhāyakamātreṇa sādhiṣṭhānā iti sādhyate tadā siddhasādhyatā, yata iṣyata eva sugatasutair bhagavatā sambuddhena sakalalokacūḍāmaṇinā sarvam eva jagat karuṇāvaśād adhiṣṭhitam, yatprabhāvād adyāpy abhyudayaniḥśreyasasampadam āsādayanti sādhujanasārthāḥ | sarveṣv api ca sarvajñasādhaneṣu paropanyasteṣu yadi sāmānyena kaścit sarvajñaḥ iti sādhyam abhipretaṃ tadā nāsmān prati bhavatām idaṃ sādhanaṃ rājate siddhasādhyatādoṣāt kintu ye sarvajñāpavādino jaiminīyāś cārvākā vā teṣv eva śobhate |
 atheśvarādhyaḥ sarvajñaḥ sādhyeta tadoktaprakāreṇa pratibandhāsiddher hetūnām anaikāntikatā, dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyavikalateti | śeṣas tu pūrvapaksagrantho niḥsāratayopeksitaḥ | ata īśvarasādhakasya tannityatvādidharmasādhakasya ca pramāṇasyābhāvāt kleśakarmavipākāśayair aparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa īśvaraḥ [YS 1.24] ityādi sarvam ayuktatayā sthitam | ato bhavaheturāgādijayāt śāsanapraṇetāro jināḥ siddhāḥ | ataḥ suvyavasthitam etad bhavajinānāṃ śāsanam iti |
 nanu yadi teṣāṃ bhavanibandharāgādijetṛtvaṃ tadā śāsanapraṇetṛtvānupapattiḥ, tajjayānantaram evāpavargaprāpteḥ śarīrābhāve vaktṛtvāsambhavāt | atha rāgādikṣayānantaraṃ nāpavargaprāptis tarhi rāgādijayo na bhavakṣayalakṣaṇāpavargaprāptikāraṇam; na hi yasmin saty api yan na bhavati tat tadavikalakāraṇaṃ vyavasthāpayituṃ śakyam, yavabījam iva śālyaṅkurasya | atha niravaśeṣarāgādyajayād apavargaprāpteḥ prāg eva tatpraṇetṛtvād adoṣaḥ; nanv enaṃ tacchāsanasya rāgaśleśāśliṣṭapuruṣapraṇītatvena naikāntikaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ, kapilādipuruṣapraṇītasyeva ity āśaṅkyāha sūriḥ – ṭhāṇam aṇovamasuham uvagayāṇaṃ iti | asyābhiprāyaḥ – yady api sarvajñatāpratibandhighātikarmacatuṣṭayakṣāyāvibhūtakevalajñānasampado jinās tathāpi bhavopagrāhiśarīranibandhanasya karmanaḥ sadbhāvād alpasthitikasya na śarīrādyabhāvāt śāsanapranetṛtvānupapattiḥ, nāpi rāgādileśasadbhāvāt tatpraṇītāgamasyāprāmāṇyam, viparyāsahetor ghātikarmaṇo 'tyantakṣayāt | na ca karmakṣayād aparasyāpravṛttinimittatvam, bhavopagrāhiṇo 'dyāpi sāmastyenākṣayāt tatkṣaye cāpavargasyānantarabhāvitvāt karmakṣayasyaivāpavargaprāptāv avikalakāraṇatvād iti | avayavārthas tu – tiṣṭhanti sakalakarmakṣayāvāptānantajñānasukharūpādhyāsitāḥ śuddhātmano 'sminn iti sthānāṃ lokāgralakṣaṇaṃ viśiṣṭakṣetram, na vidyate upamā svābhāvikātyantikatvena sakalavyābādhārahitatvena ca sarvasukhātiśāyitvād yasya tat sukham ānandarūpaṃ yasmiṃs tat tathā, tat upa iti kālasāmīpyena gatānāṃ prāptānām, yad vā upa ity upasargaḥ prakarṣe 'py upalabhyate, yathā upoḍharāgeṇa [   ] iti | tena sthānam anupamasukhaṃ prakarṣeṇa gatānām iti | parārthe prayujyamānāḥ śābdā vatim antareṇāpi tam arthaṃ gamayanti [   ] iti nyāyād  anubhūyamānatīrthakṛnnām karmaleśasadbhāve 'pi tad gatā iva gatā ity uktās tena śāsanapraṇetṛtvaṃ tasyām avasthāyāṃ teṣām upapannam eva ||
 yad vā muktāḥ sarvatra niṣṭhanti vyomavat tāpavarjitāḥ [   ] ity etasya durnayasya nirāsārtham āha sūriḥ – ṭhāṇam aṇovamasuham uvagayāṇaṃ atra ca sthānam anupamasukham – prakarṣeṇa apunarāvṛttyā gatānām – upagatānām iti vyākhyeyam |
 atha vā buddhyādīnāṃ navānāṃ viśeṣaguṇānām ātyantikaḥ kṣaya ātmano muktiḥ [   ] iti matavyavacchedārtham ācāryeṇa ṭāṇam aṇovamasuham uvagayāṇaṃ iti sūtram upanystam | asya cāyam arthaḥ – sthitiḥ sthānaṃ svarūpaprāptis tad anupamasukham upa iti sakalakarmakṣayānantaram avyavadhānena gatānāṃ prāptānāṃ – śaileśyavasthācaramasamayopādeyabhūtam anantasukhasvabhāvam ātmanaḥ kathaṃcid ananyabhūtaṃ svarūpaṃ prāptānām iti yāvat |
